śrī gaṇeśāya namaḥ śrī gaurī śaṃkarābhyām namaḥ atha saptamī vāyavīya saṃhitā prārabhyate vyāsaḥ uvāca svāmi tvam ca vibhu tvam ca svabhāvam saṃpracakṣate sākṣāt satyavatī sūnoḥ vedavyāsasya dhīmataḥ madhuraḥ śravaṇānām ca manojña pada parvaṇām kathānām nipuṇaḥ vaktā kāla vid naya vid kaviḥ tasmai sāma ca pūjām ca yathāvat pratyapādayan pratigṛhya satām pūjām munibhiḥ pratipāditām uddiṣṭa mānasam bheje niyuktaḥ yuktam ātmanaḥ sa utkaṃṭham abhavat citam śrotum paurāṇikīm kathām tadā tam anukūlābhiḥ vāgbhiḥ pūjya mahā ṛṣayaḥ atīva abhimukham kṛtvā vacanam ca idam abruvan ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ saṃprāpta udya mahābhāga śaiva rāja mahāmate purāṇa vidyām akhilām vyāsāt pratyakṣam īyivān tasmāt āścarya bhūtānām kathānām tvam hi bhājanam ratnānām uru sārāṇām ratna ākaraḥ iva arṇavaḥ tvam adṛṣṭa vaśāt asmad darśana artham iha āgataḥ tasmāt śrāvyataram puṇyam sat kathā jñāna saṃhitam vedānta sāra sarvasvam purāṇam śrāvaya āśu naḥ ślakṣṇām ca nyāya saṃyuktām pratyuvāca śubhām giram sūtaḥ uvāca abhivandya mahādevam devīm skandam vināyakam śvetakalpa prasaṃgena vāyunā kathitam purā vidyā sthānāni sarvāṇi purāṇa anukramam tathā aṣṭādaśānām vidyānām etāsām bhinna vartmanām ādikarttā kaviḥ sākṣāt śūlapāṇiḥ iti śrutiḥ brahmāṇam vidadhe sākṣāt putram agre sanātanam vidyāḥ ca imāḥ dadau pūrvam viśva sṛṣṭi artham īśvaraḥ pālanāya harim devam rakṣā śaktim dadau tatas madhyamam tanayam viṣṇum pātāram brahmaṇaḥ api hi labdha vidyena vidhinā prajā sṛṣṭim vitanvatā yadā asya vistaram śaktāḥ na adhigantum prajāḥ bhuvi tadā vidyā samāsa artham viśveśvara niyogataḥ dvāpara anteṣu viśvātmā viṣṇuḥ viśvaṃbharaḥ prabhuḥ nirmitāni purāṇāni anyāni ca tatas param sa punar dvāpare ca asmin kṛṣṇadvaipāyana ākhyayā saṃkṣipya sa punar vedān caturddhā kṛtavān muniḥ purāṇānām ca saṃkṣiptam catur lakṣa pramāṇataḥ daśadhā ca aṣṭadhā ca etat purāṇam upadiśyate skāndam ca vāmanam ca eva kaurmyam mātsyam ca gāruḍam brahmāṃḍam ca iti puṇyaḥ ayam purāṇānām anukramaḥ tatra śaivam turīyam yat śārvam sarva artha sādhakam granthaḥ lakṣa pramāṇam tat vyastam dvādaśa saṃhitam tad uktena eva dharmeṇa śaivāḥ traivarṇikāḥ narāḥ tam āśritya eva devānām api muktiḥ na ca anyathā aumam mātṛpurāṇam ca rudraikādaśakam tathā kailāsam śatarudram ca śatarudrā ākhyam eva ca sahasra koṭi rudra ākhyam vāyavīyam tatas param dharma saṃjñam purāṇam ca iti evam dvādaśa saṃhitāḥ raudram vaināyakam caumam mātṛkā ākhyam tatas param raudraka adaśaka ākhyam yat kailāsam ṣaṣ sahasrakam śatarudram tri sāhasram koṭi rudram tatas param dharma saṃjñam purāṇam yat tat dvādaśa sahasrakam tat evam lakṣam uddiṣṭam śaivam śākhā vibhedataḥ vidyeśvara ākhyā tatra ādyā dvitīyā rudra saṃhitā tṛtīyā śatarudrā ākhyā koṭirudrā caturthikā paṃcamī kathitā ca umā ṣaṣṭhī kailāsa saṃhitā saptamī vāyavīyā ākhyā saptā evam saṃhitāḥ iha vidyeśvaram dvi sāhasram raudram paṃcaśata ayutam triṃśat tathā dvi sāhasram sārddha ekaśatam īritam catvāriṃśat sa aṣṭaśatam kailāsam bhū sahasrakam catvāriṃśat ca dviśatam vāyavīyam atas param catur sāhasra saṃkhyākam evam saṃkhyā vibhedataḥ śrutam parama puṇyantu purāṇam śiva saṃjñakam catur sāhasrakam yat tu vāyavīyam udīritam na a veda viduṣe vācyam idam śāstram anuttamam na ca eva a śraddha dhānāya na a purāṇa vide tathā pradeyam śiva bhaktāya śiva dharma anusāriṇe purāṇa saṃhitā yasya prasādāt mayi varttate iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe vidyāvatārakathanam nāma prathamaḥ adhyāyaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca purā kālena mahatā kalpeti ite punar punar munīnām ṣaṭkulīyānām bruvatām itaretaram te abhijagmuḥ vidhātāram draṣṭum brahmāṇam avyayam yatra āste bhagavān brahmā stūyamānaḥ sura asuraiḥ meru śṛṃge śubhe ramye deva dānava saṃkule vihaṃga saṃgha saṃghuṣṭe maṇi vidruma bhūṣite tapta jāṃbūnada mayam prāṃśu prākāra toraṇam mahārha maṇi citrābhiḥ lelihānam iva aṃbaram mahā bhavana koṭībhiḥ anekābhiḥ alaṃkṛtam sarva lakṣaṇa yukta aṃgyā labdha cāmara hastayā munayaḥ ūcuḥ namaḥ pradhāna dehāya pradhāna kṣobha kāriṇe trayoviṃśati bhedena vikṛtāya avikāriṇe namaḥ brahmāṇḍa dehāya brahmāṃḍa udara vartine tatra saṃsiddha kāryāya saṃsiddha karaṇāya ca tvayā eva nikhilam sṛṣṭam saṃhṛtam pālitam jagat tathā api māyayā nātha na vidmaḥ tvām pitāmaha sūtaḥ uvāca evam brahmā mahābhāgaiḥ mahā ṛṣibhiḥ abhiṣṭutaḥ ṛṣayaḥ he mahābhāgāḥ mahā sattvāḥ mahā ojasaḥ kimartham sahitāḥ sarve yūyam atra samāgatāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ kaḥ pumān sarva sattvebhyaḥ purāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ paraḥ viśuddhaḥ paripūrṇaḥ ca śāśvataḥ parameśvaraḥ tat tvam vada mahā prājña sva saṃdeha apanuttaye evam pṛṣṭaḥ tadā brahmā vismaya smera vīkṣaṇaḥ devānām dānavānām ca munīnām api sannidhau ānanda klinna sarva aṃgaḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ abhāṣata iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe muniprastāvavarṇanam nāma dvitīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ kāraṇānām ca yaḥ dhātā dhyātā parama kāraṇam sarva aiśvaryeṇa saṃpannaḥ nāmnā sarveśvaraḥ svayam sarvaiḥ mumukṣubhiḥ dhyeyaḥ śaṃbhuḥ ākāśa madhya gaḥ yaḥ agre mām vidadhe putram jñānam ca prahiṇoti me īśaḥ vṛkṣaḥ iva stabdhaḥ yaḥ ekaḥ divi tiṣṭhati ekaḥ bahūnām jantūnām niṣkriyāṇām ca sa kriyaḥ jīvaiḥ ebhiḥ imān lokān sarvān īśaḥ yaḥ īśate yaḥ tu kālāt pramuktāni kāraṇāni akhilāni api svabhāvikī parāśaktiḥ nityā jñāna kriye api tau ubhau akṣara ātmānau ekaḥ devaḥ svayam haraḥ īśate tad abhidhyānāt yojana asattva bhāvanaḥ aprākṛta vapuḥ śrīmān lakṣya lakṣaṇa varjitaḥ ayam muktaḥ mocakaḥ ca hi akālaḥ kāla codakaḥ sarva uparikṛta āvāsaḥ sarva āvāsaḥ ca sarva vid akhaṃḍa jagat aṃḍānām piṃḍīkaraṇa paṃḍitaḥ audārya vīrya gāṃbhīrya mādhurya makara ālayaḥ na eva asya sadṛśam vastu na adhikam ca api kiṃcana atulaḥ sarva bhūtānām rāja rājaḥ ca tiṣṭhati ayam tu parayā bhaktyā dṛśyate na anyathā kvacid kathitāni purā sadbhiḥ bhāva artham na atra saṃśayaḥ hariḥ ca aham ca rudraḥ ca tathā anye ca sura asurāḥ tapobhiḥ ugraiḥ adya api tasya darśana kāṃkṣiṇaḥ bhaktaiḥ antar bahis ca api pūjyaḥ saṃbhāṣyaḥ eva ca tad niṣṭhaiḥ tad paraiḥ bhaktaiḥ dṛśyam tad vratam āśritaiḥ tad abhyāsa vaśāt sāmyam pūrvayoḥ puṇya pāpayoḥ sāmyāt prasāda saṃparkaḥ dharmasya atiśayaḥ tatas dharma atiśayam āsādya paśoḥ pāpa parikṣayaḥ evam prakṣīṇa pāpasya bahubhiḥ janmabhiḥ kramāt sa aṃbe sarveśvare bhaktiḥ jñāna pūrvā prajāyate bhāva anuguṇam īśasya prasādaḥ vyatiricyate prasādāt karma saṃtyāgaḥ phalataḥ na svarūpataḥ tasmāt karma phala tyāgāt śiva dharma anvayaḥ śubhaḥ tatra anapekṣāt sāpekṣaḥ mukhyaḥ śataguṇa adhikaḥ śiva dharma anvayasya asya śiva jñāna samanvayaḥ jñanā anvaya vaśāt puṃsaḥ saṃsāre doṣa darśanam tatas viṣaya vairāgyam vairāgyāt bhāva sādhanam yādṛśī yogyatā puṃsaḥ tasya tādṛś anugrahaḥ tatas vārāṇasī puṇyā purī parama śobhanā gantavyā yatra viśveśaḥ devyā saha pinākadhṛk tatra āścaryam mahat dṛṣṭvā mad samīpam gamiṣyatha tatas vaḥ kathayiṣyāmi mokṣa upāya dvijottamāḥ cakram tat api saṃkṣiptam ślakṣṇam cāru śilā tale vilāsa vaśam urvaśyā yātaḥ daivena coditaḥ akrameṇa haran mohāt yajña vāṭam hiraṇmayam satram ārebhire divyam brahma jñāḥ gārhapatya gāḥ sphaṭika maya mahībhṛt pāda jābhyaḥ śilābhyaḥ prasarat amṛta kalpaḥ svaccha pānīya ramyam ati rasa phala vṛkṣa prāyam avyāla sattvam tapasaḥ ucitam āsīt naimiṣam tat munīnām iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe naimiṣopākhyānam nāma tṛtīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca tat ca satram pravavṛte sarva āścaryam mahā ṛṣiṇām ājñāyām marutaḥ yasya saṃsthitāḥ saptasaptakāḥ prerayan śaśvat aṃgāni prāṇa ādyābhiḥ sva vṛttibhiḥ sarva bhūta śarīrāṇām kurute yaḥ ca dhāraṇam tam āśrama gatam dṛṣṭvā munayaḥ dīrgha satriṇaḥ cāmīkara mayam tasmai viṣṭaram samakalpayan vāyuḥ uvāca atra vaḥ kuśalam viprāḥ kaccit vṛtte mahā kratau kaccit abhyarcya yuṣmābhiḥ vidhiḥ āsīt su anuṣṭhitaḥ nivṛtte ca mahā satre paścāt kim vaḥ cikīrṣitam iti uktāḥ munayaḥ sarve vāyunā śiva bhāvinā prahṛṣṭa manasaḥ pūtāḥ pratyūcuḥ vinaya anvitāḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ śṛṇu ca idam purāvṛttam tamasā ākrānta mānasaiḥ upāsitaḥ purā asmābhiḥ vijñāna artham prajāpatiḥ saḥ upi asmān anugṛhya āha śaraṇyaḥ śaraṇa āgatān bhaktiḥ ca asya prasādena prasādāt eva nirvṛtiḥ iti ādiśya vayam sarve preṣitā parameṣṭhinā asmin deśe mahābhāga tava āgamana kāṃkṣiṇaḥ iti ākarṇya purāvṛttam ṛṣīṇām dīrgha satriṇām vāyuḥ prīta manāḥ bhūtvā tatra āsīt muni saṃvṛtaḥ tatas taiḥ munibhiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ teṣām bhāva vivṛddhaye sarga ādi śārvam aiśvaryam samāsāda vadat vibhuḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe vāyusamāgamaḥ nāma caturthaḥ adhyāyaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca naimiṣīyāḥ ūcuḥ bhavān katham anuprāptaḥ jñānam īśvara gocaram vāyuḥ uvāca tapasā tena tīvreṇa tuṣṭaḥ tasya pitā svayam śvetaḥ nāma muniḥ bhūtvā divyām vācam udīrayan tam dṛṣṭvā pitaram brahmā brahmaṇaḥ adhipatim patim praṇamya parama jñānam gāyatryā saha labdhavān yatas śrutvā amṛtam labdham brahmaṇā parameśvarāt munayaḥ ūcuḥ kim tat jñānam tvayā labdham tathyāt tathī antaram śubham yatra kṛtvā parām niṣṭhām puruṣaḥ sukham ṛcchati paśu pāśa pati jñānam yat labdham tu mayā purā tatra niṣṭhā parā kāryā puruṣeṇa sukha arthinā ajñāna prabhavam duḥkham jñānena eva nivarttate paśuḥ pāśaḥ patiḥ ca iti kathyate tat trayam kramāt akṣaram paśuḥ iti uktaḥ kṣaram pāśaḥ udāhṛtaḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ tayoḥ ca paramam kim vā tat etat brūhi māruta vāyuḥ uvāca prakṛtiḥ kṣaram iti uktam puruṣaḥ akṣaraḥ ucyate munayaḥ ūcuḥ kā eṣā prakṛtiḥ iti uktā kaḥ eṣa puruṣaḥ mataḥ anayoḥ kena sambandhaḥ kaḥ uyam prerakaḥ īśvaraḥ vāyuḥ uvāca māyā prakṛtiḥ uddiṣṭā puruṣaḥ māyayā vṛtaḥ saṃbandhaḥ mūlakarmabhyām śivaḥ prerakaḥ īśvaraḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ kā iyam māyā samā khyātā kiṃrūpaḥ māyayā vṛtaḥ mūlam kīdṛś kutas vā asya kim śiva tvam kutas śivaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca māyā māheśvarī śaktiḥ cit rūpaḥ māyayā vṛtaḥ malaḥ cicchādakaḥ naijaḥ viśuddhiḥ śivatā svatas munayaḥ ūcuḥ āvṛṇoti katham māyā vyāpinam kena hetunā kimartham ca āvṛtiḥ puṃsaḥ kena vā vinivartate vāyuḥ uvāca āvṛtiḥ vyapinaḥ syāt vyāpi yasmāt kalā ādi api munayaḥ ūcuḥ tat kimādi kim antam vā kim phalam vā kimāśrayam mala kṣayasya kaḥ hetuḥ kīdṛś kṣīṇa malaḥ pumān vāyuḥ uvāca kalā ādayaḥ samākhyātāḥ yaḥ bhoktā puruṣaḥ bhavet anādi mala bhoga antam ajñāna ātma samāśrayam bāhya antaḥkaraṇa dvāram śarīram bhoga sādhanam bhāva atiśaya labdhena prasādena mala kṣayaḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ kalā ādi paṃcatattvānām kim karma pṛthak ucyate kimātmakam tat avyaktam kena ākāreṇa bhujyate kim tasya śaraṇam bhuktau śarīram ca kim ucyate vāyuḥ uvāca sukha duḥkha vimoha ātmā bhujyate guṇavān tridhā prakṛtau sūkṣma rūpeṇa tile tailam iva sthitāḥ sukham ca sukha hetuḥ ca samāsāt sāttvikam smṛtam sāttvikī ūrdhva gatiḥ proktā tāmasī syāt adhas gatiḥ madhyamā tu gatiḥ yā sā rājasī paripaṭhyate tanmātra pañcakam ca eva bhūta paṃcakam eva ca pradhāna buddhi ahaṃkāra manāṃsi ca catuṣṭayam samāsāt evam avyaktam sa vikāram udāhṛtam vyaktam kārya daśā āpannam śarīra ādi ghaṭa ādi vat yathā ghaṭa ādikam kāryam mṛd ādeḥ na ati bhidyate śarīra ādi tathā vyaktam avyaktāt na ati bhidyate tasmāt avyaktam eva aikya kāraṇam karaṇāni ca śarīram ca tad ādhāram tat bhogyam ca api na itarat munayaḥ ūcuḥ vāyuḥ uvāca buddhīndriya śarīrāṇām na ātmatā sadbhiḥ iṣyate smṛteḥ aniyata jñānāt ayāvat deha vedanāt atas smartā anubhūtānām aśeṣa jñeya gocaraḥ sarvam tatra sa sarvatra vyāpya tiṣṭhati śāśvataḥ tathā api kvāpi kena api vyaktam eṣa na dṛśyate na ca strī na pumān eṣa nā eva ca api napuṃsakaḥ aśarīram śarīreṣu caleṣu sthāṇum avyayam sadā paśyati tam dhīraḥ naraḥ pratyavamarśanāt vipadām vīja bhūtena puruṣaḥ tena saṃyutaḥ sukhī duḥkhī ca mūḍhaḥ ca bhavati svena karmaṇā adbhiḥ āplavitam kṣetram janayati aṃkuram yathā ājñānāt plāvitam karma deham janayate tathā atyantam asukha āvāsāḥ smṛtāḥ ca ekānta mṛtyavaḥ anāgatāḥ atītāḥ ca tanavaḥ asya sahasraśas āgatya āgatya śīrṇeṣu śarīreṣu śarīriṇaḥ chāditaḥ ca viyuktaḥ ca śarīraiḥ eṣu lakṣyate aṣṭāpada parikṣepe hi akṣa mudrā iva lakṣyate pathi saṃgamaḥ eva ayam dāraiḥ putraiḥ ca bandhubhiḥ sa paśyati śarīram tat śarīram tat na paśyati tau paśyati paraḥ kaścid tau ubhau tam na paśyataḥ saḥ eṣa badhyate pāśaiḥ sukha duḥkha aśanaḥ paśuḥ līlā sādhana bhūtaḥ yaḥ īśvarasya iti sūrayaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca iti ākarṇya anila vacaḥ munayaḥ prīta mānasāḥ procuḥ praṇamya tam vāyum śaiva āgama vicakṣaṇam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe śivatattvajñānavarṇanam nāma paṃcamaḥ adhyāyaḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ yaḥ ayam paśuḥ iti proktaḥ yaḥ ca pāśaḥ udāhṛtaḥ abhyām vilakṣaṇaḥ kaścid koyam asti tayoḥ patiḥ vāyuḥ uvāca asti kaścid aparyanta ramaṇīya guṇa āśrayaḥ patiḥ viśvasya nirmātā paśu pāśa vimocanaḥ abhāve tasya viśvasya sṛṣṭiḥ eṣā katham bhavet acetana tvāt ajñānāt anayoḥ paśu pāśayoḥ jagat ca kartṛ sāpekṣam kāryam sa avayavam yatas tasmāt kāryasya kartṛ tvam patyuḥ na paśu pāśayoḥ paśoḥ api ca kartṛ tvam patyuḥ preraṇa pūrvakam ayathā karaṇa jñānam andhasya gamanam yathā ātmānam ca pṛthak matvā preritāram tatas pṛthak vyakta avyaktam bibharti īśaḥ viśvam viśva vimocakaḥ na atas param vijānadbhiḥ veditavyam hi kiṃcanaḥ yathā āpaḥ srotasi vyāptāḥ yathā araṇyām hutāśanaḥ evam eva mahātmānam ātmani ātma vilakṣaṇam yaḥ ekaḥ jālavān īśaḥ īśānībhiḥ sva śaktibhiḥ sarvān lokān imān kṛtvā ekaḥ eva saḥ īśate saṃsṛjya viśva bhuvanam goptā te saṃcukoca yaḥ tathā eva viśvatobāhu viśvatas pāda saṃyutaḥ dyāvābhūmī ca janayan devaḥ ekaḥ maheśvaraḥ asmāt nā asti param kiṃcid aparam paramātmanaḥ na aṇīyaḥ asti na ca jyāyaḥ tena pūrṇam idam jagat sarvavyāpī ca bhagavān tasmāt sarva gataḥ śivaḥ sarvasya prabhuḥ īśānaḥ sarvasya śaraṇam suhṛd tam akratum kratu prāyam mahima atiśaya anvitam dhātuḥ prasādāt īśānam vīta śokaḥ prapaśyati veda aham enam ajaram purāṇam sarvagam vibhum ekaḥ api trīn imān lokān bahudhā śakti yogataḥ vṛkṣe ismin puruṣaḥ magnaḥ guhyamānaḥ ca śocati juṣṭam anyam yadā paśyet īśam parama kāraṇam tadā asya mahimānam ca vīta śokaḥ sukhī bhavet chandāṃsi yajñāḥ ṛtavaḥ yat bhūtam bhavyam eva ca māyī viśvam sṛjati asmin niviṣṭaḥ māyayā paraḥ māyām tu prakṛtim vidyāt māyinam tu maheśvaram sūkṣma ati sūkṣmam īśānam kalalasya api madhyatas sraṣṭāram api viśvasya veṣṭitāram ca tasya tu śivam eva īśvaram jñātvā śāntim atyantam ṛcchati saḥ eva kālaḥ goptā ca viśvasya adhipatiḥ prabhuḥ tam viśva adhipatim jñātvā mṛtyu pāśāt pramucyate ghṛtāt param maṃḍam iva sūkṣmam jñātvā sthitam prabhum hṛdaye saṃniviṣṭam tam jñātvā eva amṛtam aśnute kevalaḥ śivaḥ eva ekaḥ yatas prajñā purātanī rudrasya asya prapadyante rakṣā artham dakṣiṇam sukham kṣaram tu avidyā hi amṛtam vidyā iti parigīyate sarva ādhipatyam kurute sṛṣṭvā sarvān pratāpavān svabhāva vācakān sarvān vācyān ca pariṇāmayan guṇān ca bhogya bhoktṛ tve tat viśvam adhitiṣṭhati te vai guhya upaṇiṣadi gūḍham brahma parātparam bhāva grāhyam anīha ākhyam bhāva abhāva karam śivam yena idam āvṛtam nityam kāla kāla ātmanā yatas tat karma bhūyaśas kṛtvā vinivṛtya ca bhūyaśas aṣṭābhiḥ ca tribhiḥ ca evam dvābhyām ca ekena vā punar kālena ātma guṇaiḥ ca api kṛtsnam eva jagat svayam guṇaiḥ ārabhya karmāṇi svabhāva ādīni yojayet teṣām abhāve nāśaḥ syāt kṛtasya api ca karmaṇaḥ tam viśvarūpam abhavam bhavam īḍyam prajāpatim kāla ādibhiḥ paraḥ yasmāt prapaṃcaḥ parivartate dharma āvaham pāpa nudam bhoga īśam viśva dhāma ca tam īśvarāṇām paramam maheśvaram tam devatānām paramam ca daivatam patim patīnām paramam parastāt vidāma devam bhuvaneśvara īśvaram parā asya vividhā śaktiḥ śrutau svābhāvikī śrutā jñānam balam kriyā ca eva yābhyaḥ viśvam idam kṛtam tasya asti patiḥ kaścid nā eva liṃgam na ca īśitā kāraṇam kāraṇānām ca sa teṣām adhipa adhipaḥ na janma hetavaḥ tadvat mala māyā ādi saṃjñakāḥ saḥ ekaḥ sarva bhūteṣu gūḍhaḥ vyāptaḥ ca viśvatas sarva bhūta adhivāsaḥ ca sākṣī cetā ca nirguṇaḥ ekaḥ vaśī niṣkriyāṇām bahūnām vivaśa ātmanām nityānām api asau nityaḥ cetanānām ca cetanaḥ ekaḥ bahūnām ca akāmaḥ kāmān īśaḥ prayacchati sāṃkhya yoga adhigamyam yatkāraṇam jagatām patim jñātvā devam paśuḥ pāśaiḥ sarvaiḥ eva vimucyate pradhānaḥ kṣetrajña patiḥ guṇa īśaḥ pāśa mocakaḥ brahmāṇam vidadhe pūrvam vedān ca upādiśat svayam mumukṣuḥ asmāt saṃsārāt prapadye śaraṇam śivam niṣphalam niṣkriyam śāntam niravadyam niraṃjanam tapaḥ prabhāvāt devasya prasādāt ca mahā ṛṣayaḥ na apraśāntāya dātavyam etat jñānam anuttamam na putrāya āśu vṛttāya na a śiṣyāya ca sarvathā atas ca saṃkṣepam idam śṛṇudhvam śivaḥ parastāt prakṛteḥ ca puṃsaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe śivatattvavarṇanam nāma ṣaṣṭhaḥ adhyāyaḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ yadā asya antargatam viśvam śaśvat saṃsāra maṇḍalam brahmā hariḥ ca rudraḥ ca tathā anye ca sura asurāḥ kaḥ eṣa bhagavān kālaḥ kasya vā vaśa vartī ayam vāyuḥ uvāca kāla kāṣṭhā nimeṣa ādi kalā kalita vigraham kāla ātmā iti samākhyātam tejaḥ māheśvaram param niyoga rūpam īśasya balam viśva niyāmakam tasya aṃśa aṃśa mayī muktiḥ kāla ātmani mahātmani tatas niṣkramya saṃkrāntā visṛṣṭa agreḥ iva āyasī tasmāt kāla vaśe viśvam na sa viśva vaśe sthitaḥ śivasya tu vaśe kālaḥ na kālasya vaśe śivaḥ yatas a pratihatam śārvam tejaḥ kāle pratiṣṭhitam eka chatrām mahīm kṛtsnām ye parākramya śāsati āyurveda vidaḥ vaidyāḥ tu anuṣṭhita rasāyanāḥ śriyā rūpeṇa śīlena balena ca kulena ca durvijñeya svabhāvasya kāla asya aho vicitra tā yaḥ śrīmān saḥ api niḥśrīkaḥ kālaḥ citra gatiḥ dvijā na ābhijātyam na vai śīlam na balam na ca naipuṇam bhavet kāryāya paryāptam kālaḥ ca hi anirodhakaḥ ye sa nāthāḥ ca dātāraḥ gīta vādyaiḥ upasthitāḥ tāni eva kālena samāhṛtāni siddhim prayānti āśu sukham diśanti na akālatas ayam mriyate jāyate vā na akālatas puṣṭim agryām upaiti na akālataḥ sukhitam duḥkhitam vā na akālikam vastu samasti kiṃcid kālena ca ūṣmā praśamam prayāti kālena sarvam saphalatvam eti kālena sasyāni layam prayānti kālena saṃjīvati jīva lokaḥ ittham kāla ātmanaḥ tattvam yaḥ vijānāti tattvataḥ kāla ātmānam atikramya kāla atītam sa paśyati iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe kālamahimavarṇanam nāma saptamaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ saṃkhyā rūpasya kālasya kaḥ punar paramaḥ avadhiḥ vāyuḥ uvāca āyuṣaḥ saṃkhyā rūpasya kālasya śānttu atīta kalā avadhi akṣi pakṣma parikṣepaḥ nimeṣaḥ parikalpitaḥ tādṛśānām nimeṣāṇām kāṣṭhāḥ daśa ca paṃca ca muhūrttānām api triṃśat ahorātram pracakṣate jñeyam pitryam ahorātram māsaḥ kṛṣṇa sita ātmakaḥ māsaiḥ taiḥ ayanam ṣaḍbhiḥ varṣam dve ca ayanam matam dakṣiṇam ca ayanam rātriḥ tathā udagayanam dinam devānām māsaiḥ dvādaśabhiḥ tathā kalpe caturdaśa ekasmin manūnām parivṛttayaḥ ajñeya tvāt ca sarveṣām asaṃkhyeya tayā punar kalpānām vai sahasram ca brāhmam varṣam iha ucyate śatāni māse catvāri viṃśatyā sahitāni ca abde paṃca sahasrāṇi catvāriṃśat yutāni ca catvāriṃśat sahasrāṇi paṃca lakṣāṇi ca āyuṣi brahmā viṣṇoḥ dine ca ekaḥ viṣṇuḥ rudra dine tathā sākṣāt śivasya tad saṃkhyaḥ tathā saḥ api sadāśivaḥ catvāriṃśat sahasrāṇi paṃca lakṣāṇi ca āyuṣi tasmin sākṣāt śivena eṣa kāla ātmā sampravartate prajāḥ prajānām patayaḥ mūrttayaḥ ca sura asurāḥ yaḥ viśvātmā karma kāla svabhāva ādi arthe śaktiḥ yasya na ullaṃghanīyā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva bhāge kālaprabhāve tridevāyurvarṇanam nāma aṣṭamaḥ adhyāyaḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ katham jagat idam kṛtsnam vidhāya ca nidhāya ca ājñayā paramām krīḍām karoti parameśvaraḥ kena vā punar eva idam grasyate pṛthu kukṣiṇā vāyuḥ uvāca śaktiḥ prathama sambhūtā śānti atīta pada uttarā tatas māyā tatas avyaktam śivāt śaktimataḥ prabhoḥ śānti atīta padam śakteḥ tatas śānti pada kramāt tatas vidyā padam tasmāt pratiṣṭhā pada saṃbhavaḥ nivṛtti padam utpannam pratiṣṭhā padataḥ kramāt evam uktā samāsena sṛṣṭiḥ īśvara coditā ānulomyāt tathā eteṣām pratilomyena saṃhṛtiḥ asmāt pañca pada uddiṣṭāt paraḥ sraṣṭā samiṣyate kalābhiḥ paṃcabhiḥ vyāptam tasmāt viśvam idam jagat kim tu tatra api kartṛ tvam na avyaktasya na ca ātmanaḥ jagat ca kartṛ sāpekṣam kāryam sa avayavam yatas an ādi nidhanaḥ ca ayam mahat aiśvarya saṃyutaḥ saḥ eva jagataḥ kartā mahādevaḥ maheśvarāḥ pātā hartā ca sarvasya tatas pṛthak ananvayaḥ iti iyam śāśvatī niṣṭhā satām manasi vartate yāvat ādi samāraṃbhaḥ yāvat yaḥ pralayaḥ mahān tāvat apyeti sakalam brahmaṇaḥ śāradām śatam avyaktam ātmanaḥ kāryam ādāya ātmani tiṣṭhati praviśya kṣobhayāmāsa māyā yogāt maheśvaraḥ avyaktāt abhavat sṛṣṭiḥ ājñayā parameṣṭhinaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva bhāge sṛṣṭipālanapralayakartṛtvavarṇanam nāma navamaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca puruṣa dhiṣṭhitāt pūrvam avyaktāt īśvara ājñayā buddhi ādayaḥ viśeṣa antāḥ vikārāḥ ca abhavan kramāt kāraṇa tvena sarveṣām trayaḥ devāḥ prajajñire jñānam apratimam śaśvat aiśvaryam ca aṇima ādikam sṛṣṭi sthiti laya ākhyeṣu karmasu triṣu hetutām sarga rakṣā laya ācāram pratyekam pradadau ca saḥ na anena teṣām ādhikyam aiśvaryam ca atiricyate mūrkhāḥ nindanti tān vāgbhiḥ saṃraṃbha abhiniveśinaḥ yātudhānāḥ bhavanti eva piśācāḥ ca na saṃśayaḥ devaḥ guṇatraya atītaḥ catur vyūhaḥ maheśvaraḥ saḥ ayam ātmā trayasya asya prakṛteḥ puruṣasya ca līlā kṛta jagat sṛṣṭiḥ īśvara tve vyavasthitaḥ saḥ eva ca tad ādhāraḥ tad ātmā tad adhiṣṭhitaḥ pradhānāt prathamam jajñe vṛddhiḥ khyātiḥ matiḥ mahān tamaḥ yuktāt ahaṃkārāt bhūta tanmātra saṃbhavaḥ bhūtānām ādi bhūta tvāt bhūtādiḥ kathyate tu saḥ bhūtādeḥ śabda mātram syāt tatra ca ākāśa saṃbhavaḥ ākāśāt sparśaḥ utpannaḥ sparśāt vāyu samudbhavaḥ gandhāt ca pṛthivī jātā bhūtebhyaḥ nyat carācaram viṣṇu tve kevalam sattvam guṇa vṛddhiḥ tridhā vibhau sṛṣṭi pālana vidhvaṃsa karma kartryaḥ dvijottamāḥ vikārān ca tathā avyaktam sṛṣṭvā bhūyas niyacchati tat idam brahmaṇaḥ yoniḥ etat aṃḍam ghanam mahat ātyantikaḥ ca pralaya unta kāle līlā kṛtaḥ kevalam īśvarasya anādi madhya antam ananta vīryam śuklam su raktam puruṣeṇa yuktam aṣṭau vikārān api ca ādi kāle sṛṣṭvā samaśnāti tathā anta kāle iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām brahmāṃḍasthitivarṇanam nāma daśamaḥ adhyāyaḥ munayaḥ ūcuḥ manvantarāṇi sarvāṇi kalpa bhedān ca sarvaśas vāyuḥ uvāca caturdaśa mahābhāgāḥ manūnām parivṛttayaḥ anādi tvāt ananta tvāt ajñeya tvāt ca kṛtsnaśas manvantarāṇi kalpāḥ ca na śakyāḥ vacanāt pṛthak ukteṣu api ca sarveṣu śṛṇvatām vaḥ vacaḥ mama tatra saṃkṣipya vartante sṛṣṭayaḥ pratisṛṣṭayaḥ prāyas samābhavanti iti tarkaḥ kāryaḥ vijānatā pūrva kalpe parāvṛtte pravṛtte kālamārute samunmūlita mūleṣu vṛkṣeṣu ca vaneṣu ca dikṣu sarvāsu magnāsu vāripūre mahīyasi tad adbhiḥ caṭula ākṣepaiḥ taraṃga bhuja maṇḍalaiḥ prārabdha caṇḍa nṛtyeṣu tatas pralaya vāriṣu tam śṛṇudhvam muni śreṣṭhāḥ tad artham ca akṣara āśrayam śiva yoga mayīm nidrām kurvantam tridaśeśvaram baddhāṃjali puṭāḥ siddhāḥ jana loka nivāsinaḥ stotraiḥ prabodhayāmāsuḥ prabhāta samaye surāḥ yathā sṛṣṭi ādi samaye īśvaram śrutayaḥ purā sa vismayaḥ iva āsīnaḥ parām cintām upāgamat kva sā bhagavatī yā tu manojñā mahatī mahī nānāvidha mahā śaila nadī nagara kānanā jñātavān salile magnām dharaṇīm dharaṇīpatiḥ nīla megha pratīkāśam dīpta śabdam bhayānakam pīna vṛtta ghana skandha pīna unnata kaṭī taṭam hrasva vṛtta ūru jaṃghā agram su tīkṣṇa pura maṇḍalam padmarāga maṇi prakhyam vṛtta bhīṣaṇa locanam udīrṇa ucchvāsa niśvāsa ghūrṇita pralaya arṇavam maṇibhiḥ bhūṣaṇaiḥ citraiḥ mahā ratnaiḥ pariṣkṛtam sa tadā śuśubhe atīva sūkaraḥ giri saṃnibhaḥ liṃga ākṛteḥ maheśasya pāda mūlam gataḥ yathā uddhṛtya āliṃgya daṃṣṭrābhyām unmamajja rasātalāt vapuḥ mahāvarāhasya śuśubhe puṣpa saṃvṛtam svam eva rūpam āsthāya sthāpayāmāsa vai vibhuḥ iti śrī śivamahāpurāṇe saptamyām sṛṣṭyādivarṇanam nāma ekādaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca tam dṛṣṭvā asādhakam brahmā prathamam sargam īdṛśam manuṣya nāmā sañjātaḥ sargaḥ duḥkha samutkaṭaḥ teṣu sṛṣṭi anapekṣeṣu gateṣu sanaka ādiṣu sraṣṭu kāmaḥ punar brahmā tatāpa paramam tapaḥ te tena uktāḥ mahātmānaḥ daśadhā ca ekadhā kṛtāḥ yūyam sṛṣṭāḥ mayā vatsāḥ loka anugraha kāraṇāt mayā te prāṇitāḥ prāṇāḥ sukham uttiṣṭha suvrata tvam hi darśana mātreṇa ca ānandayasi me manaḥ mām viddhi paramātmānam tava putra tvam āgatam tasmāt tīvrām imām mūrcchām vidhūya mad anugrahāt ugrāya ugra svarūpāya yajamāna ātmane namaḥ prārthayāmāsa viśveśam girā praṇati pūrvayā sṛṣṭi hetoḥ tvam utpannaḥ mama aṃge anaṃga nāśanaḥ sahāyam kuru sarvatra sraṣṭum arhasi sa prajāḥ sraṣṭum tena abhyanujñātaḥ tathā anyāḥ ca asṛjat prajāḥ saha rudreṇa saṃbhūtāḥ purāṇāḥ gṛhamedhinaḥ saha rudreṇa sisṛkṣuḥ aṃbhasi etāni vai vidhiḥ sa sṛṣṭi artham samādhāya brahmā ātmānam ayūyujat mukhāt ajanayat devān pitR̥n ca eva upapakṣataḥ jaghanāt asurān sarvān prajanāt api mānuṣān vayāṃsi pakṣataḥ sṛṣṭāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ vakṣasaḥ asṛjat iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe sṛṣṭivarṇanam nāma dvādaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ bhavatā kathitā sṛṣṭiḥ bhavasya paramātmanaḥ deva śreṣṭhaḥ virūpākṣaḥ dīptaḥ śūla dharaḥ haraḥ yasya brahmā ca viṣṇuḥ ca praṇāmam kurutaḥ bhayāt devaḥ svakāt aṃgāt brahma viṣṇū purā sṛjat sa katham bhagavān rudraḥ ādidevaḥ purātanaḥ sṛṣṭau parasparasya aṃgāt iti prāk api śuśruma katham punar aśeṣāṇām bhūtānām hetu bhūtayoḥ guṇa pradhāna bhāvena prādurbhāvaḥ parasparāt na a pṛṣṭam bhavatā kiṃcid na a śrutam ca kathaṃcana vayam śraddhālavaḥ tāta śrotum īśvara sat yaśaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca sthāne pṛṣṭam idam viprāḥ bhavadbhiḥ praśna kovidaiḥ trayaḥ te kāraṇa ātmānaḥ jatāḥ sākṣāt maheśvarāt parama aiśvarya saṃyuktāḥ parameśvara bhāvitāḥ tad śaktyā adhiṣṭhitāḥ nityam tad kārya karaṇa kṣamāḥ pitrā niyamitāḥ pūrvam trayaḥ api triṣu karmasu brahmā sarge hariḥ trāṇe rudraḥ saṃharaṇe tathā tathā api anyonya mātsaryāt anyonya atiśaya āśinaḥ tapasā toṣayitvā svam pitaram parameśvaram brahma nārāyaṇau pūrvam rudraḥ kalpa antare viṣṇuḥ ca bhagavān rudram brahmāṇam asṛjat punar purā nārāyaṇaḥ nāma kalpe vai meghavāhane tasya bhāvam samālakṣya viṣṇoḥ viśva jagat guruḥ śaktim labdhvā tu sarvātmā śivāt sarva īśvarāt tadā sasarja bhagāvan viṣṇuḥ viśvam viśvasṛjā saha viṣṇoḥ tat vaibhavam dṛṣṭvā sṛṣṭaḥ tena pitāmahaḥ gaccha viṣṇo mayā jñātam tava sargasya kāraṇam sraṣṭā tvam bhagavān ādyaḥ pālakaḥ paramārthataḥ aham ca tapasā ārādhya rudram tridaśa nāyakam evam viṣṇum upālabhya bhagavān abja sambhavaḥ evam vijñāpayāmāsa tapasā prāpya śaṃkaram sa matsarāt upālabdhaḥ tvad āśraya balāt mayā tasya bhaktyā yathāpūrvam prasādam kṛtavān asi tathā mama api tat sarvam dātum arhasi śaṃkara nyāyena vai dadau sarvam tasya api sa ghṛṇā nidhiḥ labdhvā evam īśvarāt eva brahmā sarva ātma tām kṣaṇāt kṣīrārṇava ālaye śubhre vimāne sūrya saṃnibhe tam dṛṣṭvā puruṣam brahmā pragalbham idam abravīt grasāmi tvām aham viṣṇo tvam ātmānam yathā purā sṛṣṭaḥ ca brahmaṇā sadyas bhruvoḥ madhyāt ayatnataḥ tasmin avasare sākṣāt bhagavān indu bhūṣaṇaḥ śaktim tayoḥ api draṣṭum arūpaḥ rūpam āsthitaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe brahmaviṣṇusṛṣṭikathanam nāma trayodaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca yatas vicchinna saṃtānā brahma sṛṣṭiḥ pravartate kalpe kalpe prajāḥ sṛṣṭvā brahmā brahmāṃḍa saṃbhavaḥ a vṛddhi hetoḥ bhūtānām mumoha bhṛśa duḥkhitaḥ tasya duḥkha praśānti artham prajānām ca vivṛddhaye tad tad kalpeṣu kāla ātmā rudraḥ rudra gaṇa adhipaḥ nirdiṣṭaḥ pamameśena maheśaḥ nīlalohitaḥ putraḥ bhūtvā anugṛhṇāti brahmāṇam brahmaṇaḥ anujaḥ tad nāma nāmā tad rūpaḥ tad kārya karaṇa kṣamaḥ tad tulya vyavahāraḥ ca tad ājñā paripālakaḥ ga tuṃga taraṃga arddha piṃgala ānana mūrddhajaḥ bhagna daṃṣṭra aṃkura ākrānta prānta kānta dharādharaḥ savya śravaṇa pārśva anta maṃḍalīkṛta kuṇḍalaḥ evam ghora mahā rūpaḥ brahmaputrīm maheśvaraḥ pravāha rūpataḥ nityā prajā sṛṣṭiḥ pravartate kadācid prārthitaḥ sraṣṭum brahmaṇā nīlalohitaḥ kaparddinaḥ nirātaṃkān nīlagrīvān tri locanān jarā maraṇa nirmuktān dīpta śūla vara āyudhān tān dṛṣṭā vividhān rudrān rudram āha pitāmahaḥ iti uktaḥ prahasan prāha brahmāṇam parameśvaraḥ iti uktvā viśvakarmāṇam viśva bhūta īśvaraḥ haraḥ saha rudraiḥ prajā sargāt nivṛtta ātmā vyatiṣṭhata iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe rudrāvirbhāvavarṇanam nāma caturdaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca tadā maithuna jām sṛṣṭim brahmā kartum amanyata na nirgatam purā yasmāt nārīṇām kulam īśvarāt tena maithuna jām sṛṣṭim na śaśāka pitāmahaḥ prajānam eva vṛddhi artham praṣṭavyaḥ parameśvara evam saṃcintya viśvātmā tapaḥ kartum pracakrame nirguṇā niṣprapaṃcā ca niṣkalā nirupaplavā nirantaratarā nityā nityam īśvara pārśva gā tīvreṇa tapasā tasya yuktasya parameṣṭhinaḥ tatas kenacid aṃśena mūrtim āviśya kām api arddhanārīśvaraḥ bhūtvā yayau devaḥ svayam haraḥ advitīyam anirdeśyam adṛśyam akṛtātmabhiḥ acalam nirguṇam śāntam ananta mahima āspadam śraddhā vinaya saṃpannaiḥ śrāvyaiḥ saṃskara saṃyutaiḥ tuṣṭāva devam devīm ca sūktaiḥ sūkṣma artha gocaraiḥ jaya śāśvatika aiśvarye jaya śāśvatika ālaya jaya śāśvatika ākāra jaya śāśvatika anuga jaya avalokana āyatta jagat kāraṇa bṛṃhaṇa jaya deva adya vijñeye sva ātma sūkṣma dṛśā ujjvale jaya vyāpta carācare jaya asura śiraḥ niṣṭha śreṣṭha anuga kadaṃbaka jaya upāśrita saṃrakṣā saṃvidhāna paṭīyasi jaya unmūlita saṃsāra viṣa vṛkṣa aṃkura udgame jaya prādeśika aiśvarya vīrya śaurya vijṛṃbhaṇa jaya praṇīta paṃcārtha prayoga parama amṛta jaya paṃcārtha vijñāna sudhā stotra svarūpiṇi jaya anādi mala ajñāna tamaḥ paṭala candrike vijñāpya evaṃvidhaiḥ sūktaiḥ viśvakarmā caturmukhaḥ namaścakāra rudrāya radrāṇyai ca muhur muhur arddhanārīśvaram nāma śivayoḥ harṣa varddhanam yaḥ idam kīrttayet bhaktyā yasya kasya api śikṣayā sa tat phalam avāpnoti śivayoḥ prīti kāraṇāt sakala bhuvana bhūta bhāvanābhyām janana vināśa vihīna vigrahābhyām nara vara yuvatī vapurddharābhyām satatam aham praṇataḥ asmi śaṃkarābhyām iti śrī śivamahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām śivaśivāstutivarṇanam nāma paṃcadaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca vācā madhura gaṃbhīra śiva da ślakṣṇa varṇayā artha saṃpanna padayā rāja lakṣaṇa yuktayā saṃbabhāṣe su saṃpītaḥ viśvakarmāṇam īśvaraḥ īśvaraḥ uvāca prajānām eva bṛddhi artham tapaḥ taptam tvayā adhunā iti uktvā parama udāram svabhāva madhuram vacaḥ sasarja vapuṣaḥ bhāgāt devīm deva varaḥ haraḥ yām āhuḥ brahma vidvāṃsaḥ devīm divya guṇa anvitām yā bhavānī bhavasya aṃgāt samāvirabhavat kila sā bhartuḥ vapuṣaḥ bhāgāt jātā iva samadṛśyata yā sā jagat idam kṛtsnam mahimnā vyāpya tiṣṭhati śarīriṇī iva sa devī vicitram samalakṣyata sarvam jagat idam ca eṣā saṃmohayati māyayā īśvarāt sā eva jātā abhūt ajātā paramārthataḥ na yasyāḥ paramaḥ bhāvaḥ surāṇām api gocaraḥ viśva amara īśvarī ca eva vibhaktā bhartuḥ aṃgataḥ paramām nikhilam bhāsā bhāsayantīm idam jagat praṇipatya mahādevīm prārthayāmāsa vai virāj na vṛddhim upagacchanti sṛjyamānāḥ punar punar mithuna prabhavām eva kṛtvā sṛṣṭim atas param na nirgatam purā tvattaḥ nārīṇām kulam avyayam tena nārī kulam sraṣṭum śaktiḥ mama na vidyate sarvāsām eva śaktīnām tvattaḥ khalu samudbhavaḥ tasmāt sarvatra sarveṣām sarva śakti pradāyinīm tvām eva vara dām māyām prārthayāmi sureśvarīm evam sā yācitā devī brahmaṇā brahma yoninā tām ājñām parameśasya śirasā pratigṛhya sā dattvā evam atulām śaktim brahmaṇe brahma rūpiṇīm tadā prabhṛti loke asmin striyām bhogaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ brahmā api prāpa sa ānandam santoṣam muni puṃgavāḥ puṇya vṛddhi karam śrāvyam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe devīśaktyudbhavaḥ nāma ṣoḍaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca evam labdhvā parām śaktim īśvarāt eva śāśvatīm ākūtiḥ ekā vijñeyā prasūtiḥ aparā smṛtā kāma ādyāḥ tu yaśaḥ antāḥ ye te trayodaśa sūnavaḥ dharmasya jajñire tāḥ tu śraddhā ādyāḥ su sukha uttarāḥ duḥkha uttarāḥ ca hiṃsāyām adharmasya ca saṃtatau nikṛti ādayaḥ utpannāḥ putrāḥ ca dharma lakṣaṇāḥ yā sā dakṣasya duhitā rudrasya dayitā satī bhartṛ nindā prasaṃgena tyaktvā dākṣāyiṇīm tanum dakṣam ca dakṣa bhāryyām ca vinindya saha bandhubhiḥ sā menāyām āvirabhūt putrī himavataḥ gireḥ rudraḥ tu tām satīm dṛṣṭvā rudrān tu ātma sama prabhān yathā asṛjat asaṃkhyātān tathā kathitam eva ca svāyaṃbhuve antare nītāḥ sarve te bhārgavāḥ matāḥ yeṣām vaṃśe samutpannaḥ bahuputrasya kaśyapaḥ smṛtiḥ ca aṃgirasaḥ patnī janayāmāsa vai sutau āgnīdhram śarabham ca eva tathā kanyā catuṣṭayam tad saṃtatīyāḥ bahavaḥ paulastyāḥ iti viśrutāḥ atreḥ bhāryā anusūyā ca pañca ātreyān asūyata somaḥ ca paṃcamaḥ tu ete paṃca ātreyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ svāyaṃbhuve antare atītāni arbudāni śatāni ca samāsāt vistarāt vaktum aśakyaḥ ayam iti dvijāḥ svāhā tasya priyā lebhe putrān trīn amita ojasaḥ sūrye tapati yaḥ ca asau śuciḥ sauraḥ udāhṛtaḥ havyavāhaḥ kavyavāhaḥ saharakṣāḥ iti trayaḥ trayāṇām kramaśas putrāḥ deva pitṛ surāḥ ca te sarve rudra ātmakaḥ ca eva sarve rudra parāyaṇāḥ tat sarvam rudram uddiśya dattam syāt na atra saṃśayaḥ yasmāt ṣaṭ ṛtavaḥ teṣām sthānam sthāna abhimāninām yuṣmāt ṛtuṣu sarve hi jāyante sthāsnu jaṃgamā evam pitRṇām eteṣām ṛtu kāla abhimāninām ātma aiśvaryāḥ mahātmānaḥ tiṣṭhanti iha abbhra saṃgamāt ayajvānaḥ ca yajvānaḥ kramāt te mṛhamedhinaḥ agniṣvātta sutā menā dharaṇī barhiṣad sutā gaurīm gaṃgām ca suṣuve bhava aṃga āśleṣa pāvanīm sākṣāt śrīkaṃṭha nāthasya śivasya āvasatham gataḥ sā asūtā dharaṇī bhūyas triṃśat kanyāḥ ca viśrutāḥ iti ete brahma putrāṇām dharma ādīnām mahātmanām na ati saṃkṣepataḥ viprāḥ na ati vistarataḥ kramāt prajānām saṃniveśaḥ ayam prajāpati samudbhavaḥ rājñām api ca yaḥ vaṃśaḥ dvidhā saḥ api pravartate sūryavaṃśaḥ somavaṃśaḥ iti puṇyatamaḥ kṣitau ikṣvākuḥ ambarīṣaḥ ca yayātiḥ nāhuṣa ādayaḥ puṇya ślokāḥ śrutāḥ ye tatra te pi tad vaṃśa saṃbhavāḥ anye ca rāja ṛṣayaḥ nānā vīrya samanvitā kim taiḥ phalam anutkrāntaiḥ ukta pūrvaiḥ purātanaiḥ kim ca īśvara kathā vṛttā yatra tatra anya kīrtanam prasaṃgāt īśvarasya eva prabhāva dyotanāt api iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe sṛṣṭikathanam nāma saptadaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ devī dakṣasya tanayā tyaktvā dākṣāyaṇī tanum utpannaḥ ca katham dakṣaḥ abhiśāpāt bhavasya tu vāyuḥ uvāva śṛṇvantu kathayiṣyāmi dakṣasya laghu cetasaḥ vṛttam pāpāt pramādāt ca viśva amara vidūṣaṇam kadācid draṣṭum īśānam himavat śikharam yayuḥ darśanam dadatuḥ teṣām deva ādīnām dvijottamāḥ jāmātaram haram draṣṭum draṣṭum ca ātma sutām satīm deva ādibhyaḥ viśeṣeṇa na kadācid abhūt smṛtiḥ tasya tasyāḥ param bhāvam ajñātuḥ ca api kevalam putrī iti evam vimūḍhasya tasyām vairam ajāyata na ajuvāha bhavam dakṣaḥ dīkṣitaḥ tām api dviṣan anyān jāmātaraḥ sarvān āhūya sa yathākramam śataśas puṣkalām arcān cakāra ca pṛthak pṛthak yayau rudrāya rudrāṇī vijñāpya bhavanam pituḥ lakṣaṇa āḍhyam sukha āroham atimātra manoharam muktā āmaya vitāna agnyam sraj dāma samalaṃkṛtam tapta kaṃcana nirvyūham ratna staṃbha śata āvṛtam puṣpa paṭṭa paristīrṇam citra ratna mahā āsanam maṇi daṃḍa manojñena mahā vṛṣabha lakṣmaṇā alaṃkṛta purobhāgam abbhra śubbhreṇa ketunā ratna kaṃcuka gupta aṃgaiḥ citra vetraka pāṇibhiḥ adhiṣṭhita mahā dvāram apradhṛṣyaiḥ guṇa īśvaraiḥ mṛdaṃga tāla gīta ādi veṇu vīṇā viśāradaiḥ vidagdha veṣa bhāṣaiḥ ca bahubhiḥ strī janaiḥ vṛtam cāmāra vyañjanam tasyāḥ vajra daṃḍa manohare chatram śaśi nibham tasyāḥ cūḍā upari su mālinī upari amṛta bhāṃḍasya maṃḍalam śaśinaḥ yathā atha ca agre samāsīnā su smita āsyā śubhāvatī suyaśāḥ pāduke devyāḥ śubhe ratna pariṣkṛte anyā kāṃcana cāru aṃgī dīptam jagrāha darpaṇam aparā tālavṛntam ca parā tāṃbūla peṭikām kācid krīḍā śukam cāru kare akuruta bhāminī kācid ābharaṇa ādhāram babhāra kamala īkṣaṇā kācid ca punar ālepam su prasūtam śubha aṃjanam anyāḥ ca sadṛśāḥ tāḥ tāḥ yathāsvam ucita kriyāḥ āvṛttyā tām mahādevīm asevanta samantataḥ atīva śuśubhe tāsām antare parameśvarī tārā pariṣadaḥ madhye candra lekhā iva śāradī prāsthānikaḥ mahā nādaḥ paṭahaḥ samatāḍyata anāhatāni sanneduḥ kāhalānām śatāni ca sa āyudhānām gaṇeśānām maheśa sama tejasām sahasrāṇi śatāni aṣṭau tadānīm puratas yayuḥ teṣām madhye vṛṣa ārūḍhaḥ gaja ārūḍhaḥ yathā guruḥ jagāma gaṇapaḥ śrīmān somanandīśvara arcitaḥ sasṛjuḥ puṣpa vṛṣṭim ca vitāna upari vāridāḥ tadā deva gaṇaiḥ ca anyaiḥ pathi sarvatra saṃgatā kṣaṇāt iva pituḥ geham praviveśa maheśvarī tām dṛṣṭvā kupitaḥ dakṣaḥ ca ātmanaḥ kṣaya kāraṇāt tasyāḥ yavīyasībhyaḥ api cakre pūjāma satkṛtām tadā śaśimukhī devī pitaram sadasi sthitam aṃbikā yuktam avyagram uvāca akṛpaṇam vacaḥ sa devaḥ sāṃpratam tāta vidhinā na arcitaḥ kila tadā āstām mama jyāyasyāḥ putryāḥ pūjām kim īdṛśīm stabdha ātmā tāmasaḥ śarvaḥ tvam imam samupāśritā tathā uktā pitaram dakṣam kruddhā devī tam abravīt śṛṇvatām eva sarveṣām ye yajña sadasi sthitāḥ akasmāt mama bhartāram ajāta aśeṣa dūṣaṇam vācā dūṣayase dakṣa sākṣāt loka mahā īśvaram te ete bahu pāpmānaḥ sarve daṃḍyāḥ iti śrutiḥ tasmāt ati utkaṭasya asya pāpasya sadṛśaḥ bhṛśam sahasā dāruṇaḥ daṃḍaḥ tava daivāt bhaviṣyati tasmāt tava kulam duṣṭam naṣṭam iti avadhāraya iti uktvā pitaram ruṣṭā satī saṃtyakta sādhvasā tad artham eva kṛtavān suciram duścaram tapaḥ tasmāt tam anugṛhṇāti bhūdhara īśvaram īśvarī yadā gatā satī dakṣam vinindya bhaya vihvalā dakṣāya ca ṛṣibhyaḥ ca cukopa ca śaśāpa tān antare brahmaṇaḥ yajñe vāruṇīm bibhrataḥ tanum tadā dakṣasya dharma artham yajñe tasya durātmanaḥ maheśaḥ kṛtavān vighnam manāḥ vavasvate sati iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe satīdehatyāgaḥ nāma aṣṭādaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ vāyuḥ uvāca saṃkīḍati tayā sārddham kāle bahutare gate dadhīcaḥ manyunā āviṣṭaḥ dakṣam evam abhāṣata dadhīcaḥ uvāca dakṣaḥ uvāca dadhīcaḥ uvāca rājā ced adhvarasya asya na rudraḥ pūjyate tvayā yaḥ sraṣṭā ca eva saṃhartā bhartā ca eva maheśvaraḥ tasmāt anyam na paśyāmi śaṃkara ātmānam adhvare dakṣaḥ uvāca dadhīcaḥ uvāca tasmāt dakṣa tava aśeṣaḥ yajñaḥ ayam na bhaviṣyati etasmin eva kāle tu jñātvā etat sarvam īśvarāt devyā saṃcoditaḥ devaḥ dakṣa adhvara jighāṃsayā gaṇḍa dvitaya saṃsṛṣṭa maṇḍalīkṛta kuṇḍalam vara amara śiraḥ mālā āvalī kalita śekharam raṇat nūpura keyūra mahā kanaka bhūṣitam ratna saṃcaya saṃdīptam tāra hāra āvṛta urasam mahā śarabha śārdūla siṃhaiḥ sadṛśa vikramam śaṃkha cāmara kunda indu mṛṇāla sadṛśa prabham sa tuṣāram iva adri indram sākṣāt jaṃgama tām gatam pārśvatas devadevasya paryatiṣṭhat gaṇeśvaraḥ tam dṛṣṭvā avasthitam vīrabhadram kālāgni sannibham sa ca vijñāpayāmāsa saha devyā maheśvaram vīrabhadram mahā bāhum vācā vipula nādayā aham api anayā sārddham raibhya āśrama sapīpataḥ sthitvā vīkṣe gaṇeśāna vikramam tava duḥsaham āliṃgya ca samāghrāya mūrdhni ṣaḍvadanam yathā dakṣam vaireṇa tam tasmāt bhindhi yajñam gaṇeśvara yajña lakṣmīm alakṣmīm tvam bhadra kṛtvā mama ājñayā yajamānam ca tam hatvā vatsa hiṃsaya bhadrayā aśeṣām iva tām ājñām śivayoḥ citra kṛtyayoḥ dakṣiṇāt bhuja deśāt tu śata koṭi ga viśvarān pādāt tathā ūru deśāt ca pṛṣṭhāt pārśvāt mukhāt galāt guhyāt gulphāt śiraḥ madhyāt kaṃṭhāt āsyāt tathā udarāt saṃchāditam abhūt sarvam sa ākāśa vivaram jagat kālāgni rudra sadṛśāḥ tri netrāḥ ca jaṭā dharāḥ nipetuḥ bhṛśam ākāśe śataśas siṃhavāhanāḥ vineduḥ ca mahā nādān jaladāḥ iva bhadrajāḥ kāla anala śataiḥ yuktaḥ yathā ante kālabhairavaḥ teṣām madhye samāruhya vṛṣa indram vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ jagāma bhagavān bhadraḥ śubham abhram yathā bhavaḥ babhāra mauktikam chatram gṛhīta sita cāmaraḥ bhagavān iva śaila indraḥ pārśve viśva jagat guroḥ saḥ api tena babhau bhadraḥ śveta cāmara pāṇinā bāla somena saumyena yathā śūla vara āyudhaḥ bhānu kaṃpaḥ mahā tejāḥ hema ratnaiḥ alaṃkṛtaḥ vavṛṣuḥ śataśas mūrdhni puṣpa varṣam balāhakāḥ phullānām madhu garbhāṇām puṣpāṇām gandha bandhavaḥ mārga anukūla saṃvāhāḥ vabuḥ ca pathi mārutāḥ tatas gaṇeśvarāḥ sarve mattāḥ yuddha bala uddhatāḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe vīrabhadrotpattivarṇanam nāma ekonaviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca tatas viṣṇu pradhānānām surāṇām amita ojasām sudarbha ṛtu saṃstīrṇam su samiddha hutāśanam vidhinā veda dṛṣṭena su anuṣṭhita bahu kramam dṛṣṭvā dakṣa adhvare vīraḥ vīrabhadraḥ pratāpavān siṃha nādam tadā cakre gaṃbhīraḥ jaladaḥ yathā gaṇeśvaraiḥ kṛtaḥ jajñe mahān nyakkṛta sāgaraḥ dudruvuḥ paritas bhītāḥ srasta vastra vibhūṣaṇāḥ kim svid bhagnaḥ mahā meruḥ kim svid saṃdīryate mahī kim idam kim idam vā iti jajalpuḥ tridaśāḥ bhṛśam mṛga indrāṇām yathā nādam gaja indrāḥ gahane vane śrutvā tathāvidham kecid tatyajuḥ jīvitam bhayāt tam dṛṣṭvā bhīta bhītaḥ api dakṣaḥ dṛḍhaḥ iva sthitaḥ vīrabhadraḥ mahā tejāḥ megha saṃbhīra nissvanaḥ smayan iva tam ālokya dakṣam devāḥ ca ṛtvijaḥ kathyatām kāraṇam tatra yudhyatām vā mayā amaraiḥ ūcuḥ mantrāḥ pramāṇam naḥ na vayam prabhavaḥ tu iti mantra uktāḥ api te devāḥ sarve saṃmūḍha cetasaḥ bhadrāya na daduḥ bhāgam tad prahāṇam abhīpsavaḥ yadā tathyam ca pathyam ca sva vākyam tat vṛthā abhavat yakṣa vāṭam mahā kūṭam yathā atisraḥ puras haraḥ yūpān utpāṭya hotR̥ṇām kaṃṭheṣu ābadhya rajjubhiḥ yajña pātrāṇi citrāṇi bhittvā saṃcūrṇya vāriṇi kṣīra nadyaḥ amṛta srāvāḥ su snigdha dadhi kardamāḥ rasavanti ca pānāni lehya coṣyāṇi tāni vai vajraiḥ cakraiḥ mahā śūlaiḥ śaktibhiḥ pāśa paṭṭiśaiḥ uddhatān tridaśān sarvān lokapāla nirmathya sa indrān amarān gaṇa indrān vṛṣa indra nāga indra mṛgendra sārāḥ cakruḥ bahūni apratima bhāvāḥ sa harṣa romāṇi viceṣṭitāni ākṣipya kecid ca vara āyudhāni mahā bhujaṃgān iva vainateyāḥ udvartita dvāra kapāṭa kuḍyam vidhvasta śālā valabhī gavākṣam aho bata abhajyata yajña vāṭam anātha vat vākyam iva ayathārtham iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe iva mattasya jagāma prekṣaṇīya tām mahā sarovaram yadvat mattaḥ vāraṇa yūthapaḥ tatra tatra mahā vegāt carantam śūla dhāriṇam prabhayā iva yuga anta agniḥ calayā dhūma dhūmrayā kalpe śeṣa anala jvālā nāsikā agram su śobhanam ciccheda ca kuṭhāreṇa bāhu daṃḍam vibhāvasoḥ agratas dvi aṃgulām jihvām mātuḥ devyāḥ lulāva ca svāhādevyāḥ tathā devaḥ dakṣiṇam nāsikā puṭam prasahya utpāṭayāmāsa bhadraḥ parama vegavān pūṣṇaḥ daśana rekhām ca dīptām muktā āvalīm iva jaghāna dhanuṣaḥ koṭyā sa tena aspaṣṭa vāc abhūt kṣaṇam kṛmi vat ākramya gharṣayāmāsa bhū tale tat prahṛṣṭā samādāya śiraḥ tāla phala abhimatā bhartuḥ api sarva aṃga sundarī sā vṛthā eva hi jāyeta sarvaiḥ api guṇa antaraiḥ tathā asatī anyathābhūtā nārī kutra upayujyate varṇa antaram bhajiṣye vā na bhajiṣyāmi vā svayam iti uktvā utthāya śayanāt devī sā ācaṣṭa gadgadam pādayoḥ praṇaman eva bhavānīm pratyabhāṣata īśvaraḥ uvāca a jānatī ca krīḍā uktim priye kim kupitā asi me pṛthagjanānām rataye kāma ātmā kalpitaḥ mayā tatas katham upālabdhaḥ kāma dāhāt aham tvayā mām vai tridaśa sāmānyam manyamānaḥ manobhavaḥ vihāraḥ upya āvayoḥ asya jagataḥ trāṇa kāraṇāt śruta pūrvam hi bhagavan tava cāṭu vacaḥ mayā prāṇān api apriyā bhartuḥ nārī yā na parityajet kula aṃganā śubhā sadbhiḥ kutsitā eva hi gamyate krīḍā uktiḥ api kāli iti ghaṭate katham anyathā śivaḥ uvāca mama icchayā sva icchayā vā varṇa antaravatī bhava īśvaraḥ uvāca mad prasādāt purā brahmā brahma tvam prāptavān purā tam āhūya mahādevi tapasā kim kariṣyasi tathā api ārādhya tapasā brahmāṇam tvad niyogataḥ purā kila satī nāmnā dakṣasya duhitā abhavam jagatām patim evam tvām patim prāptavatī tathā iti śrī śivamahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe śivamandaragirinivāsakrīḍoktavarṇanam nāma caturviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca tapaḥ kṛtavatī pūrvam deśe yasmin sakhī janaiḥ tatas sva pitaram dṛṣṭvā mātaram ca tayoḥ gṛhe praṇamya vṛttam vijñāpya tābhyām ca anumatā satī punar tapaḥ vanam gatvā bhūṣaṇāni visṛjya ca snātvā tapasvinaḥ veṣam kṛtvā parama pāvanam tam eva kṣaṇike liṃge dhyātvā bāhya vidhānataḥ tathā tapaḥ carantīm tām kāle bahutithe gate dṛṣṭaḥ kaścid mahā vyāghraḥ duṣṭa bhāvāt upāgamat gātram citra arpitam iva stabdham tasyāḥ sakāśatas tam dṛṣṭvā api tathā vyāghram duṣṭa bhāvāt upāgatam sa tu viṣṭabdha sarva aṃgaḥ bubhukṣā paripīḍitaḥ nirīkṣyamāṇaḥ satatam devīm eva tadā aniśam devyāḥ ca hṛdaye nityam mama eva ayam upāsakaḥ trātā ca duṣṭa sattvebhyaḥ iti pravavṛte kṛpā tasyāḥ eva kṛpā yogāt sadyas naṣṭa mala trayaḥ babhūva sahasā vyāghraḥ devīm ca bubudhe tadā tadā parama bhāvena jñātvā kārtārthyam ātmanaḥ duṣṭānām api sattvānām tathā anyeṣān durātmanām tapaḥ ca vavṛdhe devyāḥ tīvram tīvratara ātmakam cakruḥ nivedanam devāḥ sva duḥkhasya ari pīḍanāt saḥ api śrutvā vidhiḥ duḥkham surāṇām kṛpayā anvitaḥ āsīt daitya vadhāya eva smṛtvā hetu āśrayām kathām dadarśa ca suraśreṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhe tapasi niṣṭhitām pratiṣṭhām iva viśvasya bhavānīm parameśvarīm rudrasya ca pituḥ bhāryām āryām adrīśvara ātmajām brahmāṇam āgatam dṛṣṭvā devī deva gaṇaiḥ saha tapaḥ phalānām sarveṣām tvad adhīnāḥ hi siddhayaḥ bhartāram ātmanā prāpya prāptam ca tapasaḥ phalam sarga ādau bhavataḥ devāt utpattiḥ śrūyate yadā tadā prajānām prathamaḥ tvam me prathama jaḥ sutaḥ yadā punar prajā vṛddhyai lalāṭāt bhavataḥ bhavaḥ utpannaḥ abhūt tadā tvam me guruḥ śvaśura bhāvataḥ tat īdṛśasya bhavataḥ lokayātrā vidhāyinaḥ kim atra bahunā dehe yaḥ ca ayam mama kālimā etāvatā kimarthena tīvram devi tapaḥ kṛtam svecchā eva kim aparyāptā krīḍā iyam hi tava īdṛśī atas mama iṣṭam anayā phalam kim api sādhyatām niśuṃbha śuṃbha nāmānau daityau datta varau mayā alam vilaṃbanena atra tvam kṣaṇena sthirā bhava brāhmaṇa abhyarthitā ca eva devī giri vara ātmajā tvac kośam sahasā utsṛjya gaurī sā samajāyata sā tvak kośa ātmanā utsṛṣṭā kauśikī nāma nāmataḥ kālī kāla ambuda prakhyā kanyakā samapadyata sā tu māyā ātmikā śaktiḥ yoganidrā ca vaiṣṇavī śaṃkha cakra triśūla ādi sa āyudha aṣṭa mahā bhujā a jāta puṃsparśa ratiḥ adhṛṣyā ca ati sundarī dattā ca brahmaṇe devyā śaktiḥ eṣā sanātanī niśuṃbhasya ca śuṃbhasya nihantrī daitya siṃhayoḥ brahmaṇā api prahṛṣṭena tasyai parama śaktaye vindhye ca vasatim tasyāḥ pūjām āsava pūrvakaiḥ māṃsaiḥ matsyaiḥ apūpaiḥ ca nirvartya asau samādiśat sā ca eva saṃmatā śaktiḥ brahmaṇaḥ viśvakarmaṇaḥ praṇamya mātaram gaurīm brahmāṇam ca anupūrvaśas śaktibhiḥ ca api tulyābhiḥ sva ātmajābhiḥ anekaśas tad bāṇaiḥ kāma bāṇaiḥ ca chinna bhinna aṃga mānasau ūhanīyam parasmāt ca prastutam varṇayāmi vaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe devīgauratvabhavanam nāma paṃcaviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca utpādya kauśikīm gaurī brahmaṇe pratipādya tām dṛṣṭaḥ kim eṣa bhavatā śārdūlaḥ mad upāśrayaḥ bhavitavyam anena atas mama antaḥpura cāriṇā gaṇeśvara padam ca asmai prītyā dāsyati śaṃkaraḥ pradīyatām anujñā me prajānām patinā tvayā tasya tīvraiḥ purāvṛttaiḥ daurātmyam samavarṇayat paśau devi mṛgāḥ krūrāḥ kva ca te anugrahaḥ śubhaḥ āśīviṣa mukhe sākṣāt amṛtam kim niṣicyate vyāghra mātreṇa san eṣa duṣṭaḥ kaḥ api niśācaraḥ tarpayan tān yathākāmam kāmarūpī carati asau atas kim kṛpayā kṛtyam īdṛśeṣu durātmasu anena devyāḥ kim kṛtyam prakṛtyā kaluṣa ātmanā yat uktam bhavatā sarvam tathyam astu ayam īdṛśaḥ tathā api mām prapannaḥ abhūt na tyājyaḥ mām upāśritaḥ puṇya karmā api kim kuryyāt tvadīya ajñāna apekṣayā tvad adhīnā hi sarveṣām bandha mokṣa vyavasthitiḥ tvat ṛte paramā śaktiḥ saṃsiddhiḥ kasya karmaṇā tvam eva vividhā śaktiḥ bhavānām atha vā svayam aśaktaḥ karma karaṇe kartā vā kim kariṣyati tad tad aiśvarya samprāptyai tava eva ājñā hi kāraṇam atītāḥ khalu asaṃkhyātāḥ brahmāṇaḥ harayaḥ bhavāḥ anāgatāḥ tu asaṃkhyātāḥ tvad ājñā anuvidhāyinaḥ tvām an ārādhya deveśi puruṣa artha catuṣṭayam sukṛtam duṣkṛtam ca api tvayā iva sthāpitam yatas samasta lokayātrā artham mūrtim āviśya kām api krīḍase vividhaiḥ bhāvaiḥ kaḥ tvām jānāti tattvataḥ iti ātmanaḥ param bhāvam smārayitvā anurūpataḥ devīm ca mātaram dṛṣṭvā menām himavatā saha praṇamya āśvāsya bahudhā pitarau viraha asahau viprayoga śucā iva agre puṣpa bāṣpam vimuṃcataḥ tad tuccha ākhā samārūḍha vihagaḥ dīritaiḥ rutaiḥ vyākulam bahudhā dīnam vilāpam iva kurvataḥ sarveṣām jagatām dhātā kartā pātā vināśa kṛt iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe vyāghragativarṇanam nāma ṣaḍviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ kṛtvā gauram vapuḥ divyam devī giri vara ātmajā katham dadarśa bhartāram praviṣṭā manditam satī gaṇeśaiḥ kim kṛtam devaḥ tān dṛṣṭvā kim tadā akarot vāyuḥ uvāca pravaktum aṃjasā yena praṇaya garbheṇa bhāvaḥ bhāvavatām hṛtaḥ śaṃkamānā praviṣṭā antar tat ca sā samapaśyata praṇamya nā utthitā yāvat tāvat tām parameśvaraḥ pragṛhya dorbhyām āśliṣya paritas parayā mudā sva aṃke dhartum pravṛttā api sā paryyaṃke nyaṣīdata tayā saṃbhāṣaṇāya īśaḥ pūrva bhāṣitam abravīt sā daśā ca vyatītā kim tava sarva aṃga sundari sva icchayā api na kālī iti na anya varṇavatī iti ca tvad svabhāva āhṛtam cittam subhru ciṃtā āvaham mama vismṛtaḥ paramaḥ bhāvaḥ katham sva icchā aṃga yogataḥ aham agni śiraḥ niṣṭhaḥ tvam soma śirasi sthitā agnīṣoma ātmakam viśvam āvābhyām samadhiṣṭhitam jagat hitāya caratoḥ sva icchā dhṛta śarīrayoḥ tvam hi vāc amṛtam sākṣāt aham artha amṛtam param vidyā vedya ātmanoḥ eva viśleṣaḥ katham āvayoḥ na karmaṇā sṛjāmi idam jagat pratisṛjāmi ca sarvasya ājñā eka labhya tvāt ājñā tvam hi garīyasī ājñā eka sāram aiśvaryyam yasmāt svātantrya lakṣaṇam ājñayā viprayuktasya ca aiśvaryyam mama kīdṛśam devānām kāryyam uddiśya līlā uktim kṛtavān aham iti priyaṃvade sākṣāt īśvare parameśvare śṛṃgāra bhāva sārāṇām janma bhūmiḥ akṛtrimā lajjayā na kim api ūce kauśikī varṇanāt param tat eva varṇayāmi adya śṛṇu devyāḥ ca varṇanam kim devena na sā dṛṣṭā yā sṛṣṭā kauśikī mayā tasyāḥ vīryyam balam vindhya nilayam vijayam tathā śuṃbhasya ca niśuṃbhasya māraṇe ca raṇe tayoḥ lokānām rakṣaṇam śaśvat brahmā vijñāpayiṣyati iti saṃbhāṣamāṇāyāḥ devyāḥ eva ājñayā tadā vyāghraḥ sakhyā samānīya puras avasthāpitaḥ tadā tam prekṣya āha punar devī deva ānītam upāyatam vyāghram paśya na ca anena sadṛśaḥ mad upāsakaḥ atīva mama bhaktaḥ ca viśrabdhaḥ ca sva rakṣaṇāt sva deśam ca parityajya prasāda artham samāgataḥ yadi prītiḥ abhūt mattaḥ parām prītim karoṣi me vāyuḥ uvāca madhuram praṇaya udarkam śrutvā devyāḥ śubham vacaḥ prītaḥ asmi iti āha tam devaḥ sa ca adṛśyata tad kṣaṇāt churikām uraga prakhyām gaṇeśaḥ rakṣa veṣa dhṛk somanandī iti vikhyātaḥ tasmāt eṣa samākhyayā ittham devyāḥ priyam kṛtvā devaḥ ca ṛddha indu bhūṣaṇaḥ bhūṣayāmāsa tat divyaiḥ bhūṣaṇaiḥ ratna bhūṣitaiḥ tatas sa gaurīm giriśaḥ girīndrajām sa gauravām sarva manoharām haraḥ paryyaṃkam āropya varāṃga bhūṣaṇaiḥ vibhūṣayāmāsa śaśāṃka bhūṣaṇaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ devīm samādadhānena devena idam kim īritam agniṣoma ātmakam viśvam vāc artha ātmakam iti api ājñā eka sāram aiśvaryyam ājñā tvam iti ca uditam tat idam śrotum icchāmaḥ yathāvat anupūrvaśas vāyuḥ uvāca somaḥ śāktaḥ amṛta mayaḥ śakteḥ śānti karī tanuḥ amṛtam yat pratiṣṭhā sā tejaḥ vidyā kalā svayam bhūta sūkṣmeṣu sarveṣu te eva rasa tejasī dvividhā tejasaḥ vṛtti sūryya ātmā ca anala ātmikā tathā eva rasa vṛttiḥ ca soma ātmā ca jala ātmikā haviṣe sasya sampattiḥ vṛṣṭiḥ sasya abhivṛddhaye vṛṣṭeḥ eva haviḥ tasmāt agnīṣoma dhṛtam jagat agniḥ ūrddhvam jvalati eṣa yāvat saumyam para amṛtam yāvat ādahanam ca ūrddhvam adhas ca āplāvanam bhavet ādhāra śaktyā eva dhṛtaḥ kālāgniḥ ayam ūrddhva gaḥ tathā eva nimnagaḥ somaḥ śiva śakti pada āspadaḥ śivaḥ ca ūrddhvam adhas śaktiḥ ūrddhvam śaktiḥ adhas śivaḥ tat ittham śiva śaktibhyāt na avyāptam iha kiñcana yaḥ ca ittham bhasma sadbhāvam jñātvā snāti ca bhasmanā śāktena amṛtavarṣeṇa ca adhikārān nivartayet atas mṛtyuṃjayāya ittham amṛta plāvanam sadā śiva śakti amṛta sparśe labdham yena kutas mṛtiḥ agnīṣoma padam hitvā na sa bhūyas abhijāyate śiva agninā tanum dagdhvā śakti saumyā mṛtena yaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe bhasmatattvavarṇanam nāma aṣṭāviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca nivedayāmi jagataḥ vāc arthātmyam kṛtam yathā ṣaḍadhva vedanam samyak samāsāt na tu vistarāt nā asti kaścid aśabda arthaḥ nā api śabdaḥ nirarthakaḥ prakṛteḥ pariṇāmaḥ dvidhā śabda artha bhāvanā tām āhuḥ prākṛtīm mūrtim śivayoḥ parama ātmanoḥ śabda ātmikā vibhūtiḥ yā sā tridhā kathyate budhaiḥ sthūlā sūkṣmā parā ca iti sthūlā yā śruti gocarā sūkṣmā cintā mayī proktā ciṃtayā rahitā parā yā śaktiḥ sā parā śaktiḥ śivatattva samāśrayā jñānaśakti samāyogāt icchā upodbalikā tathā sarva śakti samaṣṭi ātmā śakti tattva samākhyayā samasta kārya jātasya mūlaprakṛti tām gatā sā eva kuṇḍalinī māyā śuddha adhva paramā satī sā vibhāga svarūpā eva ṣaḍadhva ātmā vijṛṃbhate tatra śabdāḥ trayaḥ adhvānaḥ trayaḥ ca arthāḥ samīritāḥ sarveṣām api vai puṃsām naija śuddhi anurūpataḥ parasyāḥ prakṛteḥ ādau paṃcadhā pariṇāmataḥ kalāḥ ca tāḥ nivṛttya ādyāḥ paryyāptāḥ iti niścayaḥ mantra adhvā ca pada adhvā ca varṇa adhvā ca iti śabdataḥ bhuvana adhvā ca tattva adhvā kalā adhvā ca arthataḥ kramāt atra anyonyam ca sarveṣām vyāpya vyāpaka tā ucyate mantrāḥ sarvaiḥ padaiḥ vyāptāḥ vākya bhāvāt padāni ca varṇaiḥ varṇa samūham hi padam āhuḥ vipaścitaḥ varṇāḥ tu bhuvanaiḥ vyāptāḥ teṣām teṣu upalaṃbhanāt vyāptāni kāraṇaiḥ tattvaiḥ ārabdha tvāt anekaśas paurāṇikāni ca anyāni vijñeyāni śiva āgame śiva śāstra prasiddhāni tatonyāni api kṛtsnaśas parasyāḥ prakṛteḥ ādau paṃcadhā pariṇāmataḥ kalāḥ ca tāḥ nivṛttya ādyāḥ vyāptāḥ paṃca yathottaram vyāpikā atas parā śaktiḥ avibhaktā ṣaḍadhvanām vyāptam ekena tena eva mṛdā kuṃbha ādikam yathā śaivam tat paramam dhāma yat prāpyam ṣaḍbhiḥ adhvabhiḥ tat ūrdhvam vidyayā madhye yāvat viśveśvara avadhi śāntyā tad ūrdhvam madhu ante viśuddhiḥ śānti atītayā yām āhuḥ paramam vyoma para prakṛti yogataḥ śaktipāta samāyogāt ṛte tattvāni tattvataḥ tad vyāptiḥ tad vivṛddhiḥ ca jñātum evam na śakyate śaktiḥ ājñā parā śaivī cit rūpā marameśvarī śivaḥ adhitiṣṭhati akhilam yayā kāraṇa bhūtayā na ātmanaḥ nā eva māyā eṣā na vikāraḥ vicārataḥ sarva aiśvarya parākāṣṭā śivasya vyabhicāriṇī samāna dharmiṇī tasya taiḥ taiḥ bhāvaiḥ viśeṣataḥ sa tayā eva gṛhī sā api tena eva gṛhiṇī sadā tasmāt śivaḥ paraḥ hetuḥ tasya ājñā parameśvarī tayā eva preritā śaivī mūlaprakṛtiḥ avyayā mahāmāyā ca māyā ca prakṛtiḥ triguṇā iti ca trividhā kārya vedhena sā prasūte ṣaḍadhvanaḥ sa vāc artha mayaḥ ca adhvā ṣaḍvidhaḥ nikhilam jagat asya eva vistaram prāhuḥ śāstra jātam aśeṣatas iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe vāgarthakatattvavarṇanam nāma ekonatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ durvijñeyāni devaiḥ ca mohayanti manāṃsi naḥ yadi evam īdṛśa aiśvaryyam tat tu svātantrya lakṣaṇam mūrta ātmā sakalaḥ kaścid sa ca anyaḥ niṣphalaḥ śivaḥ mūrti ātmā eva tadā mūrtiḥ śivasya asya bhavet iti anyathā nirapekṣeṇa mūrtiḥ svīkriyate katham mūrti svīkaraṇam tasmāt mūrtau sādhya phala īpsayā sva icchā eva tādṛśī puṃsām yasmāt karma anusāriṇī brahma ādayaḥ piśāca antāḥ kim te karma ativartinaḥ icchayā deha nirmāṇam indrajāla upamam viduḥ aṇima ādi guṇa aiśvaryya vaśīkāra anatikramāt viśva rūpam dadhat viṣṇuḥ dadhīcena mahā ṛṣiṇā śarīravat tayā anya ātma sādharmyam pratibhāti naḥ sa nirgṛhṇāti devānām sarva anugrāhakaḥ katham viṣṇoḥ api nṛsiṃhasya rabhasā śarabha ākṛtiḥ deva strīṣu ca deveṣu dakṣasya adhvara kāraṇāt vīreṇa vīrabhadreṇa na hi kaścid a daṇḍitaḥ krośatām eva devānām hutaḥ netra hutāśane sa ruṣā prekṣya devena tad kṣaṇe bhasmasātkṛtaḥ jalaṃdhara asuraḥ dīrṇaḥ cakrīkṛtya jalam padā tam eva jala saṃdhāyī śūlena eva jaghāna saḥ jighāṃsatām surārīṇām kulam nirghṛṇa cetasām triśūlena andhakasya uraḥ śikhinā eva upatāpitam kaṇṭhāt kāla aṃganām sṛṣṭvā dārakaḥ api nipātitaḥ kauśikīm janayitvā tu gauryāḥ tvac kośa gocarām śuṃbhaḥ saha niśuṃbhena prāpitaḥ maraṇam raṇe śrutam ca mahat ākhyānam skānde skanda samāśrayam vihṛtya su ciram devyāḥ rasām rasātalam nītām iva kṛtvā abhidhām tatas devīm ca vaṃcayan tasyām sva vīrya mati durvaham a visṛjya visṛjya agnau haviḥ pūtam iva amṛtam gaṃgā ādiṣu api nikṣipya vahni dvārā tad aṃśataḥ svāhayā kṛttikā rūpāt sva bhartrā ramamāṇayā suvarṇībhūtayā nyastam merau śaravaṇe kvacid saṃdīpayitvā kālena tasya bhāsā diśaḥ daśa rañjayitvā girīn sarvān kāṃcanīkṛtya meruṇā kumāre sukumāra aṅge kumārāṇām nidarśane tat śaiśavam sva rūpam ca tasya dṛṣṭvā manoharam devaḥ svayam āyātaḥ putra darśana lālasaḥ pīta amṛtam iva sneha vivaśena antarātmanā anubhūya ca tad krīḍām saṃbhāvya ca parasparam stanyam ājñāpayan devyāḥ pāyayitvā amṛta upamam tava avatāraḥ jagatām hitāya iti anuśāsya ca tatas śakreṇa saṃdhāya bibhyatā tāraka asurāt kārayitvā abhiṣekam ca senāpatye divaukasām cheditam tārakasya api śiraḥ śakra bhiyā saha stutim cakruḥ viśeṣeṇa hari dhātṛ mukhāḥ surāḥ tathā rakṣodhipaḥ sākṣāt rāvaṇaḥ bala garvitaḥ pada aṃguṣṭha parispandāt mamajja mṛditaḥ bhuvi baṭoḥ kenacid arthena sva āśritasya gata āyuṣaḥ sva vāhanam a vijñāya vṛṣa indram vaḍavānalaḥ śāntaḥ eva sadā sarvam anugṛhṇāti ced śivaḥ kāraṇam khalu karma api bhavet īśvara kāritam iti śrī śivamahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe śivatattvapraśnaḥ nāma triṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca jijñāsā hi na nāstikyam sādhayet sādhu buddhiṣu param sarvam anugrāhyam paśu pāśa ātmakam jagat parasya anugraha artham tu patyuḥ ājñā samanvayaḥ patiḥ ājñāpakaḥ sarvam anugṛhṇāti sarvvadā atas svātantrya śabdārtha an anapekṣa tva lakṣaṇaḥ mūrta ātmanaḥ śiva ajñāna nivartanāt ajñāna dhiṣṭhitam śambhoḥ na kiṃcid iha vidyate sa mūrti ātmā śivaḥ śaiva mūrtiḥ iti upacaryate na hi asau niṣkalaḥ sākṣāt śivaḥ parama kāraṇam sa ākāreṇa anubhāvena kena api anupalakṣitaḥ pramāṇa gamya tā mātram tad svabhāva upapādakam na tāvatā atra upekṣā dhīḥ upalakṣaṇam antarā ātma upama ulvaṇam sākṣāt mūrtiḥ eva hi kācana śivasya mūrtiḥ mūrti ātmā paraḥ tasya upalakṣaṇam yathā kāṣṭheṣu an ārūḍhaḥ na vahniḥ upalabhyate evam śivaḥ api mūrti ātmani anārūḍhaḥ iti sthitiḥ yathā agnim ānaya iti ukte jvalat kāṣṭhāt ṛte svayam na agniḥ ānīyate tadvat pūjyaḥ mūrti ātmanā śivaḥ mūrti ātmani kṛtam sākṣāt śive eva kṛtam yatas liṃga ādau api tat kṛtyam arcāyām ca viśeṣataḥ yathā anugṛhyate saḥ api mūrti ātmā pārameṣṭhinā tathā mūrti ātma niṣṭhena śivena paśavaḥ vayam loka anugrahaṇāya eva śivena parameṣṭhinā sadāśiva ādayaḥ sarve mūrti ātmanaḥ adhiṣṭhitāḥ ātmanām eva bhogāya mokṣāya ca viśeṣataḥ tattva atattva svarūpeṣu mūrti ātmasu śiva anvayaḥ bhogaḥ karma vipāka ātmā sukha duḥkha ātmakaḥ mataḥ sarvam śivaḥ anugṛhṇāti na nigṛhṇāti kiṃcana nigṛhṇatām tu ye doṣāḥ śive teṣām asaṃbhavāt ye punar nigrahāḥ kecid brahma ādiṣu nidarśitāḥ te api loka hitāya eva kṛtāḥ śrīkaṇṭha mūrtinā brahmāṇḍasya ādhipatyam hi śrīkaṇṭhasya na saṃśayaḥ śrīkaṇṭha ākhyām śivaḥ mūrtim krīḍatīm adhitiṣṭhati sa doṣāḥ eva deva ādyāḥ nigṛhītāḥ yathā uditam tatas te pi vipāpmānaḥ prajāḥ ca api gata jvarāḥ nigrahaḥ sva rūpeṇa viduṣām na jugupsitaḥ atas eva hi daṇḍyeṣu daṇḍaḥ rājñām praśasyate na sa ced īśatām kuryāt jagataḥ katham īśvaraḥ īśa icchā ca vidhātṛ tvam vidheḥ ājñāpanam param ājñā vaśyam idam kuryāt na kuryāt iti śāsanam tad śāsana anuvarti tvam sādhu bhāvasya lakṣaṇam viparīta samādhoḥ syāt na sarvam tat tu dṛśyate sādhu saṃrakṣaṇīyam ced vinivartyam asādhu yat hita artha lakṣaṇam ca idam daṇḍa antam anuśāsanam hite sadā niṣaṇṇānām īśvarasya nidarśanam ayukta kāriṇaḥ loke garhaṇīyā aviveki tā na hi dveṣṭi pitā putram yaḥ nigṛhya ati śikṣayet mādhyasthena api nigrāhya anyaḥ nigṛhṇāti mārgataḥ tasmāt duḥkha ātmikām hiṃsām kurvāṇaḥ yaḥ sa nirghṛṇaḥ nidāna jñasya bhiṣajaḥ rugṇaḥ hiṃsām prayuṃjataḥ na kiṃcid api nairghṛṇyam ghṛṇā eva atra prayojikā ghṛṇā api na guṇāya eva hiṃsreṣu pratiyogiṣu tādṛśeṣu ghṛṇī bhrāntyā ghṛṇā antarita nirghṛṇaḥ upekṣā api iha doṣā āha rakṣyeṣu pratiyogiṣu doṣa ābhāsān samutprekṣya phalataḥ saḥ api nirghṛṇaḥ tasmāt ghṛṇā guṇāya eva sarvathā iti na saṃmatam mūrti ātmasu api rāga ādyāḥ doṣāḥ santi eva vastutas agnau api samāviṣṭam tāmram khalu sa kālikam iti na agniḥ asau duṣyet tāmra saṃsarga kāraṇāt na agneḥ aśuci saṃsargāt aśuci tvam apekṣate evam śodhya ātma saṃsargāt na hi aśuddhaḥ śivaḥ bhavet ayasi agnau samāviṣṭe dāhaḥ agneḥ eva na ayasaḥ mūrtta ātmani evam aiśvaryyam īśvarasya eva na ātmanām kāṣṭhasya aṃgāra tā nā agneḥ evam atra api yojyatām atas eva jagati asmin kāṣṭha pāṣāṇa mṛtsu api śiva āveśa vaśāt eva śiva tvam upacaryate maitrī ādayaḥ guṇāḥ gauṇāḥ tasmāt te bhinna vṛttayaḥ taiḥ guṇaiḥ uparaktānām doṣāya ca guṇāya ca yat tu gauṇam agauṇam ca tat sarvam anugṛhṇataḥ na guṇāya na doṣāya śivasya guṇa vṛttayaḥ na ca anugraha śabda artham gauṇam āhuḥ vipaścitaḥ saṃsāra mocanam kim tu śaivam ājñā mayam hitam hitam tat ājñā karaṇam yat hitam tat anugrahaḥ sarvam hite niyuñjāvaḥ sarva anugraha kārakaḥ tasya api hita rūpa tvāt śivaḥ sarva upakārakaḥ yathā vikāsayati eva raviḥ padmāni bhānubhiḥ svabhāvaḥ api hi bhāvānām bhāvinaḥ arthasya kāraṇam suvarṇam eva nā aṃgāram drāvayati agni saṃgamaḥ svabhāva vimalaḥ yadvat sarva anugrāhakaḥ śivaḥ svabhāva malināḥ tadvat ātmanaḥ jīva saṃjñitāḥ āgantuka tve kasya api bhāvyam kena api hetunā ātmatāyāḥ samatve api baddhāḥ muktāḥ pare yatas jñāna aiśvarya ādi vaiṣamyam bhajante sa uttara adharāḥ kecid mūrti ātma tām yānti kecid āsanna gocarāḥ mūrti ātmasu śivāḥ kecid adhvanām mūrddhasu sthitāḥ madhye maheśvarāḥ rudrāḥ tu arvācīna pade sthitāḥ āsanne ca māyāyāḥ parasmāt kāraṇāt trayam tatra api ātmā sthitaḥ adhastāt antarātmā ca madhyatas vartante vasavaḥ kecid paramātma pada āśrayāḥ śānti atīta pade śaivāḥ śānte māheśvare tatas vidyāyāntu yathā raudrāḥ pratiṣṭhāyām tu vaiṣṇavāḥ nivṛttau ca tathā ātmānaḥ brahmā brahma aṃga yonayaḥ pakṣi ādayaḥ paṃcayonayaḥ tāḥ caturdaśa uttara adhara bhāvaḥ jñeyaḥ saṃsāriṇaḥ malaḥ yathā āma bhāvaḥ muktasya pūrvam paścāt tu pakva tā malaḥ api āmaḥ ca pakvaḥ ca bhavet saṃsāra kāraṇam āme tu adhara tā puṃsām pakve tu uttara tā kramāt paśu ātmānaḥ tridhā bhinnāḥ eka dvi tri malāḥ kramāt atra uttarāḥ eka malāḥ dvi malāḥ madhyamāḥ matāḥ tri malāḥ tu adhamāḥ jñeyāḥ yathottaram adhiṣṭhitāḥ aśiva ātmakam api etat śivena adhiṣṭhitam yathā aṇḍa antā hi mahā bhūmiḥ śata rudra ādi adhiṣṭhitā māyā antam antarikṣam tu hi amara īśa ādibhiḥ kramāt mahāmāyā avasānā dyauḥ vāyu ādyaiḥ bhuvana adhipaiḥ anāśrita antaiḥ adhva antarvarttibhiḥ samadhiṣṭhitāḥ te hi sākṣāt diviṣadaḥ tu antarikṣa sadaḥ tathā pṛthivī padaḥ iti evam devāḥ deva vrataiḥ stutā nidāna bhūtaiḥ saṃsāra rogaḥ puṃsām pravartate bhiṣaj ājñāpakaḥ śambhuḥ śivaḥ parama kāraṇam aduḥkhena a api śaktaḥ asau paśūn mocayitum śivaḥ katham duḥkham aduḥkham syāt svabhāvaḥ hi aviparyayaḥ na hi rogī hi arogī syāt bhiṣaj bhaiṣajya kāraṇāt evam svabhāva malinān svabhāvāt duḥkhinaḥ paśūn sva ajña auṣadha vidhānena duḥkhāt mocayate śivaḥ na bhiṣaj kāraṇam roge śivaḥ saṃsāra kāraṇam duḥkhe svabhāva saṃsiddhe katham tad kāraṇam śivaḥ svābhāvikaḥ malaḥ puṃsām sa hi saṃsārayati amūn saṃsāra kāraṇam yat tu malam māyā ādi acetanam yathā maṇiḥ ayaskāntaḥ sānnidhyāt upakārakaḥ na nivartayitum śakyam sānnidhyam sat akāraṇam adhiṣṭhātā tatas nityam ajñātaḥ jagataḥ śivaḥ śaktiḥ rājñā ātmikā tasya niyantrī viśvatomukhī tayā tatam idam śaśvat tathā api sa na duṣyati an idam prathamam sarvam īśitavyam saḥ īśvaraḥ īśanāt ca tadīya ājñā tathā api sa na duṣyati tatas hṛṣṭatarāḥ sarvve vinaṣṭa aśeṣa saṃśayāḥ munayaḥ vismaya āviṣṭāḥ preṇemuḥ pavanam prabhum na ete pratiṣṭhita jñānāḥ iti matvā evam abravīt vāyuḥ uvācva parokṣam aparokṣam ca dvividham jñānam iṣyate hetu upadeśa gamyam yat tat parokṣam pracakṣate aparokṣam punar śreṣṭhāt anuṣṭhānāt bhaviṣyati na aparokṣāt ṛte mokṣaḥ iti kṛtvā viniścayam śreṣṭha anuṣṭhāna siddhi artham prayatadhvam atandritāḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe jñānopadeśaḥ nāma ekatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ kim tat śreṣṭam anuṣṭhānam mokṣaḥ yena parokṣitaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca śaivaḥ hi paramaḥ dharmaḥ śreṣṭha anuṣṭhāna śabditaḥ yatra aparokṣaḥ lakṣyeta sākṣāt mokṣa pradaḥ śivaḥ sa tu paṃcavidhaḥ jñeyaḥ paṃcabhiḥ parvabhiḥ kramāt parokṣam aparokṣam ca jñānam yatra ca mokṣa dam dharma śabda abhidheya īrthe pramāṇam śrutiḥ eva naḥ a paśu ātma adhikāra tvāt yaḥ dharamaḥ paramaḥ mataḥ sādhāraṇaḥ tatas anyaḥ tu sarveṣām adhikārataḥ dharma śāstra ādibhiḥ samyak sa aṃgaḥ eva upabṛṃhitaḥ śaivaḥ yaḥ paramaḥ dharmaḥ śreṣṭha anuṣṭhāna śabditaḥ śaiva āgamaiḥ tu saṃpannaḥ saha aṃgopām vistaraḥ tad saṃskāra adhikāraiḥ ca samyak eva upabṛṃhitaḥ śaiva āgamaḥ hi dvividhaḥ śrautaḥ saṃskṛtaḥ kāmikā ādi samākhyābhiḥ siddhaḥ siddhānta saṃjñitaḥ param pāśupatam yatra vratam jñānam ca kathyate yuga āvarteṣu śiṣyeta yoga ācāryya svarūpiṇā saṃkṣipya asya pravaktāraḥ catvāraḥ parama ṛṣaya ruruḥ dadhīcaḥ agastyaḥ ca upamanyuḥ mahā yaśāḥ te ca pāśupatāḥ jñeyāḥ saṃhitānām pravartakāḥ teṣu pāśupataḥ yogaḥ śivam pratyakṣayet dṛḍham tasmāt śreṣṭham anuṣṭhānam yogaḥ pāśupataḥ mataḥ nāma aṣṭaka mayaḥ yogaḥ śivena parikalpitaḥ tena yogena sahasā śaivī prajñā prajāyate prasīdati śivaḥ tasya yasya jñānam pratiṣṭhitam prasādāt paramaḥ yogaḥ yaḥ śivam ca aparokṣayet śiva aparokṣāt saṃsāra kāraṇena viyujyate saṃsāra vaidyaḥ sarvajñaḥ paramātmā iti mukhyatas nāma aṣṭakam idam mukhyam śivasya pratipādakam ādi antu pañcakam jñeyam śānti atīta ādi anukramāt saṃjñā sadāśiva ādīnām paṃca upādhi parigrahāt padam eva hi tat nityam anityāḥ padinaḥ smṛtāḥ ātma antara abhidhānam syāt yat ādyam nāma pañcakam anyat tu tritayam nāmnām upādāna ādi yogataḥ anādi mala saṃśleṣaḥ prāgabhāvāt svabhāvataḥ yam veda ādau svaram prāhuḥ vācya vācaka bhāvataḥ veda eka vedya yāthātmyāt vedānte ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ tad adhīna pravṛtti tvāt prakṛteḥ puruṣasya ca māyām tu prakṛtim vidyāt māyinam tu maheśvaram kāla ātmā paramātma ādiḥ sthūlaḥ sūkṣmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ rud duḥkham duḥkha hetuḥ vā tat rāvayati naḥ prabhuḥ tattva ādi bhūta paryantam śarīra ādiṣu atandritaḥ vyāpya adhitiṣṭhati śivaḥ tatas rudraḥ itas tatas jagataḥ pitṛ bhūtānām śivaḥ mūrti ātmanām api pitṛ bhāvena sarveṣām pitāmahaḥ udīritaḥ saṃsārasya īśvaraḥ nityam sa mūlasya nivartakaḥ tri kāla bhāvinaḥ bhāvān sthūlān sūkṣmān aśeṣatas aṇavaḥ nā eva jānanti māyayā eva mala āvṛtāḥ svasmāt parātma virahāt paramātmā śivaḥ svayam nāma aṣṭakam idam ca eva labdhvā ācārya prasādataḥ nivṛttya ādi kalā granthim śiva ādyaiḥ paṃca nāmabhiḥ yathāsvam kramaśas chitvā śodhayitvā yathāguṇam chittvā paryaṣṭaka ākāram sva ātmānam ca suṣumṇayā saṃhṛtyam vadanam paścāt yathā saṃskaraṇam layāt śāktena amṛta varṣeṇa saṃsiktāyām tanau punar avatārya svam ātmānam amṛta ātma ākṛtim hṛdi dvādaśāntar sthitasya indoḥ parastāt śveta paṃkaje śiva nāma aṣṭakena eva bhāva puṣpaiḥ samarcayet abhyarccana ante tu punar prāṇān āyamya mānavaḥ samyak cittam samādhāya śārvam nāma aṣṭakam japet nābhau ca aṣṭa āhutīḥ hutvā pūrṇāhutyā namaḥ tatas evam kṛtvā cirāt eva jñānam pāśupatam śubham labhate tad pratiṣṭhām ca vṛttam ca anuttamam tathā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe śreṣṭhānuṣṭhānavarṇanam nāma dvātriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ vāyuḥ uvāca vratam pāśupatam śrautam atharvaśirasi śrutam eka ambaraḥ valkalī vā bhavet daṇḍī ca mekhalī prakṣālya caraṇau paścāt dvis ācamya ātmanaḥ tanum padmam tasya api abhāve tu kevalam bhāvanā mayam tad padma karṇikā madhye kṛtvā liṃgam kanīyasam sphīṭikam pīṭhikā upetam pūjayet vidhivat kramāt parikalpya āsanam mūrtim paṃcavaktra prakārataḥ paṃcagavya ādibhiḥ pūrṇaiḥ yathā vibhava saṃbhṛtaiḥ snāpayet kalaśaiḥ pūrṇaiḥ aṣṭāpada samudbhavaiḥ sa vedikam samālipya liṃgam bhūṣaṇa bhūṣitam puṇyaiḥ praśastaiḥ patraiḥ ca citraiḥ dūrvā akṣata ādibhiḥ samabhyarcya yathālābham mahā pūjā vidhānataḥ iṣṭāni ca viśiṣṭāni nyāyena upārjitāni ca sarva dravyāṇi deyāni vrate tasmin viśeṣataḥ śrīpatra utpala padmānām saṃkhyā sāhasrikī matā pratyekam aparā saṃkhyā śatam aṣṭa uttaram dvijāḥ aṣṭāGgam arghyam utkṛṣṭam dhūpa ālepau viśeṣataḥ candanam vāmadeva ākhye haritālam ca pauruṣe īśāne bhasitam kecid ālepanam iti īdṛśām sita agurum aghora ākhye mukhe kṛṣṇa agurum punar īśāne api hi uśīra ādi deyāt dhūpam viśeṣataḥ śarkarā madhu karpūra kapilā ghṛta saṃyutam candana aguru kāṣṭha ādyam sāmānyam saṃpracakṣate karpūra varti rājya āḍhyā deyā dīpa āvaliḥ tatas brahma aṃgāni tatas ca eva prathama āvaraṇa īrcite dvitīya āvaraṇe pūjyāḥ vighna īśāḥ cakravartinaḥ mahādeva ādayaḥ tatra tathā ekādaśa mūrtayaḥ daśa dikpatayaḥ pūjyāḥ sa astrāḥ sa anucarāḥ tathā sarvāḥ devyaḥ ca devāḥ ca sarve sarve ca khecarāḥ pātāla vāsinaḥ ca anye sarve muni gaṇāḥ api kṣetrapālāḥ ca sa gaṇāḥ sarvam ca etat carācaram pūjanīyam śiva prītyā mat tvā śaṃbhu vibhūtimat atha āvaraṇa pūjā ante saṃpūjya parameśvaram sa ājyam savyam janam hṛdyam haviḥ bhaktyā nivedayet mukha vāsa ādikam dattvā tāmbūlam sa upadaṃśakam alaṃkṛtya ca bhūyas api nānā puṣpa vibhūṣaṇaiḥ nīrājana ante vistīrya pūjā śeṣam samāpayet caṣakam sa upakāram ca śayanam ca samarpayet candra saṃkāśa hāram ca śayanīyam samarpayet ādyam nṛpa ucitam hṛdyam tat sarvam anurūpataḥ kṛtvā ca kārayitvā ca hitvā ca pratipūjanam stotram vyapohanam japtvā vidyām paṃcākṣarīm japet pradakṣiṇām praṇāmam ca kṛtvā ātmānam samarpayet dattvā arghyam aṣṭau puṣpāṇi devam udvāsya liṃgataḥ saṃpūjya ca gurūn viprān vratinaḥ ca viśeṣataḥ bhaktān dvijān ca śaktaḥ ced dīna anāthān ca toṣayet svayam ca anaśane śaktaḥ phala mūla aśane atha vā payaḥ vrataḥ vā bhikṣā āśī bhavet eka aśanaḥ tathā naktam yukta aśanaḥ nityam bhū śayyā nirataḥ śuciḥ bhasma śāyī tṛṇeśāyī cīra ajina dhṛtaḥ athavā arkavāre tathā ārdrāyām paṃcadaśyām ca pakṣayoḥ pākhaṇḍi patita udakyāḥ sūtakāntya ja pūrvakān kṣama dāna dayā satya ahiṃsā śīlaḥ sadā bhavet santuṣṭaḥ ca praśāntaḥ ca japa dhyāna rataḥ tathā kuryāt triṣavaṇa snānam bhasma snānam atha api vā pūjām vaiśeṣikīm ca eva manasā vacasā girā pramādāt tu tathā ācāre nirūpya gurulāghave ucitām niṣkṛtim kuryāt pūjā homa japa ādibhiḥ godānam ca vṛṣotsargam kuryāt pūjām ca saṃpadā pratimāsam viśeṣam ca pravadāmi yathāśrutam āśvine māsi vidyāt vai liṃgam gomedakam varam kārtikyām vaidrumam liṃgam vaidūryam mārgaśīrṣake puṣparāga mayam pauṣe māghe dyumaṇi jan tathā sarva māseṣu ratnānām alābhe haimam eva vā haima abhāve rājatam vā tāmra jam śailajam tathā mṛd mayam vā yathālābham jātuṣam ca anyat eva vā kṛtvā vaiśeṣikīm pūjām hutvā ca eva yathā purā saṃpūjya ca tathā ācāryam vratinaḥ ca viśeṣataḥ deśikena api anujñātaḥ prāc mukhaḥ vā api uda mukhaḥ darbha āsanaḥ darbha pāṇiḥ prāṇa apānau niyamya ca japitvā śaktitaḥ mūlam dhyātvā sa ambam triyambakam tatas daṇḍa jaṭā cīra mekhalāḥ api ca utsṛjet yaḥ kṛtvā ātyantikīm dīkṣām ā dehāntam an ākulaḥ saḥ atyāśramī ca vijñeyaḥ mahāpāśupataḥ tathā saḥ eva tapatām śreṣṭha saḥ eva ca mahāvratī na tena sadṛśaḥ kaścid kṛtakṛtyaḥ mumukṣuṣu ghṛta aktaḥ yaḥ caret etat vratam vrata parāyaṇaḥ śiva arpita ātmā satatam na tena sadṛśaḥ kvacid tasmāt sarveṣu kāleṣu vīryavān bhasma saṃyutaḥ bhūti bhūti karī ca eva rakṣā rakṣā karī param parama astram ca śaivānām bhasma etat pārameśvaram iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe paśupativratavidhānavarṇanam nāma trayastriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ dhaumya agrajena śuśunā kṣīra artham hi tapaḥ kṛtam tasmāt kṣīrārṇavaḥ dattaḥ tasmai devena śūlinā sa katham śiśukaḥ lebhe śiva śāstra pravaktṛ tām katham vā śiva sadbhāvam jñātvā tapasi niṣṭhitaḥ katham ca labdha vijñānaḥ tapaḥ caraṇa parvaṇi vāyuḥ uvāca na hi eṣa śiśukaḥ kaścid prākṛtaḥ kṛtavān tapaḥ janma antareṇa saṃsiddhaḥ kena api khalu hetunā sva pada pracyutaḥ diṣṭyā prāptaḥ muni kumāra tām mahādeva prasādasya bhāgya āpannasya bhāvinaḥ dugdha abhilāṣa prabhava dvāra tām agamat tapaḥ atas sarva gaṇa īśa tvam kumāra tvam ca śāśvatam tasya jñāna āgama upyasya prasādāt eva śāṃkarāt kaumāram hi param sākṣāt jñānam śakti mayam viduḥ dṛṣṭam tu kāraṇam tasya śiva jñāna samanvaye sva mātṛ vacanam sākṣāt śoka jam kṣīra kāraṇāt upamanyuḥ vyāghrapādiḥ prītyā provāca mātaram vāyuḥ uvāca vyāghrapādasya mahiṣī duḥkham āpat tadā ca sā tat haṭham sā parijñāya dvija patnī tapasvinī śāntaye tad haṭhasya atha śubha upāyam arīracat kṛtsna abhāvena dāridryāt mayā te bhāgya hīnayā mithyādugdham idam dattam piṣṭam āloḍya vāriṇā tvam mātula gṛhe su alpam pītvā svādu payaḥ śṛtam jñātvā svādu tvayā pītam tad jātīyam anusmaran dattam na payaḥ iti uktvā rudan duḥkhīkaroṣi mām sa kāmānām yathākāmam yathā ukta phala dāyakaḥ dhanāni uddiśya na asmābhiḥ itas prāk arcitaḥ śivaḥ atas daridrāḥ saṃjātāḥ vayam tasmāt na te payaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca iti mātṛ vacaḥ śrutvā tathyam śoka ādi sūcakam śokena alam itaḥ mataḥ sa aṃbaḥ yadi asti śaṃkaraḥ tyaja śokam mahābhāge sarvam bhadram bhaviṣyati vāyuḥ uvāca praytuvāca tadā mātā su prasannā manasvinī mātā uvāca śubham vicāritam tāta tvayā mad prīti varddhanam vilaṃbam mā kathāḥ tvam hi bhaja sa aṃbam sadāśivam tat kṛtam hi jagat sarvam brahma ādyāḥ tasya kiṃkarāḥ tad prasāda kṛta aiśvaryāḥ dāsāḥ tasya vayam prabhoḥ tam eva sa aṃbam sa gaṇam bhaja bhāva purassaram sākṣāt namaḥ śivāya iti mantraḥ ayam vācakaḥ smṛtaḥ sapta koṭi mahā mantrāḥ sarve sa praṇavāḥ pare sa prasādāḥ ca te mantrāḥ sva adhikāra ādi apekṣayā sarva adhikāraḥ tu ekaḥ ayam mantraḥ eva īśvara ājñayā yathā nikṛṣṭān utkṛṣṭān sarvān api ātmanaḥ śivaḥ sarva rakṣā kṣamaḥ api eṣa na aparaḥ kaścid iṣyate tasmāt mantra antarān tyaktvā paṃcākṣara paraḥ bhava adhorāstram ca śaivānām rakṣā hetuḥ anuttamam mantram ca te mayā dattam gṛhāṇa mad anujñayā vāyuḥ uvāca evam mātrā samādiśya śivam astu iti udīrya ca tam āha ca tadā mātā śubham kurvantu te surāḥ aṣṭa iṣṭakābhiḥ prasādam kṛtvā liṃgam ca mṛd mayam bhaktyā pañcākṣareṇa eva putraiḥ puṣpaiḥ vana udbhavaiḥ samabhyarcya ciram kālam cacāra paramam tapaḥ tatas tapaḥ carat tam tam bālam ekākinam kṛśam purā marīcinā śaptāḥ kecid muni piśācakāḥ saṃpīḍya rākṣasaiḥ bhāvaiḥ tapasaḥ vighnam ācaran sadā namaḥ śivāya iti krośati smārta nāda vat tad nāda śravaṇāt eva tapasaḥ vighna kāriṇaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe upamanyutapovarṇanam nāma catustriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca atha sarve pradīpta aṃgāḥ vaikuṇṭham prayayuḥ drutam praṇamya āhuḥ ca tat sarvam haraye deva sattamāḥ viṣṇuḥ uvāca vāyuḥ uvāca iti śrutvā vacaḥ viṣṇoḥ prāha devaḥ maheśvaraḥ śiśum nivārayiṣyāmi tat tvam gaccha svam āśramam tat śrutvā śaṃbhu vacanam sa viṣṇuḥ deva vallabhaḥ jagāma āśvāsya tān sarvān sva lokam amara ādikān sa vāraṇaḥ cāru tadā vibhum tam nivījya vāla vyajanena divyam vāyuḥ uvāca śakraḥ uvāca vāyuḥ uvāca tat niśamya hariḥ prāha mām na jānāsi lekhapam rudreṇa nirguṇena api kim te kāryam bhaviṣyati deva paGkti bahis bhūtaḥ yaḥ piśāca tvam āgataḥ vāyuḥ uvāca upāsate yam tattva jñāḥ varam tasmāt vṛṇomi aham na asti śaṃbhoḥ param tattvam sarva kāraṇa kāraṇāt brahma viṣṇu ādi devānām sraṣṭuḥ guṇa parāt vibhoḥ nihatya tvām śiva astreṇa tyajāmi etam kalevaram vāyuḥ uvāca kṣīre vāñchām api tyaktvā nihantum śakram udyataḥ smṛtvā śaṃbhu pada dvandvam sva deham dugdhum udyataḥ āgneyīm dhāraṇām bibhrat upamanyuḥ avasthitaḥ tat visṛṣṭam aghora astram nandīśvara niyogataḥ jagṛhe madhyatas kṣiptam nandī śaṃkara vallabhaḥ apūpānām girim ca eva darśayāmāsa sa prabhuḥ evam sa dadṛśe devaḥ devyā sārddham vṛṣā upari gaṇeśvaraiḥ triśūla ādyaiḥ divya astraiḥ api saṃvṛtaḥ ehi ehi iti tam āhūya mūrdhni āghrāya dadau varān śivaḥ uvāca ete dattāḥ mayā te hi tvam gṛhṇīṣva mahā mune varān varaya su prītyā mano abhilaṣitān parān vāyuḥ uvāca kṣīrābdhiḥ api sa ākāraḥ kṣīram svādu kare dadhat upasthāya dadau piṇḍībhūtam kṣīram anaśvaram samṛddhim paramān tasmai dadau santuṣṭa mānasaḥ atha śaṃbhuḥ prasanna ātmā dṛṣṭvā tasya tapaḥ mahaḥ tasmāt śivāt ca tasyāḥ ca śivāyāḥ muditaḥ tatas prasanna cetaskaḥ su praṇamya kṛtāṃjaliḥ sva dāsyam paramam sneham sānnidhyam ca eva sarvadā prasīda karuṇā sindho sa amba śaṃkara sarvadā vāyuḥ uvāca evam uktaḥ mahādevaḥ sarveṣām ca vara pradaḥ śivaḥ uvāca akṣayāḥ bāndhavāḥ ca eva kulam gotram ca te sadā bhaviṣyati dvijaśreṣṭha mayi bhaktiḥ ca śāśvatī upa kaṃṭham mama tvam vai sa ānandam vihariṣyasi upamanyuḥ prasanna ātmā prāpya tasmāt varāt varān jagāma jananī sthānam sukham prāpa adhikam ca saḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe vaiyāsikyām caturviṃśati sāhasryām saṃhitāyām tad antargatāyām saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām pūrva khaṇḍe upamanyucaritavarṇanam nāma pañcatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ u ayam saptamyāḥ vāyavīyasaṃhitāyāḥ pūrva khaṇḍaḥ śrīḥ śrī gaṇeśāya namaḥ śrī gaurī śaṃkarābhyām namaḥ atha saptamyām vāyavīya saṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍaḥ prārabhyate gaurī kuca taṭa dvandva kuṃkuma aṃkita vakṣase sūtaḥ uvāca atha ayam arthaḥ praṣṭavyaḥ iti kṛtvā viniścayam kṛtvā yathā svakam kṛtyam pratyaham te yathā purā bhagavantam upāyāntam samīkṣya samupāviśan sukha āsana upaviṣṭaḥ ca vāyuḥ loka namaskṛtaḥ śrīmat vibhūtim īśasya hṛdi kṛtvā idam abravīt iti ākarṇya śubhām vāṇīm ṛṣayaḥ kṣīṇa kalmaṣāḥ vibhūti vistaram śrotum ūcuḥ te paramam vacaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ kṣīra arthena api tapasā yat prāptam parameśvarāt prāptam ca paramam jñānam iti prāk eva śuśruma katham sa labdhavān kṛṣṇaḥ jñānam pāśupatam param vāyuḥ uvāca tad śiṣya bhūtaiḥ munibhiḥ śāstraiḥ vedam iva āvṛtam śiva dhyāna ratam śāntam upamanyum mahā dyutim namaścakāra tam dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭa sarva tanūruhaḥ stutim cakāra su prītyā nata skandhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ tapaḥ kṣīṇam alam kṛṣṇam upamanyuḥ yathā vidhiḥ tatas guru niyogāt vai kṛṣṇaḥ parama śaktimān tapaḥ cakāra putra artham sa aṃbam uddiśya śaṃkaram vara artham āvirbhūtasya harasya subhaga ākṛteḥ stutim cakāra natvā asau kṛṣṇaḥ samyak kṛtāṃjaliḥ tapasā tuṣṭa cittena dattam viṣṇoḥ śivena vai yasmāt sa aṃbaḥ mahādevaḥ pradadau putram ātmanaḥ tasmāt jāṃbavatī sūnum sāṃbam cakre sa nāmataḥ mahā ṛṣeḥ jñāna lābhaḥ ca putra lābhaḥ ca śaṃkarāt yaḥ idam kīrtayet nityam śṛṇuyāt śrāvayet tathā sa viṣṇoḥ jñānam āsādya tena eva saha modate iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe kṛṣṇaputraprāptivarṇanam prathamaḥ adhyāyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ kim tat pāśupatam jñānam katham paśupatiḥ śivaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca iti ākarṇya vacaḥ teṣām mahā ṛṣīṇām prabhaṃjanaḥ vāyuḥ uvāca purā sākṣāt maheśena śrīkaṃṭha ākhyena mandare devyai devena kathitam jñānam pāśupatam param tat eva pṛṣṭam kṛṣṇena viṣṇunā viśvayoninā paśu tvam ca sura ādīnām pati tvam ca śivasya ca yathā upadiṣṭam kṛṣṇāya muninā hi upamanyunā purā upamanyum āsīnam viṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇa vapuḥ dharaḥ śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca divyam pāśupatam jñānam vibhūtim vā asya kṛtsnaśas iti saṃcoditaḥ śrīmān upamanyuḥ mahātmanā praṇamya devam devīm ca prāha puṣṭaḥ yathā tathā mala māyā ādibhiḥ pāśaiḥ sa badhnāti paśūn patiḥ viṣayāḥ iti kathyante pāśāḥ jīva nibandhanāḥ pāśaiḥ etaiḥ patiḥ devaḥ kāryam kārayati svakam tasya ājñayā maheśasya prakṛtiḥ puruṣa ucitām buddhim prasūte sā buddhiḥ ahaṃkāram ahaṃkṛtiḥ tanmātrāṇi api tasya eva śāsanena mahīyasā mahābhūtāni aśeṣāṇi bhāvayanti anupūrvaśas śrotra ādīni ca gṛhṇanti śabda ādīn viṣayān pṛthak svān eva na anyān devasya divyena ājñā balena vai vāc ādīni api yāni āsan tāni karmendriyāṇi ca yathā svam karma kurvanti na anyat kiṃcid śiva ājñayā ākāśaḥ parameśasya śāsanāt eva sarva gaḥ pāka ādyam ca karoti agniḥ parameśvara śāsanāt saṃjīvana ādyam sarvasya kurvati apaḥ tad ājñayā viśvambharāḥ jagat nityam dhatte viśveśvara ājñayā ādhipatyam apām nityam kurute varuṇaḥ sadā pāśaiḥ badhnāti ca yathā daṃḍyān tasya eva śāsanāt dadāti nityam yakṣa indraḥ draviṇam draviṇa īśvaraḥ puṇya anurūpam bhūtebhyaḥ puruṣasya anuśāsanāt karoti saṃpadaḥ śaśvat jñānam ca api sumedhasām nigraham ca api asādhūnām īśānaḥ śiva śāsanāt dhatte tu dharaṇīm mūrdhnā śeṣaḥ śiva niyogataḥ yām āhuḥ tāmasīm raudrīm hareḥ sṛjati aśeṣam īśasya śāsanāt caturānanaḥ anyābhiḥ mūrtibhiḥ svābhiḥ pāti ca ante nihanti ca viṣṇuḥ pālayate viśvam kāla kālasya śāsanāt kālaḥ pālayate viśvam kāla kālasya śāsanāt tribhiḥ aṃśaiḥ jagat bibhrat tejobhiḥ vṛṣṭim ādiśan puṣṇāti oṣadhi jātāni bhūtāni āhlādayati api devaiḥ ca pīyate candraḥ candrabhūṣaṇa śāsanāt khecarāḥ ṛṣayaḥ siddhāḥ bhoginaḥ manujāḥ mṛgāḥ nadyaḥ samudrāḥ girayaḥ kānanāni sarāṃsi ca vedāḥ sa aṃgāḥ ca śāstrāṇi mantra stoma makha ādayaḥ kālāgni ādi śiva antāni bhuvanāni saha adhipaiḥ brahmāṃḍāni api asaṃkhyāni teṣām āvaraṇāni ca vartamānāni atītāni bhaviṣyanti api kṛtsnaśas diśaḥ ca vidiśaḥ ca eva kāla bhedāḥ kalā ādayaḥ tat sarvam śaṃkarasya ājñā balena samadhiṣṭhitam jyotiḥ gaṇāḥ śakra mukhāḥ ca devāḥ sthiram ciram vā cit acit yat asti ati āścaryam idam kṛṣṇa śaṃbhoḥ amita karmaṇaḥ ājñā kṛtam śṛṇuṣva etat śrutam śruti mukhe mayā tadā maheśvaraḥ teṣām madhyatas vara veṣa dhṛk sva lakṣaṇaiḥ vihīna aṃgaḥ svayam yakṣaḥ iva abhavat sa tān āha surān ekam tṛṇam ādāya bhū tale kiṃcid kruddhaḥ vihasya enam tṛṇam ādātum udyataḥ na tat tṛṇam upadātum manasā api ca śakyate tat vajram nija vajreṇa saṃsṛṣṭam iva sarvatas tṛṇena abhihatam tena tiryak agram papāta ha tatas ca anye su saṃrabdhāḥ lokapālāḥ mahā balāḥ sasṛjuḥ tṛṇam uddiśya sva āyudhāni sahasraśas evam devaiḥ samārabdham tṛṇam uddiśya yatnataḥ vyartham āsīt aho kṛṣṇa yakṣasya ātma balena vai tat āha yakṣam deva indraḥ kaḥ bhavān iti amarṣitaḥ tad antare haimavatī devī divya vibhūṣaṇā āvirāsīt nabhoḥ aṃge śobhamānā śuci smitā praṇamya yakṣam papracchuḥ kaḥ asau yakṣaḥ vilakṣaṇaḥ sā abravīt sa smitam devī sa yuṣmākam agocaraḥ tena idam bhramyate cakram saṃsāra ākhyam carācaram devāḥ ca vismitāḥ sarve tām praṇamya divam yayuḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṃḍe dvitīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ sa śivaḥ sarvam eva idam svakīyābhiḥ ca mūrtibhiḥ atha anyāḥ ca api tanavaḥ paṃca brahma samāhvayāḥ brahmāṇi etāni devasya mūrttayaḥ paṃca viśrutāḥ īśāna ākhyā tu yā tasya mūrttiḥ ādyā garīyasī bhoktāram prakṛteḥ sākṣāt kṣetrajñam adhitiṣṭhati sthāṇoḥ tatpuruṣa ākhyā yā mūrttiḥ mūrttimataḥ prabhoḥ guṇa āśraya ātmakam bhogyam avyaktam adhitiṣṭhati dharma ādi aṣṭa aṅga saṃyuktam buddhi tattvam pinākinaḥ adhitiṣṭhati aghora ākhyā mūrttiḥ atyanta pūjitā vāmadeva āhvayām mūrttim mahādevasya vedhasaḥ ahaṃkṛteḥ adhiṣṭhātrīm āhuḥ āgama vedinaḥ sadyas jāta āhvayām mūrttim śambhoḥ amita varcasaḥ mānasaḥ samadhiṣṭhātrīm matimantaḥ pracakṣate īśvarīm īśvarasya imām īśa ākhyām hi viduḥ budhāḥ tvak pāṇi sparśa vāyūnām īśvarīm mūrtim aiśvarīm cakṣuṣaḥ caraṇasya api rūpasya agneḥ
