tathā eva ca aghora ākhyām adhiṣṭhātrīm mūrtim āhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ ramanāyāḥ ca pāyoḥ ca rasasya apām tathā eva ca īśvarīm vāmadeva ākhyām mūrttim tad niratām viduḥ sadyas jāta āhvayām mūrtim īśvarīm saṃpracakṣate tasmin vyāpya sthitam viśvam sūtre maṇi gaṇāḥ iva śarvaḥ bhavaḥ tathā rudraḥ ugraḥ bhīmaḥ paśoḥ patiḥ īśānaḥ ca mahādevaḥ mūrttayaḥ ca aṣṭa viśrutāḥ bhūmi aṃbhaḥ agni marut vyoma kṣetrajña arka niśākarāḥ adhiṣṭhitāḥ maheśasya śarva ādyaiḥ aṣṭa mūrttibhiḥ carācara ātmakam viśvam dhatte viśvaṃbharā ātmikā śārvīḥ śiva āhvayā mūrtiḥ iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ bhāvi iti gīyate mūrtti bhavasya paramātmanaḥ bahis antargatā viśvam vyāpya tejaḥ mayī śubhā raudrī rudra avyayā mūrtiḥ āsthitā ghora rūpiṇī mūrtiḥ bhīmasya bhīma ākhyā bhūta vṛndasya bhedikā sarva ātmanām adhiṣṭhātrī sarva kṣetra nivāsinī mūrtiḥ paśupateḥ jñeyā paśu pāśa nikṛṃtanī dīpayantī jagat sarvam divākara samāhvayā īśāna ākhya maheśasya mūrtiḥ divi visarpati āpyāyayati yaḥ viśvam amṛtāṃśuḥ niśākaraḥ mahādevasya sā mūrtiḥ mahādeva samāhvayā vyāpikā itara mūrtīnām viśvam tasmāt śiva ātmakam sarva upakāra karaṇam śivasya ārādhanam viduḥ aniṣṭam aṣṭamūrteḥ tat kṛtam eva na saṃśayaḥ aṣṭamūrti ātmanā viśvam adhiṣṭhāya sthitam śivam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe tṛtīyaḥ adhyāyaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca mūrtibhiḥ viśvam eva idam yathā vyāptam tathā śrutam athā etat jñātum icchāmi yāthātmyam pamā īśayoḥ strī puṃs bhāva ātmakam ca idam tābhyām katham adhiṣṭhitam śrīmat vibhūtim śivayoḥ yāthātmyam ca samāsatas śaktiḥ sākṣāt mahādevī mahādevaḥ ca śaktimān dvayam śuddham aśuddham ca param ca aparam eva ca śivasya ca śivāyāḥ ca svāmyam ca etad svabhāvataḥ śivayoḥ vai vaśe viśvam na viśvasya vaśe śivau īśitavyam idam yasmāt tasmāt viśveśvarau śivau na bhāti vidyamānaḥ api tathā śaktyā vinā śivaḥ prabhā ca bhānunā tena sutarām tad upāśrayā evam paraspara apekṣā śakti śaktimatoḥ sthitā ādyā sā ekā parā śaktiḥ cit mayī śiva saṃśrayā samāna dharmiṇīm eva śivasya paramātmanaḥ sā ekā parā ca cit rūpā śaktiḥ prasava dharmiṇī vibhajya bahudhā viśvam vidadhāti śiva icchayā sā mūlaprakṛtiḥ māyā triguṇā ca tridhā smṛtā śiva icchayā parāśaktiḥ śivatattva eka tām gatā tatas kriyā ākhyayā śaktyā śaktau śaktimat utthayā tasyām vikṣobhyamāṇāyām ādau nādaḥ samudbabhau nādāt viniḥsṛtaḥ binduḥ bindodevaḥ sadāśivaḥ tasmāt maheśvaraḥ jātaḥ śuddhavidyā maheśvarāt sā vācām īśvarī śaktiḥ vāgīśa ākhyāḥ hi śūlinaḥ yā sā varṇa svarūpeṇa mātṛkā ipi vijṛmbhate atha ananta samāveśāt māyā kālam avāsṛjat niyatim ca kalām vidyām kalātas rāga pūruṣau māyātaḥ punar eva abhūt avyaktam triguṇa ātmakam triguṇāt ca tatas vyaktāt vibhaktāḥ syuḥ trayaḥ guṇāḥ guṇebhyaḥ kṣobhyamāṇebhyaḥ guṇeśa ākhyāḥ trimūrttayaḥ tebhyaḥ syuḥ aṇḍa piṇḍāni tu asaṃkhyāni śiva ājñayā adhiṣṭhitāni ananta ādyaiḥ vidyeśaiḥ cakravartibhiḥ śarīra antara bhedena śakteḥ bhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ nānā rūpāḥ tu vijñeyāḥ sthūla sūkṣma vibhedataḥ rudrasya raudrī sā śaktiḥ viṣṇauḥ vai vaiṣṇavī matā śakya ātmanā eva tat vyāptam yathā dehe antarātmanā kalā yā paramā śaktiḥ kathitā paramātmanaḥ evam eṣā parā śaktiḥ īśvara icchā anuyāyinī sthiram caram ca yat viśvam sṛjati iti viniścayaḥ jñāna kriyā cikīrṣābhiḥ tisṛbhiḥ sva ātma śaktibhiḥ śaktimān īśvaraḥ śaśvat viśvam vyāpya adhitiṣṭhati icchā śaktiḥ maheśasya nityā kāryya niyāmikā jñānaśaktiḥ tu tat kāryam karaṇam kāraṇam tathā kalpayati akhilam kāryam kṣaṇāt saṃkalpa rūpiṇī yathā śakti traya utthānam śakti prasava dharmiṇī śaktyā paramayā nunnā prasūte sakalam jagat evam śakti samāyogāt śaktimān ucyate śivaḥ śakti śaktimat uttham tu śāktam śaivam idam jagat yathā na jāyate putraḥ pitaram mātaram vinā tathā bhavam bhavānīm ca vinā nā etat carācaram strīpuṃsa prabhavam viśvam strīpuṃsa ātmakam eva ca strīpuṃsayoḥ vibhūtiḥ ca strīpuṃsābhyām adhiṣṭhitam śivaḥ sadāśivaḥ proktaḥ śivā sā ca manonmanī śivaḥ maheśvaraḥ jñeyaḥ śivā māyā iti kathyate viṣṇuḥ viśveśvaraḥ devaḥ lakṣmīḥ viśveśvara priyā brahmā śivaḥ yadā sraṣṭā brahmāṇī brahmaṇaḥ priyā bhāskaraḥ bhagavān śaṃbhuḥ prabhā bhagavatī śivā nirṛtiḥ bhagavān īśaḥ nairṛtī naganandanī yakṣaḥ yajñaśiraḥ hartā ṛddhiḥ himagiri indrajā īśānaḥ parameśānaḥ tad āryā parameśvarī dakṣaḥ sākṣāt mahādevaḥ prasūtiḥ parameśvarī vasiṣṭhaḥ manmatha arātiḥ devī sākṣāt arundhatī viṣayī bhagavān īśaḥ viṣayaḥ parameśvarī rasa jātam mahādevī devaḥ rasayitā śivaḥ preya jātam ca girijā preyān ca eva gara aśanaḥ prāṇaḥ pinākī sarveṣām prāṇinām bhagavān prabhuḥ prāṇa sthitiḥ tu sarveṣām aṃbikā ca ambu rūpiṇī kṣetrajña tvam tadā dhatte bhagavān antaka antakaḥ samudraḥ bhagavān īśaḥ velā śailendra kanyakā puṃlliṃgam akhilam dhatte bhagavān puraśāsanaḥ strī liṃgam ca akhilam dhatte devī deva manoramā śabda jālam aśeṣam tu dhatte sarvasya vallabhā yasya yasya padārthasya yā yā śaktiḥ udāhṛtā tat tat āha mahābhāgāḥ tayoḥ tejaḥ vijṛṃbhitam yathā dīpasya dīptasya śikhā dīpayate gṛham tṛṇa ādi śiva mūrti antam viśva khyā atiśaya kramaḥ sannikarṣa krama vaśāt tayoḥ iti parā śrutiḥ pūjanīyau namaskāryau ciṃtanīyau ca sarvadā yathāprajñam idam kṛṣṇa yāthātmyam parameśayoḥ tat katham śakyate vaktum yāthātmyam parameśayoḥ antargatam ananyānām īśvara arpita cetasām anyeṣām buddhi an ārūḍham ārūḍham ca yathā eva tat yā iyam uktā vibhūtiḥ vai prākṛtī sā parā matā aprākṛtām parām anyām guhyām guhya vidaḥ viduḥ aprākṛtī parā ca eṣā vibhūtiḥ pārameśvarī sā eva iha paramā kāṣṭhā vibhūtiḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ tām prāptum prayatante atra jita śvāsāḥ jita indriyāḥ garbhakārāḥ gṛha dvāram niśchidram ghaṭitum yathā saṃsāra āśīviṣa ālīḍha mṛta saṃjīvana auṣadham yaḥ parām aparām ca eva vibhūtim vetti tattvataḥ saḥ aparaḥ bhūtim ullaṃghya parām bhūtim samaśnute na a śiṣyebhyaḥ api a śaivebhyaḥ na abhaktebhyaḥ kadācana vyāharet īśayoḥ bhūtim iti veda anuśāsanam tvādṛśebhyaḥ anurūpebhyaḥ kathaya etat na ca anyathā kīrtanāt asya naśyanti mahāntyaḥ pāpa koṭayaḥ bhaktiḥ parāḥ śive sa ambe sa anuge sa paricchide iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe gaurīśaṃkaravibhūtiyogaḥ nāma caturthaḥ adhyāyaḥ tat evam na vijānanti paśavaḥ pāśa gauravāt a jānantaḥ param bhāvam avikalpam mahā ṛṣayaḥ vidyām tu cetanām prāhuḥ tathā avidyām acetanām vidyā ca eva viśvam viśva guroḥ vibhoḥ rūpam eva na saṃdehaḥ viśvam tasya vaśe yatas bhrāntiḥ vidyā parā ca iti śārvam rūpam param viduḥ te rūpe parameśasya tad icchāyāḥ pravartanāt kāraṇa artha vidaḥ prāhuḥ samaṣṭi vyaṣṭi kāraṇam vyaktiḥ vyāvṛtti rūpam tam piṇḍa jāteḥ samāśrayam jātayaḥ vyaktayaḥ ca eva tad ājñā paripālitāḥ kālaḥ kāryya prapaṃcasya pariṇāma eka kāraṇam eṣām īśaḥ āvirbhāva tirobhāva hetuḥ ekaḥ svarāj ajaḥ hetuḥ netā adhipaḥ teṣām dhātā ca uktā maheśvaraḥ antaryāmī paraḥ ca iti kathyate kavibhiḥ śivaḥ mātā mānam ca meyam ca matim ca āhuḥ atha apare tam āhuḥ viguṇam kecid guṇavantam pare viduḥ kecid saṃsāriṇam prāhuḥ tam asaṃsāriṇam pare rāgavantam pare prāhuḥ vīta rāgam tathā pare niṣkriyam ca pare prāhuḥ sa kriyam ca itare janāḥ arūpam kecid āhuḥ vai rūpavantam pare viduḥ adṛśyam apare prāhuḥ dṛśyam iti apare viduḥ śabda ātmakam pare prāhuḥ śabdātītam atha apare kecid cintā mayam prāhuḥ cintayā rahitam pare jñāna ātmakam pare prāhuḥ vijñānam iti ca apare na adhyavasyanti munayaḥ nānā pratyaya kāraṇāt yāvat paśuḥ na eva paśyati anīśam purāṇam bhuvanasya īśitāram tāvat duḥkhe vartate baddha pāśaḥ saṃsāre yadā paśyaḥ paśyate rukma varṇam kartāram īśam puruṣam brahma yonim iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe paśupatitvajñānayogaḥ nāma paṃcamaḥ adhyāyaḥ na śivasya āṇavaḥ bandhaḥ kāryaḥ māyā iyaḥ eva vā prākṛtaḥ vā atha boddhā vā hi ahaṃkāra ātmakaḥ tathā na rāgaḥ na ca vidveṣaḥ śaṃbhoḥ amita tejasaḥ na ca asti abhiniveśaḥ asya kuśalā akuśalāni api āśayaiḥ nā api saṃbandhaḥ saṃskāraiḥ karmaṇām api na tasya kāraṇam kartā na ādiḥ antaḥ tathā antaram na karma karaṇam vā api na akāryam kāryam eva ca na janma maraṇe tasya na kāṃkṣitam a kāṃkṣitam kalyāṇam sakalam ca asti paramātmā śivaḥ yatas apracyutaḥ svatas bhāvaḥ sthitaḥ sthāṇuḥ atas smṛtaḥ ekaḥ rudraḥ param brahma puruṣaḥ kṛṣṇa piṃgalaḥ bālāgra mātraḥ hṛd madhye viciṃtyaḥ dahara antare hiraṇyakeśaḥ padmākṣaḥ hi aruṇaḥ tāmraḥ eva ca saumyaḥ ghoraḥ tathā miśraḥ ca akṣāraḥ ca amṛtaḥ sa puṃviśeṣaḥ paramaḥ bhagavān antaka antakaḥ śuddhā svābhāvikī tasya śaktiḥ sarva atiśāyinī tejaḥ prabhāvaḥ vīryam ca kṣamā kāruṇyam eva ca tasmāt ekākṣaram devam āhuḥ āgama pāragāḥ vācya vācakayoḥ aikyam manyamānāḥ manasvinaḥ asya mātrāḥ samākhyātāḥ catasraḥ veda mūrddhani akāram bahvṛcam prāhuḥ ukāraḥ yajuḥ ucyate makāraḥ sāma nādaḥ asya śrutiḥ ātharvaṇī smṛtāḥ akāraḥ ca mahābījam rajaḥ sraṣṭā caturmukhaḥ ukāraḥ prakṛtiḥ yoniḥ sattvam pālayitā hariḥ makāraḥ puruṣaḥ bījam tamaḥ saṃhārakaḥ haraḥ sarvam tisṛbhiḥ evā idam mātrābhiḥ nikhilam tridhā abhidhāya śiva ātmānam bodhayati ardha mātrayā yasmāt param na aparam asti kiṃcid yasmāt na aṇīyaḥ na jyāyaḥ asti kiṃcid vṛkṣaḥ iva stabdhaḥ divi tiṣṭhati ekaḥ tena idam pūrṇam puruṣeṇa sarvam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śivatattvavarṇanam nāma ṣaṣṭhaḥ adhyāyaḥ śaktiḥ svābhavikī tasya vidyā viśva vilakṣaṇā eka anekasya rūpeṇa bhāti bhānoḥ iva prabhā anantāḥ śaktayaḥ yasyāḥ icchā jñāna kriyā ādayaḥ māyā ādyāḥ ca abhavan vahnoḥ visphuliṃgāḥ yathā tathā sadāśiva īśvara ādyāḥ hi vidyā avidyā īśvara ādayaḥ mahat ādi viśeṣa antāḥ tu aja ādyāḥ ca api mūrttayaḥ yat ca anyat asti tat sarvam tasyāḥ kāryyam na saṃśayaḥ sā śaktiḥ sarva gā sūkṣmā prabodha ānanda rūpiṇī śaktimān ucyate devaḥ śivaḥ śītāṃśu bhūṣaṇaḥ vedyaḥ śivaḥ śivā vidyā prajñā ca eva śrutiḥ smṛtiḥ dhṛtiḥ eṣā sthitiḥ niṣṭhā jñāna icchā karma śaktayaḥ śuddhavidyā śuddha kalā sarvam śakti kṛtam yatas māyā ca prakṛtiḥ jīvaḥ vikāraḥ vikṛtiḥ tathā anayā saha sarva īśaḥ saptaviṃśa prakārayā saṃśaya āviṣṭa manasaḥ vismṛśanti yathātatham kim kāraṇam kutas jātāḥ jīvāmaḥ kena vā vayam kutra asmākam saṃpratiṣṭhā kena vā adhiṣṭhitāḥ vayam kena vartāmahe śaśvat sukheṣu anyeṣu ca aniśam avilaṃghyā ca viśvasya vyavasthā kena vā kṛtā bhūtāni yoniḥ puruṣaḥ yogī ca eṣām paraḥ atha vā acetana tvāt kāla ādeḥ cetana tve pi ca ātmanaḥ sukha duḥkhāni bhūta tvāt anīśa tvāt vicāryate tad dhyāna yoga anugatām prapaśyan śaktim aiśvarīm pāśa vicchedikām sākṣāt nigūḍhām sva guṇaiḥ bhṛśam śaktimantam mahādevam apaśyan divya cakṣuṣā yaḥ kāraṇāni aśeṣāṇi kāla ātma sahitāni ca aprameyaḥ anayā śaktyā sakalam yaḥ adhitiṣṭhati dṛṣṭena bhakti yogena divyaḥ gatim avāpnuyuḥ tasmāt saha tathā śaktyā hṛdi paśyanti ye śivam teṣām śāśvatikī śāntiḥ na etareṣām iti śrutiḥ tasmāt śakteḥ śaktimataḥ tādātmyāt nirvṛtiḥ dvayoḥ kramaḥ vivakṣitaḥ nūnam vimuktau jñāna karmaṇoḥ kīraḥ vā atha kṛmiḥ vā api mucyate tad prasādataḥ vṛddhaḥ vā mriyamāṇaḥ vā svarga sthaḥ vā atha nārakī patitaḥ vā api dharma ātmā paṃḍitaḥ mūḍhaḥ eva vā ayogyānām ca kāruṇyāt bhaktānām parameśvaraḥ prasāda pūrvikā yā iyam bhukti mukti vidhāyinī na yoni yantra pīḍāyai bhavet na eva atra saṃśayaḥ sāṃgā ca yā sevā sā bhaktiḥ iti kathyate śiva rūpa ādi ciṃtā yā sā sevā mānasī smṛtā japa ādiḥ vācikī sevā karma pūjā ādi kāyikī sā iyam tri sādhanā sevā śiva dharmaḥ ca kathyate sa tu paṃcavidhaḥ proktaḥ śivena paramātmanā tapaḥ karma japaḥ dhyānam jñānam ca iti samāsatas japaḥ tridhā śiva abhyāsaḥ cintā dhyānam śivasya tu śiva āgama uktam yat jñānam tat atra jñānam ucyate śrīkaṃṭhena śivena uktam śivāyai ca śiva āgamaḥ śiva āśritānām kāruṇyāt śreyasām eka sādhanam tyajet ca viṣaya āsaṃgam śreyaḥ arthī matimān naraḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śivatattvakathanam nāma saptamaḥ adhyāyaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca vedasāre śiva jñānam sva āśritānām vimuktaye abhaktānām abuddhīnām ayuktānām agocaram śata koṭi pramāṇena vistīrṇam grantha saṃkhyayā kasya adhikāraḥ pūjā ādau jñāna yoga ādayaḥ katham sisṛkṣayā purā avyaktāt śivaḥ sthāṇuḥ maheśvaraḥ janayāmāsa ca tadā ṛṣiḥ viśva adhikaḥ prabhuḥ devānām prathamam devam brahmāṇam brahmaṇaspatim brahmā api pitaram devam jāyamānam nyavaikṣata tam jāyamānam janakaḥ devaḥ prāpaśyat ājñayā dṛṣṭaḥ rudreṇa devaḥ asau asṛjat viśvam īśvaraḥ varṇa āśrama vyavasthām ca cakāra sa pṛthak pṛthak dharā ca vahniḥ sūryaḥ ca yajñaḥ viṣṇuḥ śacīpatiḥ te ca anye ca surāḥ rudram rudrādhyāyena tuṣṭuvuḥ apahṛtya sva līlā artham teṣām jñānam maheśvaraḥ tam apṛcchan tatas devāḥ kaḥ bhavān iti mohitāḥ a paśyantaḥ tam īśānam stuvantaḥ ca eva sāmabhiḥ vratam pāśupatam kṛtvā tu atharvaśirasi sthitam yam vinidrāḥ jita śvāsāḥ yoginaḥ dagdha kilbiṣāḥ hṛdi paśyanti tam devam dadṛśuḥ deva puṃgavāḥ yām āhuḥ paramām śaktim īśvara icchā anuvartinīm tām apaśyat maheśasya vāmatas vāma locanām ye vinirdhūta saṃsārāḥ prāptāḥ śaivam param padam nitya siddhāḥ ca ye vā anyam te ca dṛṣṭāḥ gaṇeśvarāḥ atha tam tuṣṭuvuḥ devāḥ devyā saha maheśvaram tuṣṭaḥ asmi iti āha su prītaḥ svabhāva madhurām giram devāḥ ūcuḥ kasya adhikāraḥ pūjāyām vaktum arhasi tattvataḥ śaktibhiḥ mūrtibhiḥ ca aṃgaiḥ grahaiḥ devaiḥ ca saṃvṛtam buddhvā divākaram devam devīm ca eva niśākaram su ratna pūrṇam sa suvarṇa toyam su kuṃkuma ādyam sa kuśam sa puṣpam praṇamet vā paṭhet etān ślokān śruti mukhān imān atha devān samālokya maṇḍala sthaḥ maheśvaraḥ sarva āgama uttaram dattvā śāstram antaradhāt haraḥ tatra pūjā adhikāraḥ jñātvā praṇamya deveśam devāḥ jagmuḥ yathāgatam tayā sa coditaḥ devaḥ devyā candra vibhūṣaṇaḥ mayā agastyena guruṇā dadhīcena mahā ṛṣiṇā svayam api avatīrya urvyām yuga āvarteṣu śūladhṛk sva āśritānām vimukti artham kurute jñāna santatim teṣām saṃbhāvanāt loke śaiva ājñā karaṇa ādibhiḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śivatattvajñāne vyāsāvatāravarṇanam nāma aṣṭamaḥ adhyāyaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca avatārān hi śarvasya śiṣyān ca bhagavan vada supālakaḥ gautamaḥ ca tathā vedaśirāḥ muniḥ jaṭāmālī ca aṭṭahāsaḥ dārukaḥ lāṃgulī tathā mahākālaḥ ca śūlī ca ḍaṃḍī muṇḍīśaḥ eva ca sa viṣṇuḥ somaśarmā ca lakuli īśvaraḥ eva ca aṣṭāviṃśati saṃkhyātāḥ yoga ācāryāḥ yuga kramāt śiṣyāḥ pratyekam eteṣām catvāraḥ śānta cetasaḥ śveta ādayaḥ ca ruṣya antān tān bravīmi yathākramam kapilaḥ ca āsuriḥ paṃcaśikhaḥ bāṣkalaḥ eva ca ete śiṣyāḥ maheśasya yoga ācārya svarūpiṇaḥ śiva āśrama ratāḥ sarve śiva jñāna parāyaṇāḥ sarve saṃga vinirmuktāḥ śiva eka āsakta cetasaḥ śikhā jaṭāḥ sarva jaṭāḥ ajaṭāḥ muṃḍa śīrṣakāḥ samunmathita saṃsāra viṣa vṛkṣa aṃkura udgamāḥ prayātum eva sannaddhāḥ param śiva puram prati sa deśikān imān matvā nityam yaḥ śivam arcayet iti śrī śivamahāpur saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śivasya yogāvatāravarṇanam nāma navamaḥ adhyāyaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca prāyas tvam avatīrya urvyām pāśa vicchittaye nṛṇām tvattaḥ nyaḥ paramam bhāvam kaḥ jānīyāt śiva ātmakam tasmāt tava mukha udgīrṇam sākṣāt iva pinākinaḥ śiva jñāna amṛtam pītvā na me tṛptam abhūt manaḥ sākṣāt sarva jagat kartuḥ bhartuḥ aṃkam samāśritā sthāne pṛṣṭam tvayā kṛṣṇa tat vakṣyāmi yathātatham tadā devyāḥ priya sakhī su smita āsyā śubhāvatī phullāni ati manojñāni puṣpāṇi samudāharat tatas svam aṃkam āropya devīm alaṃkṛtya ca taiḥ puṣpaiḥ āste hṛṣṭataraḥ svayam atha antaḥpura cāriṇyaḥ devyaḥ divya vibhūṣaṇāḥ antaraṃgāḥ gaṇa indrāḥ ca sarva loka maheśvarīm cāmara āsakta hastāḥ ca devīm devam siṣevire tatas priyāḥ kathāḥ vṛttāḥ vinodāya maheśayoḥ trāṇāya ca nṛṇām loke ye śivam śaraṇam gatāḥ kena vaśyaḥ mahādevaḥ martyānām manda cetasām ātma tattva ādi aśaktānām ātmanām akṛtātmanām īśvaraḥ uvāca na karmaṇā na tapasā na japaiḥ na āsana ādibhiḥ na jñānena na ca anyena vaśyaḥ aham śraddhayā vinā śraddhā mayi asti ced puṃsām yena kena api hetunā vaśyaḥ spṛśyaḥ ca dṛśyaḥ ca pūjyaḥ saṃbhāṣyaḥ eva ca sādhyā tasmāt mayi śaddhā mām vaśīkartum icchatā śraddhā hetuḥ svadharmasya rakṣaṇam varṇinām iha āmnāya siddham akhilam dharmam āśramiṇām iha brahmaṇā kathitam pūrvam mama eva ājñā purassaram tena dharmeṇa mahatām śraddhām prāpya su durllabhām varṇinaḥ ye prapadyante mām an anya samāśrayāḥ teṣām sukhena mārgeṇa dharma kāma artha muktayaḥ tasmin bhaktimatām eva madīyānām tu varṇinām adhikāraḥ na ca anyeṣām iti ājñā naiṣṭhikī mama tad ājñaptena mārgeṇa varṇinaḥ mad upāśrayāḥ mala māyā ādi pāśebhyaḥ vimuktāḥ mad prasādataḥ paramam mama sādharmyam prāpya nirvṛtim āyayuḥ alabdha lābhaḥ eva eṣa koṭi koṭi guṇa adhikaḥ mama avatārāḥ hi śubhe yoga ācārya chalena tu ayuktānām abuddhīnām abhaktānām sureśvari durlabham santati jñānam tatas yatnāt samāśrayet yat anyatra śramam kuryyāt mokṣa mārga bahiṣkṛtaḥ jñānam kriyā ca caryā ca yogaḥ ca iti sureśvari aśvamedha gaṇāt śreṣṭham devi citta prasādhanam mukti dam ca tathā hi etat duṣprāpyam viṣaya eṣiṇām dayā kāryā atha satatam ahiṃsā jñāna saṃgrahaḥ dhyānam īśvara bhāvaḥ ca satatam jñāna śīla tā acirāt eva vijñānam labdhvā yogam ca vindati dagdhvā deham imam jñānī kṣaṇāt jñāna agninā priye tasmāt niyogataḥ yogī puṇya apuṇyam vivarjayet jñāna yoga rataḥ bhūtvā paścāt yogam samabhyaset na yajanti hi mām yuktāḥ sama loṣṭa aśma kāṃcanāḥ nitya yuktaḥ muniḥ śreṣṭhaḥ mad bhaktaḥ ca samāhitaḥ jñāna yoga rataḥ yogī mama sāyujyam āpnuyāt atha a virakta cittāḥ ye varṇinaḥ mad upāśritāḥ vāc manaḥ kāya bhedāt ca tridhā mad bhajanam viduḥ tapaḥ karma japaḥ dhyānam jñānam vā iti anupūrvaśas mad nāma niratā vāṇī vāGmatā khalu na itarā liṃgaiḥ mad śāsana ādiṣṭaiḥ tripuṃḍra ādibhiḥ aṃkitaḥ mama upacāra nirataḥ kāyaḥ kāyaḥ na ca itaraḥ mad arcā karma vijñeyam bāhye yāga ādinā ucyate japaḥ paṃcākṣara abhyāsaḥ praṇava abhyāsaḥ eva ca rudra adhyāya ādika abhyāsaḥ na veda adhyayana ādikam na ātma ādi artha samādhayaḥ mama āgama artha vijñānam jñānam na anya artha vedanam prāk vāsanā vaśāt devi tattva niṣṭhām samācaret bāhyāt ābhyantaram śreṣṭham bhavet śataguṇa adhikam asaṃkara tvāt doṣāṇām dṛṣṭānām api asambhavāt śaucam ābhyantaram vidyāt na bāhyam śaucam ucyate bahis vā abhyantaram vā atha mayā bhāvaḥ hi gṛhyate bhāva eka ātmā kriyā devi mama dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ phala arthī tad abhāve mām parityaktum kṣamaḥ yatas phala anapekṣayā yeṣām manaḥ mad pravaṇam bhavet prārthayeyuḥ phalam paścāt bhaktāḥ te api mama priyāḥ prāk saṃskāra vaśāt eva ye viciṃtya phala aphale vivaśāḥ mām prapadyante mama priyatamāḥ matāḥ mama api lābhaḥ tad lābhāt na aparaḥ parameśvari mahātmanām ananyānām mayi saṃnyasta cetasām aṣṭadhā lakṣaṇam prāhuḥ mama dharma adhikāriṇām iti śrī śivamahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śivabhaktivarṇanam nāma daśamaḥ adhyāyaḥ īśvaraḥ uvāca atha vakṣyāmi deveśi bhaktānām adhikāriṇām viduṣām dvijamukhyānām varṇa dharma samāsataḥ tris snānam ca agni kāryam ca liṃga arcanam anukramam dānam īśrara bhāvaḥ ca dayā sarvatra sarvadā vyākhyānam brahmacaryam ca śravaṇam ca tapaḥ kṣamā śaucam śikhā upavītam ca uṣṇīṣam ca uttarīyakam niṣiddha asevanam ca eva bhasma rudrākṣa dhāraṇam pānam ca brahmakūrcasya māsi māsi yathāvidhi abhyarcanam viśeṣeṇa tena eva snāpya mām priye tathā paryuṣita annasya yāvakasya viśeṣataḥ kṣamā śāntiḥ ca santoṣaḥ satyam asteyam eva ca sarva saṃga nivṛttiḥ ca daśa etāni viśeṣataḥ liṃgāni yoginām bhūyas divā bhikṣā aśanam tathā rātrau na bhojanam kāryam sarveṣām brahmacāriṇām vaiśyasya ca viśeṣeṇa mayā na atra vidhīyate rakṣaṇam sarva varṇānām yuddhe śatru vadhaḥ tathā duṣṭa pakṣi mṛgāṇām ca duṣṭānām śātanam nṛṇām aviśvāsaḥ ca sarvatra viśvāsaḥ mama yogiṣu strī saṃsargaḥ ca kāleṣu camū rakṣaṇam eva ca sadā saṃcāritaiḥ cāraiḥ loka vṛttānta vedanam sadā astra dhāraṇam ca eva bhasma kaṃcuka dhāraṇam rājñām mama āśrama sthānām eṣa dharmasya saṃgrahaḥ gorakṣaṇam ca vāṇijyam kṛṣiḥ vaiśyasya kathyate śuśrūṣā itara varṇānām dharmaḥ śūdrasya kathyate udyāna karaṇam ca eva mama kṣetra samāśrayaḥ yā nārī bhartṛ śuśrūṣām vihāya vrata tatparā atha bhartṛ vihīnāyāḥ vakṣye dharmam sanātanam brahmacaryam sadā snānam bhasmanā salilena vā śāntiḥ maunam kṣamā nityam saṃvibhāgaḥ yathāvidhi iti saṃkṣepataḥ proktaḥ mayā āśrama niṣeviṇām tathā eva vānaprasthānām gṛhasthānām ca sundari dhyeyaḥ tvayā aham deveśi sadā jāpyaḥ ṣaṣ akṣaraḥ atha ye mānavāḥ loke sva icchayā dhṛta vigrahāḥ bhāva atiśaya saṃpannāḥ pūrva saṃskāra saṃyutāḥ viraktā vā anuraktā vā strī ādīnām viṣayeṣu api teṣām mama ātma vijñānam viśuddhānām vivekinām mad prasādāt viśuddhānām duḥkham āśrama rakṣaṇāt na asti kṛtyam akṛtyam ca samādhiḥ vā parāyaṇam na vidhiḥ na niṣedhaḥ ca teṣām mama yathā tathā tathā eva kṛtakṛtyānām teṣām api na saṃśayaḥ mad bhaktānām hita arthāya mānuṣam bhāvam āśritāḥ mama anuśāsanam yadvat brahma ādīnām pravartakam tathā narāṇām anyeṣām tad niyogaḥ pravartakaḥ mama ājñā dhāra bhāvena sadbhāva atiśayena ca mayi bhāvavatām puṃsām prāk adṛṣṭa artha gocarāḥ kaṃpa svedaḥ kaṇṭhe ca svara vikriyā ānanda ādi upalabdhiḥ ca bhavet ākasmikī muhur prākṛtān iva manvānaḥ na avajānīta paṃḍitaḥ avajñānam kṛtam teṣu naraiḥ vyāmūḍha cetanaiḥ āyuḥ śriyam kulam śīlam hitvā nirayam āvahet brahma viṣṇu sureśānām api tūlāyate padam aśuddham bauddham aiśvaryam prākṛtam pauruṣam tathā guṇa īśānām atas tyājyam guṇa atīta pada eṣiṇām ittham śrīkaṇṭha nāthena śivena paramātmanā hitāya jagatām uktaḥ jñāna sāra artha saṃgrahaḥ sa itihāsa purāṇāni vidyā vyākhyāna vistaraḥ jñānam jñeyam anuṣṭheyam adhikāraḥ atha sādhanam sādhyam ca iti ṣaṣ arthānām saṃgraha tva eṣa saṃgrahaḥ guroḥ adhikṛtam jñānam jñeyam pāśaḥ paśuḥ patiḥ liṃga arcana ādi anuṣṭheyam bhaktaḥ tu adhikṛtaḥ api yaḥ sādhanam śiva mantra ādyam sādhyam śiva samāna tā ṣaṣ artha saṃgrahasya asya jñānāt sarvajña tā ucyate na karma karaṇīyam hi bahis tasya mahātmanāḥ na antar na ca bahis kṛṣṇa kṛtyam asti kadācana jñānena jñeyam ālokya ajñānam ca api parityajet na ekāgram ced śive cittam kim kṛtena api karmaṇā ekāgram eva ced cittam kim kṛtena api karmaṇā tasmāt karmāṇi a kṛtvā vā kṛtvā vā antar bahis kramāt śive niviṣṭa cittānām pratiṣṭhita dhiyām satām iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṃḍe śivajñānavarṇanam nāma ekādaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca yāvat phalam ca tat pūrṇam sarvajñaḥ tu yathā vadet sa aṃgāni veda śāstrāṇi saṃsthitāni ṣaṣ akṣare mantraḥ ṣaṣ akṣaraḥ bhinnaḥ sūtram vṛtyā ātmanā yathā paṃcākṣara jape niṣṭhaḥ mucyate pāpa paṃjarāt iti uktam parameśena devyā pṛṣṭena śūlinā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe paṃcākṣaramāhātmyavarṇanam nāma dvādaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ kṣīṇe varṇa āśrama ācāre saṃkaṭe samupasthite īśvaraḥ uvāca āśritya paramām vidyām hṛdyām paṃcākṣarīm mama bhaktyā ca bhāvitātmānaḥ mucyante kali jāḥ narāḥ dūṣitānām kṛtaghnānām nindakānām chala ātmanām lubdhānām vakra manasām api mad pravaṇa ātmanām mama paṃcākṣarī vidyā saṃsāra bhaya tāriṇī īśvaraḥ uvāca sa mantrakam mām patitaḥ pūjayet yadi mohitaḥ nārakī syāt na sandehaḥ mama paṃcākṣaram vinā abbhakṣāḥ vāyubhakṣāḥ ca ye ca anye vrata karśitāḥ bhaktyā paṃcākṣareṇa eva yaḥ hi mām sakṛt arcayet baddhaḥ vā api atha muktaḥ vā pāśāt paṃcākṣareṇa yaḥ arudraḥ vā sa rudraḥ vā sakṛt paṃca akṣareṇa yaḥ pūjayet patitaḥ vā api mūḍhaḥ vā mucyate naraḥ ṣaṣ akṣareṇa vā devi tathā paṃca akṣareṇa vā alabdha alabdhaḥ evā iha koṭi koṭi guṇa adhikaḥ labdhvā saṃpūjayet yaḥ tu maitrī ādi guṇa saṃyutaḥ mama paṃca akṣare mantre tasmāt śreṣṭhataraḥ hi saḥ tatas sṛṣṭiḥ abhūt mattaḥ prakṛti ātma prabhedataḥ guṇa mūrti ātmanām ca eva tatas vā antara saṃhṛtiḥ jñātvā prayogam vividham siddha mantraḥ prajāpatiḥ tad ājñaptena mārgeṇa mad ārādhana kāṃkṣiṇaḥ ṛṣim chandaḥ ca kīlam ca bīja śaktim ca daivatam proktavān aham āryāṇām jagat sṛṣṭi vivṛddhaye tatas te mantra māhātmyāt ṛṣayaḥ tapasā idhitāḥ ādau namaḥ prayoktavyam śivāya tu tatas param sā eṣā pañcākṣarī vidyā sarva śrutiśiraḥ gatā sarva jātasya sarvasya bīja bhūtā sanātanī prathamam mad mukha udgīrṇā sā mama eva asti vācikā catur bhujā tri nayanā bāla indu kṛta śekharā padma utpala karā saumyā vara da abhaya pāṇikā asyāḥ pañcavidhāḥ varṇāḥ prasphurat raśmi maṃḍalāḥ pītaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ tathā dhūmraḥ svarṇa ābhaḥ raktaḥ eva ca pṛthak prayojyāḥ yadi ete bindu nāda vibhūṣitāḥ bījam dvitīyam bījeṣu mantrasya asya varānane dīrgha pūrvam turīyasya pañcamam śaktim ādiśet gautamaḥ atriḥ varārohe viśvāmitraḥ tathā aṃgirāḥ bharadvājaḥ ca varṇānām kramaśas ca ṛṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ gāyatrī anuṣṭubh triṣṭubh ca chandāṃsi bṛhatī virāj mama paṃca mukhāni āhuḥ sthāne teṣām varānane pūrva ādeḥ ca ūrddhva paryantam nakāra ādi yathākramam adāttaḥ prathamaḥ varṇaḥ caturthaḥ ca dvitīyakaḥ mūlavidyā śivam śaivam sūtram paṃcākṣaram tathā nāmāni asya vijānīyāt śaivam me hṛdayam mahat yakāraḥ phaṭ iti api ca varṇānām ante yadā tadā tatra api paṃcamaḥ varṇaḥ dvādaśa svara bhūṣitaḥ tāḥ asmāt anena mantreṇa manaḥ vāc kāya bhedataḥ āvayoḥ arcanam kuryyāt japa homa ādikam tathā yathāśakti yathāsaṃpad yathāyogam yathārati yadā kadāpi vā bhaktyā yatra kutra api vā kṛtā yena kena api vā devi pūjā muktim nayiṣyate mad priyam ca śivam ca eva krameṇa api akrameṇa vā tathā api mama bhaktāḥ ye na atyanta vivaśāḥ punar teṣām sarveṣu śāstreṣu mayā iva niyamaḥ kṛtaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe paṃcākṣaramāhātmyavarṇanam nāma trayodaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ īśvaraḥ uvāca śuśrūṣum anahaṃkāram snātam śucim upoṣitam snāpayitvā viśuddhi artham pūrṇa kuṃbha ghṛtena vai alaṃkṛtya su veṣam ca gandha sraj vastra bhūṣaṇaiḥ anugṛhya tatas dadyāt jñānam mama yathāvidhi evam dadyāt guruḥ mantram ājñām ca eva tatas param snānam kṛtvā śucau deśe baddhvā rucira mānasam tvayā mām hṛdi saṃciṃtya saṃciṃtya sva gurum tatas viśodhya paṃcatattvāni dahana plāvana ādibhiḥ āvayoḥ vigrahau dhyāyan prāṇa apānau niyamya ca vidyā sthānam svakam rūpam ṛṣin chandaḥ adhidaivatam bījam śaktim tathā vākyam smṛtvā paṃcākṣarīm japet uttamam mānasam jāpyam upāṃśum ca eva madhyamam adhamam vācikam prāhuḥ āgama artha viśāradāḥ jihvā mātra parispandāt īṣat uccāritaḥ api vā aparaiḥ aśrutaḥ kiṃcid śrutaḥ vā upāṃśuḥ ucyate vācikaḥ tu ekaḥ eva syāt upāṃśuḥ śatam ucyate sāhasram mānasaḥ proktaḥ sagarbhaḥ tu śata adhikaḥ ādi antayoḥ agarbhaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ praśasyate mantram mantra artha vid dhīmān aśaktaḥ śaktitaḥ japet eṣu paṃcavidheṣu ekaḥ karttavyaḥ śaktitaḥ japaḥ rekhayā aṣṭaguṇam vidyāt putrajīvaiḥ daśa adhikam triṃśat akṣaiḥ kṛtā mālā dhana dā japa karmaṇi saptaviṃśati saṃkhyātaiḥ akṣaiḥ puṣṭi pradā bhavet paṃcaviṃśati saṃkhyātaiḥ kṛtā muktim prayacchati aṣṭottaraśatam mālā tatra syāt uttamottamā śata saṃkhyā uttamā mālā paṃcāśadbhiḥ tu madhyamā catuḥ paṃcāśat akṣaiḥ tu hṛd śreṣṭhā hi prakīrtitā iti evam mālayā kuryāt japam kasmai na darśayet puṇya araṇye tathā ārāme sahasraguṇam ucyate ayutam parvate puṇye nadyām lakṣam udāhṛtam viprāṇām ca gavām ca eva sannidhau śasyate japaḥ sūryya agni vipra devānām gurūṇām api sannidhau anyeṣām ca prasaktānām mantram na vimukhaḥ japet darśanam ca śva nīcānām varjayet japa karmaṇi ācamet saṃbhave teṣām smaret vā mām tvayā saha jyotīṃṣi ca prapaśyet vā kuryāt vā prāṇasaṃyamam anāsanaḥ śayāne vā gacchan utthitaḥ eva vā yāna śayyā adhirūḍhaḥ vā ciṃtā vyākulitaḥ atha vā paratra ca sukhī na syāt tasmāt ācāravān bhavet tasya tena samācāraḥ sat ācāraḥ na ca itaraḥ sat ācārasya tasya āhuḥ āstikyam mūla kāraṇam āstikaḥ ced pramāda ādyaiḥ sat ācārāt avicyutaḥ na duṣyati naraḥ nityam tasmāt āstika tām vrajet yathā iha asti sukham duḥkham sukṛtaiḥ duṣkṛtaiḥ api na vācyam yasya kasya api nāstikasya atha vā paśoḥ paṃcākṣarāt param na asti paritrāṇam kalau yuge aśuceḥ vā śuceḥ vā api mantraḥ na ca niṣphalaḥ anācāravatām puṃsām aviśuddha ṣaḍadhvanām an ādiṣṭaḥ api guruṇā mantraḥ ayam na ca niṣphalaḥ asādhitaḥ sādhitaḥ vā sidhya tvena na saṃśayaḥ tasmāt mantra antarān tyaktvā sa apāyā anadhikārataḥ āśramet paramām vidyām sākṣāt paṃcākṣarīm budhaḥ ye doṣāḥ sarva mantrāṇām na te asmin saṃbhavanti api sahasā viniyuṃjīta tasmāt eṣa mahā balaḥ evam sākṣāt mahādevyai mahādevena śūlinā jagatām uktaḥ pañcākṣara vidhiḥ yathā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṃḍe paṃcākṣaramahimavarṇanam nāma caturdaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca tad prayoga vidhānam ca sākṣāt śruti samam yathā saṃskāram paramam puṇyam śivena pati bhāṣitam samyak kṛta adhikāraḥ syāt pūjā ādiṣu naraḥ yatas dīyate yena vijñānam kṣīyate pāśa bandhanam tasmāt saṃskāraḥ eva ayam dīkṣā iti api ca kathyate śāṃbhavī ca eva śāktī ca māntrī ca eva śiva āgame dīkṣā upadiśyate tredhā śivena paramātmanā sā dīkṣā śāṃbhavī proktā sā punar bhidyate dvidhā tīvrā tīvratarā ca iti pakṣaya bhedataḥ yayā syāt nirvṛtiḥ sadyas sā eva tīvratarā matā tīvrā tu jīvatā atyantam puṃsaḥ pāpa viśodhikā śaktī jñānavatī dīkṣā śiṣya deham praviśya tu guruṇā yoga mārgeṇa kriyate jñānacakṣuṣā manda mandatara uddeśāt kartavyā guruṇā bahis śaktipāta anusāreṇa śiṣyaḥ anugraham arhati śaiva dharma anusārasya tad mūla tvāt samāsatas tasmāt liṃgāni saṃvīkṣya śaktipātasya bhūyasaḥ jñānena kriyayā vā atha guruḥ śiṣyam viśodhayet tasmāt sarva prakāreṇa guruḥ śiṣyam parīkṣayet lakṣaṇam śaktipātasya prabodha ānanda saṃbhavaḥ sā yasmāt paramā śaktiḥ prabodha ānanda rūpiṇī ānanda bodhayoḥ liṃgam antaḥkaraṇa vikriyāḥ yathā syāt kaṃpa romāñca svara netra aṃga vikriyāḥ śiṣyaḥ api lakṣaṇaiḥ ebhiḥ kuryyāt guru parīkṣaṇam tad saṃparkaiḥ śiva arcā ādau saṃgataiḥ vā atha tad gataiḥ śiṣyaḥ tu śikṣaṇīya tvāt guroḥ gaurava kāraṇāt guruḥ vā śivaḥ eva atha vidyākāreṇa saṃsthitaḥ samakṣam yadi kurvīta sarvam ca anujñayā guroḥ tathā ayam api santuṣṭaḥ guruḥ pāpam kṣaṇāt dahet manasā karmaṇā vācā gurum uddiśya yatnataḥ śreyaḥ arthī ced naraḥ dhīmān na mithyā ācāram ācaret guru priya karaḥ śiṣyaḥ śaiva dharmān tatas arhati guruḥ ced guṇavān prājñaḥ parama ānanda bhāsakaḥ na punar nāma mātreṇa saṃvid ārahitaḥ tu yaḥ paśubhiḥ preritaḥ ca api paśu tvam na ativartate gurum tam eva vṛṇuyāt na aparam matimān naraḥ yāvat vijñāyate tāvat sevanīyaḥ mumukṣubhiḥ jñāte tasmin sthirā bhaktiḥ yāvat tattvam samāśrayet vatsarāt api śiṣyeṇa saḥ anyam gurum upāśrayet gurum ārādhayet prājñam śubhagam priya darśanam sarva abhaya pradātāram karuṇā ākrānta mānasam toṣayet tam prayatnena manasā karmaṇā girā etāni gurave dadyāt bhaktyā vitta anusārataḥ nivedya paścāt sva ātmānam sa anvayam sa parigraham yadā śivāya sva ātmānam dattavān deśika ātmane guruḥ ca sva āśritam śiṣyam varṣam ekam parīkṣayet uttamān ca adhame kṛtvā nīca anuttama karmaṇi te yogyāḥ saṃyatāḥ śuddhāḥ śiva saṃskāra karmaṇi ṛjavaḥ mṛdavaḥ su acchāḥ vinītāḥ sthira cetasaḥ śauca ācāra samāyuktāḥ śiva bhaktāḥ dvijātayaḥ śodhyāḥ bodhyāḥ yathānyāyam iti śāstreṣu niścayaḥ na adhikāraḥ svatas nāryāḥ śiva saṃskāra karmaṇi tathā eva bhartṛ hīnāyāḥ putra ādeḥ abhyanujñayā adhikāraḥ bhavati eva kanyāyāḥ pituḥ ājñayā śūdrāṇām martya jātīnām patitānām viśeṣataḥ tathā saṃkara jātīnām na adhva śuddhiḥ vidhīyate pāda udaka pradāna ādyaiḥ kuryuḥ pāpa viśodhanam atra anuloma jātāḥ ye yuktāḥ eva dvijātiṣu teṣām adhva viśuddhi ādi kuryāt mātṛ kula ucitam yā tu kanyā sva pitṛ ādyaiḥ śiva dharme niyojitā kṛtakṛtyā abhavat pūrvam tapasā ārādhya śaGkaram yathā nārāyaṇam devam tapasā ārādhya pāṃḍavān asvātantrya kṛtaḥ doṣaḥ na iha asti paramārthataḥ śiva dharme niyuktāyāḥ śiva śāsana gauravāt guroḥ ālokanāt eva sparśāt saṃbhāṣaṇāt api yasya saṃjāyate prajñā tasya na asti parājayaḥ kriyāvān yaḥ tu saṃskāraḥ kuṃḍa maṃḍapa pūrvakaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe dīkṣāvidhāne gurumāhātmyam nāma pañcadaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ deśikaḥ prathamam kuryāt saṃskāram samaya āhvayam śilpi śāstra ukta mārgeṇa maṇḍapam tatra kalpayet kṛtvā vedim ca tad madhye kuṇḍāni parikalpayet aṣṭa dikṣu tathā dikṣu tatra aiśānyām punar kramāt pradhāna kuṃḍam kurvīta yat vā paścima bhāgataḥ pradhānam ekam eva atha kṛtvā śobhām prakalpayet vedi madhye tatas kuryāt maṃḍalam śubha lakṣaṇam rakta hema ādibhiḥ cūrṇaiḥ īśvara āvāhana ucitam sindūra śālinī vāra cūrṇaiḥ eva atha nirddhanaḥ eka hastam dvi hastam vā sitam vā raktam eva vā eka hastasya padmasya karṇikā aṣṭa aṃgulā matā kesarāṇi tad arddhāni śeṣam ca aṣṭadala ādikam kṛtvā śobhā upaśobhā āḍhyam aiśānyām tasya kalpayet vrīhi tandula siddhārtha tila puṣpa kuśa āstṛte tatra lakṣaṇa saṃyuktam śiva kuṃbham prasādhayet gandha puṣpa akṣata ākīrṇam kuśa dūrvā aṃkura ācitam sita sūtra āvṛtam kaṃṭhe nava vastra yuga āvṛtam śuddha ambu pūrṇam utkūrcam sa dravyam sa pidhānakam bhṛGgāram vardhanīm ca api śaṃkham ca cakram eva vā vinā sūtra ādikam sarvam padma patram atha api vā maṇḍalasya tatas prācyām mantra kuṃbhe ca pūrvavat kṛtvā vidhivat īśasya mahā pūjām samācaret atha arṇavasya tīre vā nadyām goṣṭhe api vā girau kṛtvā pūrva uditam sarvam vinā vā maṃḍapa ādikam maṃḍalam pūrvavat kṛtvā sthaṃḍilam ca vibhāvasoḥ praviśya pūjā bhavanam prahṛṣṭa vadanaḥ guruḥ mahā pūjām maheśasya kṛtvā maṇḍala madhyatas śiva kuṃbhe tathā bhūyas śivam āvāhya pūjayet paścima abhimukham dhyātvā yajñarakṣakam īśvaram kṛtvā mudrā ādikam sarvam mantra yāgam samācaret tatas śiva anale homam kuryāt deśika sattamaḥ pradhāna kuṇḍe paritas juhuyuḥ ca apare dvijāḥ ācāryāt pādam arddham vā homaḥ teṣām vidhīyate pradhāna kuṇḍe eva atha juhuyāt deśika uttamaḥ japam ca vidhivat ca anye śiva bhakti parāyaṇāḥ nṛtyam gītam ca vādyam ca maṃgalāni aparāṇi ca pūjanam ca sadasyānām kṛtvā samyak vidhānataḥ puṇyāham kārayitvā atha punar saṃpūjya śaṃkaram prārthayet deśikaḥ devam śiṣya anugraha kāmyayā vimocaya enam viśveśa ghṛṇayā ca ghṛṇā nidhe atha ca evam karomi iti labdha anujñaḥ tu deśikaḥ ānīya upoṣitam śiṣyam haviṣya āśinam eva vā eka aśanam vā viratam snātam prātar kṛta kriyam japantam praṇavam devam dhyāyantam kṛta maṃgalam dvārasya paścimasya agra maṇḍale dakṣiṇasya vā darbha āsane samāsīnam vidhāya uda mukham śiśum svayam prāc vadanaḥ tiṣṭhan ūrdhva kāyam kṛta aṃjalim puṣpa kṣepeṇa santāḍya badhnīyāt locanam guruḥ tatas praveśayet śiṣyam guruḥ dvāreṇa maṃḍalam saḥ api tena īritaḥ śaṃbhoḥ ācaret tris pradakṣiṇam tatas suvarṇa saṃmiśram dattvā puṣpa aṃjalim prabhoḥ prāñc mukhaḥ ca udañc mukhaḥ vā praṇamet daṇḍa vat kṣitaḥ sa dṛṣṭvā maṃḍalam bhūyas praṇamet sa añjaliḥ prabhum atha āsīnam śiva ācāryaḥ maṃḍalasya tu dakṣiṇe upaveśya ātmanaḥ savye śiṣyam darbha āsane guruḥ ārādhya ca mahādevam śiva hastam pravinyaset śiva tejaḥ mayam pāṇim śiva mantram udīrayet sarva aṃga ālaṃbanam ca eva kuryāt tena eva deśikaḥ śiṣyaḥ praṇamet bhūmau deśikā kṛtam īśvaram huta āhuti trayam śiṣyam upaveśya yathā purā darbha agraiḥ saṃspṛśan tam ca vidyayā ātmānam āviśet namaskṛtya mahādevam nāḍīsaṃdhānam ācaret śiva śāstra ukta mārgeṇa kṛtvā prāṇasya nirgamam śiṣya deha praveśam ca smṛtvā mantrān tu tarpayet deyāḥ tisraḥ tathā aṃgānām aṃgaiḥ eva yathākramam tatas pūrṇāhutim dattvā prāyaścittāya deśikaḥ punar saṃpūjya deveśam samyak ācamya deśikaḥ hutvā ca eva yathānyāyam sva jātyā vaiśyam uddharet tasya evam janayet kṣātram uddhāram ca tatas punar rājanyam ca evam uddhṛtya kṛtvā vipram punar tayoḥ prokṣaṇam tāḍanam kṛtvā śiśoḥ sva ātmānam ātmani nāḍyā yathā uktayā vāyum recayet mantratas guruḥ nirgamya praviśet nāḍyā śiṣyasya hṛdayam tathā tam ādāya tayā nāḍyā mantrī saṃhāra mudrayā na pūrakeṇa niveśya enam ekībhāva artham ātmanaḥ kuṃbhakena tathā nāḍyā recakena yathā purā tasmāt ādāya śiṣyasya hṛdaye tat niveśayet hutvā paścāt dadyāt pūrṇāhutim tatas devasya dakṣiṇe śiṣyam upaveśya varāsane svastika āsanam ārūḍham vidhāya svayam samādāya ghaṭam pūrṇam pūrṇam eva prasāditam dhyāyamānaḥ śivam śiṣyam ā abhiṣiṃceta deśikaḥ athā apanudya snāna aṃbu paridhāya sita aṃbaram ācānta ulaṃkṛtaḥ śiṣyaḥ prāṃjaliḥ maṃḍapam vrajet upaveśya yathāpūrvam tam guruḥ darbha viṣṭare saṃpūjya maṃḍalam devam kara nyāsam samācaret samālabheta pāṇibhyām śiśum śivam udīrayet sakalīkaraṇam kṛtvā mātṛkā nyāsa vartmanā tatas śiva āsanam dhyātvā śiṣya mūrddhni deśikaḥ tatra āvāhya yathānyāyam arcayet manasā śivam iti vijñāpya tam śaṃbhoḥ tejasā bhāsuram smaret saṃpūjya atha śivam śaivīm ājñām prāpya śiva ātmikām karṇe śiṣyasya śanakais śiva mantram udīrayet sa tu baddhāṃjaliḥ śrutvā mantram tad gata mānasaḥ śanais tam vyāharet śiṣya śiva ācāryasya śāsanāt tatas śāktam ca saṃdiśya mantram mantra vicakṣaṇaḥ uccārayitvā ca sukham tasmai maṃgalam ādiśet tatas samāsāt mantra artham vācya vācaka yogataḥ samadiśya īśvaram rūpam yogam āsanam ādiśet atha guru ājñayā śiṣyaḥ śiva agni guru sannidhau bhaktyā evam abhisaṃdhāya dīkṣā vākyam udīrayet tatas sa samayaḥ nāma bhaviṣyati śiva āśrame labdha adhikāraḥ guru ājñā pālakaḥ tad vaśaḥ bhavet dadyāt śiṣyāya mūlena rudrākṣam ca abhimantritam pratimā vā api devasya gūḍha deham atha api vā pūjā homa japa dhyāna sādhanāni ca saṃbhave saḥ api śiṣyaḥ śiva ācāryāt labdhāni bahu mānataḥ ādadīta ājñayā tasya deśikasya na ca anyathā ācāryāt āptam akhilam śirasi ādhāya bhaktitaḥ rakṣayet pūjayet śaṃbhum maṭhe vā gṛhe eva vā atas param śiva ācāram ādiśet asya deśikaḥ bhakti śraddhā anusāreṇa prajñāyāḥ ca anusārataḥ yat uktam yat samājñātam yat ca eva anyat prakīrtitam śiva ācāryeṇa samaye tat sarvam śirasā vahet śiva āgamasya grahaṇam vācanam śravaṇam tathā deśika deśataḥ kuryāt na sva icchātaḥ na ca anyatas sākṣāt śiva pura prāptau nṛṇām parama sādhanam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṃḍe śiṣyasaṃskāravarṇanam nāma ṣoḍaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ atas param samāvekṣya guruḥ śiṣyasya yogyatām kalām tattvam ca bhuvanam varṇam padam atas param mantraḥ ca iti samāsena ṣaḍadhvā paripaṭhyate nivṛttya ādyāḥ kalāḥ paṃca kalādhvā kathyate budhaiḥ vyāptāḥ kalābhiḥ itare tu adhvānaḥ paṃca paṃcabhiḥ śivatattva ādi bhūmi antam tattvādhvā samudāhṛtaḥ ṣaḍviṃśat saṃkhyayā upetaḥ śuddha aśuddha ubhaya ātmakaḥ ādhāra ādi unmanā antaḥ ca bhuvanādhvā prakīrtitaḥ vinā bheda upabhedābhyām ṣaṣṭi saṃkhyā samanvitaḥ paṃcāśat rudra rūpāḥ tu varṇāḥ varṇa adhva saṃjñitāḥ aneka bheda saṃpannaḥ padādhvā samudāhṛtaḥ yathā śivaḥ na tattveṣu gaṇyate tattva nāyakaḥ kalādhvanaḥ vyāpaka tvam vyāpya tvam ca itara adhvanām vyāpya vyāpaka tā tena jñātum eva na śakyate tasmāt adhva svarūpam ca vyāpya vyāpaka tām tathā kuṃḍa maṃḍala paryantam tatra kṛtvā yathā purā dvi hasta mānam kurvīta prācyām kalaśa maṃḍalam tatas snātaḥ śiva ācāryaḥ sa śiṣyaḥ kṛta naityakaḥ praviśya maṃḍalam śaṃbhoḥ pūjām pūrvavat ācaret tatra āḍhakā varaiḥ siddham tandulaiḥ pāyasam prabhoḥ puratas kalpite vā atha maṃḍale varṇi maṃḍite sthāpayet paṃca kalaśān dikṣu madhye ca deśikaḥ teṣu brahmāṇi mūla arṇaiḥ bindu nāda samanvitaiḥ īśānam madhyame kuṃbhe puruṣam puratas sthite aghoram dakṣiṇe vāme vāmam sadyam ca paścime rakṣām vidhāya mudrā ca baddhvā kuṃbha abhimantraṇam kṛtvā śiva analaiḥ homam prārabhet yathā purā yat arddham pāyasam pūrvam homa artham parikalpitam tarpaṇa antam ca mantrāṇām kṛtvā karma yathā purā hutvā pūrṇāhutim teṣām tatas kuryyāt pradīpanam okārāt anu huṃkāram tatas mūlam phaṭ antakam svāhā antam dīpane prāhuḥ aṃgāni ca yathākramam teṣām āhutayaḥ tisraḥ deyāḥ dīpana karmaṇi sūtram sūtreṇa saṃmantrya śikhā agre bandhayet śiśoḥ caraṇa aṃguṣṭha paryyantam ūrddhvakāyasya tiṣṭhataḥ laṃbayitvā tu tat sūtram suṣumṇām tatra yojayet śāntayā mudrayā ādāya mūlamantreṇa mantra vid hṛdi santāḍya śiṣyasya puṣpa kṣepeṇa pūrvavat caitanyam samupādāya dvādaśānte nivedya ca sūtram sūtreṇa saṃyojya saṃrakṣya astreṇa varmaṇā avaguṃṭhya atha tat sūtram śiṣya deham viciṃtayet mūla traya mayam pāśam bhoga bhogya tva lakṣaṇam vyoma ādi bhūta rūpiṇyaḥ śānti atīta ādayaḥ kalāḥ athavā bīja bhūtaiḥ tat kṛtvā pūrva uditam kramāt kalā vyāptim mala ādau ca hutvā saṃdīpayet kalāḥ śiṣyam śirasi santāḍya sūtram dehe yathākramam śānti atīta pade sūtram lāñchayet mantram uccaran evam kṛtvā nivṛtti antam śānti atītam anukramāt hutvā āhuti trayam paścāt maṇḍale ca śivam yajet devasya dakṣiṇe śiṣyam upaveśya uttarā mukham sa darbhe maṇḍale dadyāt homa śiṣṭam carum guruḥ śiṣyaḥ tad guruṇā dattam satkṛtya śiva pūrvakam apare maṇḍale dadyāt paṃcagavyam tathā guruḥ tṛtīye maṇḍale śiṣyam upaveśya yathā purā pradadyāt dantapavanam yathā śāstra ukta lakṣaṇam vācam niyamya ca āsīnaḥ śiṣyaḥ dantān viśodhayet praviśet deśika ādiṣṭaḥ prāṃjaliḥ śiva maṇḍalam tyaktam tat dantapavanam dṛśyate guruṇā yadi aśasta āśā mukhe tasmin guruḥ tad doṣa śāntaye śatam arddham tad arddham vā ājuhuyāt mūlamantrataḥ tatas śiṣyam samālabhya japitvā karṇayoḥ śivam devasya dakṣiṇe bhāge tam śiṣyam adhivāsayet ahata āstaraṇa āstīrṇe sa darbha śayane śuciḥ mantrite antar śivam dhyāyan śayīta prāc śirāḥ niśi śikhāyām baddha sūtrasya śikhayā tad śikhām guruḥ ābadhya ahata vastreṇa tam ācchādya ca varmaṇā kṛtvā astra japtaiḥ tad vāhye digīśānām balim haret iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śivadīkṣāvidhānavarṇanam nāma saptadaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ tatas snāna ādikam sarvam samāpya ācārya coditaḥ gacchet baddhāṃjaliḥ dhyāyan śiva maṇḍala pārśvatas atha pūjām vinā sarvam kṛtvā pūrva dine yathā baddha netreṇa śiṣyeṇa puṣpa avakiraṇe kṛte tam ca upanīya nirmālya maṇḍale asmin yathā purā pūjayet devam īśānam juhuyāt ca śiva anale śiṣyeṇa yadi duḥsvapnaḥ dṛṣṭaḥ tad doṣa śāntaye śatam arddham tad arddham vā juhuyāt mūlavidyayā tatas sūtram śikhā baddham laṃbayitvā yathā purā ādhāra pūjā prabhṛti yat nivṛtti kalā āśrayam vāgīśvarī pūjana antam kuryāt homa purassaram atha praṇamya vāgīśam nivṛtteḥ vyāpikām satīm prāpayet ca śiśoḥ prāptim yugapad sarva yoniṣu sūtra dehe atha śiṣyasya tāḍana prokṣaṇa ādikam kṛtvā ātmānam samādāya dvādaśānte nivedya ca tatas api ādāya mūlena mudrayā śāstra dṛṣṭayā jātyā ekayā ca mānuṣyāḥ yonayaḥ ca caturdaśa tāsu sarvāsu yugapad praveśāya śiśoḥ ddhiyā vāgīśānyām yathānyāyam śiṣya ātmānam niveśayet ārjavam bhoga niṣpattiḥ kuryāt prītim parām tathā niṣkṛti artham ca jāti āyuḥ bhoga saṃskāra siddhaye bhoktṛ tva viṣaya asaṃga malam tat kāya śodhanam kṛtvā evam eva śiṣyasya chindyāt pāśa trayam tatas hutvā pūrṇāhutim vahnau brahmāṇam pūjayet tatas hutvā āhuti trayam tasmai śiva ājñām anusaṃdiśet pitāmaha tvayā na asya yātuḥ śaivam param padam pratibandhaḥ vidhātavyaḥ śaiva ājñā eṣā garīyasī nivṛttyā śuddham uddhṛtya śiṣya ātmānam yathā purā niveśya ātmani sūtre ca vāgīśam pūjayet tatas kuryāt nivṛttaḥ saṃdhānam pratiṣṭhām kalayā saha saṃdhāne yugapad pūjām kṛtvā hutvā āhuti trayam śiṣya ātmanaḥ pratiṣṭhāyām praveśam tu atha bhāvayet tatas pratiṣṭhām āvāhya kṛtvā aśeṣam purā uditam tad vyāptim vyāpikām tasya vāgīśānīm ca bhāvayet pūrṇedu maṃḍala prakhyām kṛtvā śeṣam ca pūrvavat viṣṇoḥ visarjana ādyam ca kṛtvā śeṣam ca vidyayā pratiṣṭhām anusaṃdhāya tasyām ca api yathā purā kṛtvā anucintya tad vyāptim vāgīśām ca yathākramam dīpta agnau pūrṇahoma antam kṛtvā śeṣam ca pūrvavat nīlarudram upasthāpya tasmai pūjā ādikam tathā kṛtvā karma śiva ājñām ca dadyāt pūrva ukta vartmanā tapaḥ tam api ca udvāsya kṛtvā tasya atha śāntaye ca sva ātmanaḥ vyāpikām tadvat vāgīśīm ca yathā purā bāla arka sadṛśa ākārām bhāsayantīm diśaḥ daśa tatas śeṣam yathāpūrvam kṛtvā devam maheśvaram āvāhya ārādhya hutvā asmai śiva ājñām manasā diśet maheśvaram tathā utsṛjya kṛtvā anyām ca kalām imām śānti atītām kalām nītvā tad vyāptim avalokayet sva ātmanaḥ vyāpikām tadvat vāgīśām ca viciṃtayet nabhaḥ maṃḍala saṃkāśām pūrṇa antam ca api pūrvavat kṛtvā śeṣa vidhānena samabhyarcya sadāśivam tasmai samādiśet ājñām śaṃbhoḥ amita karmaṇaḥ tatra api ca yathāpūrvam śivam śirasi pūrvavat tatas śivena samprokṣya śiṣyam śirasi pūrvavat vilayam śānti atītāyāḥ śakti tattve atha ciṃtayet ṣaḍadhvanaḥ pare pāre sarva adhva vyāpinī parām koṭi sūrya pratīkāśam śaivīm śaktim ca cintayet tad agre śiṣyam ānīya śuddha sphaṭika nirmalam prakṣālya karttarīm paścāt śiva śāstra ukta mārgataḥ kuryāt tasya śikhā ācchedam saha sūtreṇa deśikaḥ vauṣaṭ antena mūlena punar prakṣālya kartarīm tatas snātam samācāntam kṛta svastyayanam śiśum praveśya maṃḍala abhyāsam praṇipatya ca daṃḍa vat pūjām kṛtvā yathānyāyam kriyā vaikalya śuddhaye upāṃśu uccāra yogena juhuyāt āhuti trayam tatra śaṃbhum samārādhya maṃḍala stham saha aṃbayā hutvā āhuti trayam paścāt prārthayet prāṃjaliḥ guruḥ kṛtā tasmāt param dhāma gamaya enam tava avyayam iti vijñāpya devāya nāḍī saṃdhāna pūrvakam pūrṇa antam pūrvavat kṛtvā tatas bhūtāni śodhayet sthira asthire tatas śuddhyai śīta uṣṇe ca tatas pade dhyāyet vyāptyā ekatā ākāre bhūta śodhana karmaṇi bhūtānām granthi vicchedam kṛtvā tyaktvā saha adhipaiḥ viśodhya asya tanum dagdhvā plāvayitvā sudhā kaṇaiḥ sthāpya ātmānam tatas kuryāt viśuddha adhva mayam vapuḥ tatra ādau śānti atītām tu vyāpikām svādhvanaḥ kalām śuddhām eva śiśoḥ mūrdhni nyaset śānti mukhe tathā vidyām gala ādinā abhyantam pratiṣṭhām tad adhas kramāt sva bījaiḥ sūtra mantram ca nyasyām gaiḥ tam śiva ātmakam buddhvā tam hṛdaya aṃbhoje devam āvāhya pūjayet āśāsya nitya sāṃnidhyam śiva svātmyam śiśau guruḥ śiva tejaḥ mayasya asya śiśoḥ āpādayet guṇān aṇima ādīn prasīda iti pradadyāt āhuti trayam sarvajña tām tathā tṛptim bodham ca ādi anta varjitam abhiṣiṃceta deveśam dhyāyan hṛdi yathākramam athā upaveśya tam śiṣyam śivam abhyarcya pūrvavat labdha anujñaḥ śivāt śaivīm vidyām asmai samādiśet oṃkāra pūrvikām tatra namaḥ anta gām śiva śakti yutām ca eva śakti vidyām ca tādṛśīm ṛṣim chandaḥ ca devam ca śivatām śivayoḥ tathā pūjām sa āvaraṇām śambhoḥ āsanāni ca sandiśet saha śiṣyaḥ guruḥ devam daṇḍa vat kṣiti maṃḍale praṇamya udvāsayet tasmāt maṃḍalāt pāvakāt api tatas sadasikāḥ sarve pūjyāḥ pūjā arhakāḥ kramāt sevyāḥ vitta anusāreṇa sadasyāḥ ca saha ṛtvijaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe ṣaḍadhvaśuddhyādikathanam nāma aṣṭādaśaḥ adhyāyaḥ saṃskāra mantra māhātmyam kathane sūcitam mayā saṃpūjya maṃḍale devam sthāpya kumbhe ca pūrvavat hutvā śiṣyam an uṣṇīṣam prāpayet bhuvi maṃḍale pūrva antam pūrvavat kṛtvā hutvā āhuti śatam tathā sandīpya ca yathāpūrvam kṛtvā pūrva uditam kramāt tatra vidyā upadeśa antam kṛtvā vistaraśaḥ kramāt puṣpa ambunā śiśoḥ pāṇau vidyām śaivīm samarpayet iti utvā devam abhyarcya labdha anujñaḥ śivāt guruḥ sādhanam śiva yogam ca sādhakāya samādiśet tat śrutvā guru saṃdeśam kramaśas mantra sādhakaḥ puratas caraṇīya tvāt viniyoga ākhya karmaṇaḥ tat iha anyatra śubha dam bhavet śubhe ahani śubhe deśe kāle vā doṣa varjite śukla danta nakhaḥ snātaḥ kṛta pūrvāhṇika kriyaḥ sa uṣṇīṣaḥ sa uttarāsaṃgaḥ sarva śukla samāhitaḥ sukhena abhyasta pūrveṇa tu āsanena kṛta āsanaḥ tanum kṛtvā ātmanaḥ śaivīm śiva śāstra ukta vartmanā praṇipatya ca tam devam prāpta anujñaḥ ca tad mukhāt koṭi vāram tad arddham vā tad arddham vā japet śivam lakṣa viṃśatikam vā api daśa lakṣam atha api vā tatas ca pāyasā kṣāra lavaṇa eka mita aśanaḥ ahiṃsakaḥ kṣamī śāntaḥ dāntaḥ ca eva sadā bhavet alābhe pāyasasya aśnan phala mūla ādikāni vā vihitāni śivena eva viśiṣṭāni uttarottaram śākam payaḥ dadhi ghṛtam mūlam phalam atha udakam sādhane asmin viśeṣeṇa nityam bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ snāyāt nadīnada utthena prokṣayet vā atha śaktitas ittham bhaktyā śivam śaivaḥ yaḥ sādhayati sādhakaḥ iha vidyām śriyam saukhyam labdhvā muktim ca vindati dhṛta tripuṃḍra rudra akṣaḥ vidyām pañcākṣarīm japet iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṃḍe sādhakasaṃskāramantramāhātmyam nāma ekonaviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ atha evam saṃskṛtam śiṣyam kṛta pāśupata vratam ācārya tve abhiṣiṃceta tad yoga tvena ca anyathā nivṛttim puratas nyasya pratiṣṭhām paścime ghaṭe vidyām dakṣiṇatas śāntim uttare madhyatas parām kṛtvā rakṣā ādikam tatra baddhvā mudrām ca dhainavīm abhimantrya ghaṭān hutvā pūrṇa antam ca yathā purā praveśya maṃḍale śiṣyam an uṣṇīṣam ca deśikaḥ tarpaṇa ādyam tu mantrāṇām kuryāt pūrva avasānakam tatas saṃpūjya deveśam anujñāpya ca pūrvavat abhiṣekāya tam śiṣyam āsanam tu adhirohayet sakalīkṛtya tam paścāt kalā paṃcaka rūpiṇam nyasta mantra tanum baddhvā śivam śiṣyam samarpayet tatas nivṛtti kuṃbha ādi ghaṭān uddhṛtya vai kramāt śivahastam samarpya atha śiśoḥ śirasi deśikaḥ śiva bhāva samāpannaḥ śiva ācāryam tam ādiśet athā alaṃkṛtya tam devam ārādhya śiva maṇḍale punar sampūjya deveśam praṇamya bhuvi daṃḍa vat śirasi aṃjalim ādhāya śivam vijñāpayet guruḥ anugṛhya tvayā deva divyā ājñā asmai pradīyatām evam vijñāpya śiṣyeṇa saha bhūyas praṇamya ca śivam śiva āgamam divyam pūjayet śiva vat guruḥ punar śivam anujñāpya śiva jñānasya pustakam ubhābhyām atha pāṇibhyām dadyāt śiṣyāya deśikaḥ sa tām mūrdhni samādhāya vidyām vidyā āsana upari ācārya padavīm prāptaḥ rājyam ca api yatas arhati atha anuśāsanam kuryāt pūrvaiḥ ācaritam yathā yathā ca śiva śāstra uktam yathā lokeṣu pūjyate śiṣyān parikṣya yatnena śiva śāstra ukta lakṣaṇaiḥ saṃskṛtya ca śiva jñānam tebhyaḥ dadyāt ca deśikaḥ evam sarvam anāyāsam śaucam kṣāntim dayām tathā aspṛhām api asūyām ca yatnena ca vibhāvayet ittham ādiśya tam śiṣyam śivam udvāsya maṃḍalāt śiva kuṃbha anala ādīn ca sadasyān api pūjayet yugapad vā atha saṃskārān kurvīta sa gaṇaḥ guruḥ kṛtvā samaya saṃskāram abhiṣekam vinā akhilam samabhyarcya śivam bhūyas kṛtvā ca adhva viśodhanam hutvā santarpya saṃdīpya āśāsya ca īśvaram samarpya mantram śiṣyasya pāṇau śeṣam samāpayet athavā mantra saṃskāram anuciṃtya akhilam kramāt adhva śuddhim guruḥ kuryāt abhiṣeka avasānikam tatra yaḥ śānti atīta ādi kalāsu vihitaḥ vidhiḥ śivena śānti atīta adhvā vyāptaḥ tad aparaḥ paraḥ vidyayā pariśiṣṭaḥ adhvā hi ātmanā nikhilaḥ kramāt durlabham śāṃbhavam matvā mantra mūlam manīṣiṇaḥ śāktam śaṃsīta saṃskāram śiva śāstra artha pāragāḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe viśeṣādisaṃskṛtiḥ nāma viṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca bhagavan śrotum icchāmi śiva āśrama niṣeviṇām prātar utthāya śayanāt dhyātvā devam saha ambayā abādhe vijane deśe kuryāt āvaśyakam tatas kṛtvā śaucam vidhānena dantadhāvanam ācaret alābhe dantakāṣṭhānām aṣṭamī ādi dineṣu ca nyāsam prakurvīta śivaśāstre yathā uditam vibhajya bāhu netra āsya lalāṭe ghrāṇayoḥ api kaṭhyoḥ pāṇyoḥ gulphayoḥ ca yat vā paṃcāṃga vartmanā haṃsa nyāsam imam kṛtvā nyaset pañcākṣarīm tatas yathā yajña ratāḥ kecid tapaḥ yajña ratāḥ pare japa yajña ratāḥ ca anye dhyāna yajña ratāḥ tathā jñāna yajña ratāḥ ca anye viśiṣṭāḥ ca uttarottaram krama yajñaḥ dvidhā proktaḥ kāma a kāma vibhedataḥ kāmān kāmī tatas bhuktvā kāma āsaktaḥ punar bhavet japa dhyāna rataḥ bhūtvā jāyate bhuvi mānavaḥ tasmāt muktaḥ śiva ājñaptaḥ karma yajñaḥ api dehinām akāmaḥ kāma saṃyuktaḥ bandhāya eva bhaviṣyati tasmāt pañcasu yajñeṣu dhyāna jñāna paraḥ bhavet dhyāna yajña ratāḥ tasmāt devān pāṣāṇa mṛṇmayān jñānāt dhyānam bhavet dhyānāt jñānam bhūyas pravartate dvādaśānte tathā mūrdhni lalāṭe bhrū yuga antare nāsā agre vā tathā āsye vā kandhare hṛdaye tathā nābhau vā śāśvata sthāne śraddhā viddhena cetasā bahis yāga upacāreṇa devam devīm ca pūjayet athavā pūjayet nityam liṃge vā kṛtake api vā vahnau vā sthaṇḍile vā atha bhaktyā vitta anusārataḥ antaryāga rataḥ pūjām bahis kurvīta vā na vā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śivaśāstroktanityanaimittikakarmavarṇanam nāma dvāviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śivaśāstre śivena eva śivāyai kathitasya tu aṃgam abhyantaram yāgam agni kārya avasānakam vidhāya vā na vā paścāt bahis yāgam samācaret dakṣiṇe ca uttare ca eva nandīśam suyaśam tathā ārādhya manasā samyak āsanam kalpayet budhaḥ ārādhana ādikaiḥ yuktaḥ siṃha yoga āsana ādikam rakta āsya pāṇi caraṇam kunda candra smita ānanam vara da abhaya hastam ca mṛga ṭaṃka dharam haram bhujaṃga hāra valayam cāru nīla gala antaram tatas saṃcintayet tasya vāma bhāge maheśvarīm praphulla utpala patra ābhām vistīrṇa āyata locanām tanu madhyām pṛthu śroṇīm pīta sūkṣma vara ambarām vicitra puṣpa saṃkīrṇa keśapāśa upaśobhitām sarvatas hema aravindam vilasat dadhānām dakṣiṇe kare daṃḍa vat ca aparam haste nyasya āsīnām mahā āsane pāśa vicchedikām sākṣāt saccidānanda rūpiṇīm evam devam ca devīm ca dhyātvā āsana vare śubhe śaivīm sadāśiva ākhyām vā tathā māheśvarīm parām ṣaḍviṃśaka abhidhānām vā śrīkaṃṭha ākhyām atha api vā mantra nyāsa ādikām ca api kṛtvā svasyām tanau yathā asyām mūrtau mūrtimantam śivam sat asataḥ param dhyātvā bāhya krameṇa eva pūjām nirvarttayet dhiyā samidh ājya ādibhiḥ paścāt nābhau homam ca bhāvayet bhrū madhye ca śivam dhyāyet śuddha dīpa śikhā ākṛtim atha ciṃtā mayam sarvam samāpya ārādhana kramam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe pūjāvidhānavyākhyānavarṇanam nāma trayoviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ prokṣayet mūlamantreṇa pūjā sthānam viśuddhaye astreṇa utsārya vai vighnān avaguṇṭhya ca varmaṇā tatra darbhān paristīrya kṣālayet prokṣaṇa ādibhiḥ tathā eva ācamanīyasya pātram ca iti catuṣṭayam prakṣālya prokṣya vīkṣya atha kṣipet teṣu jalam śivam puṇya dravyāṇi sarvāṇi yathālābham vinikṣipet ratnāni rajatam hema gandha puṣpa akṣata ādayaḥ phala pallava darbhān ca puṇya dravyāṇi anekadhā snāna udake sugandha ādi pānīye ca viśeṣataḥ śītalāni kusuma ādīni nikṣipet jāti kaṃkola karpūra bahumūla tamālakān kṣipet ācamanīye ca cūrṇayitvā viśeṣataḥ elām pātreṣu sarveṣu karpūram candanam tathā sarvatra mantram vinyasya varmaṇā āveṣṭya bāhyatas paścāt astreṇa saṃrakṣya dhenumudrām pradarśayet pūjā dravyāṇi sarvāṇi prokṣaṇī pātra vāriṇā pātrāṇām prokṣaṇīm ekām alābhe sarva karmasu tatas vināyakam devam bhakṣya bhojya ādibhiḥ kramāt pūjayitvā vidhānena dvāra pārśve atha dakṣiṇe antaḥpura adhipam sākṣāt nandinam samyak arcayet dīpta śūla mṛgī ṭaṃka tigma vetra dharam prabhum uttare dvāra pārśvasya bhāryyām ca marutām sutām aiśānyām caṇḍam ārādhya nirmālyam tasya dāpayet kalpayet āsanam paścāt ādhāra ādi yathākramam ādhāra śaktim kalyāṇīm śyāmām dhyāyet adhas bhuvi tasyāḥ purastāt utkaṃṭham anantam kuṇḍala ākṛtim dhavalam paṃca phaṇinam lelihānam iva ambaram tasya upari āsanam bhadram kaṇṭhī rava catuṣpadam āgneya ādi śveta rakta pīta śyāmāni varṇataḥ adharma ādīni pūrva ādīni uttara antāni anukramāt rājāvarta maṇi prakhyān nyasya gātrāṇi bhāvayet aṣṭa patrāṇi tasya āhuḥ aṇima ādi guṇa aṣṭakam kesarāṇi ca vāma ādyāḥ rudra vāma ādi śaktibhiḥ bījāni api ca tāḥ eva kandaḥ ca śiva dharma ātmā karṇikā ante tri maṇḍale sarva āsana upari sukham vicitra āstaraṇa āstṛtam namaskāram ca kurvīta badhvāḥ mudrāḥ pṛthak pṛthak dhūpam dīpam ca tāṃbūlam dattvā atha svāpayet śivau sakalīkṛtya mūlena brahma ābhiḥ ca aparaiḥ tathā bhaktānām aprayatnena dṛśyam īśvaram avyayam pañcagavya ghṛta kṣīra dadhi madhu ādi pūrvakaiḥ mūlaiḥ phalānām sāraiḥ ca tila sarṣapa saktubhiḥ bījaiḥ yava ādibhiḥ śastaiḥ cūrṇaiḥ māṣa ādi saṃbhavaiḥ saṃsnāpya ālipya piṣṭa ādyaiḥ snāpayet uṣṇa vāribhiḥ tatas saṃśodhya salilaiḥ liṃgam beram atha api vā asaṃbhave tu dravyāṇām yathā saṃbhava saṃbhṛtaiḥ kalaśena atha śaṃkhena varddhanyā pāṇinā tathā liṃga sūkta ādi sūktaiḥ ca śirasā ātharvaṇena ca ṛgbhiḥ ca sāmabhiḥ śaivaiḥ brahmabhiḥ ca api paṃcabhiḥ yathā devasya devyāḥ ca kuryāt snāna ādikam tathā na tu kaścid viśeṣaḥ asti tatra tau sadṛśau yatas devyaiḥ praścāt prakurvīta devadevasya śāsanāt tatra tatra upacārāṇām liṃge vā anyatra vā kvacid kṛtvā abhiṣekam liṃgasya śucinā ca sugandhinā saṃmṛjya vāsasā dadyāt aṃbaram ca upavītakam punar ca ācamanīyam ca mukha vāsam tatas param mukuṭam ca śubham bhadram sarva ratnaiḥ alaṃkṛtam vyajane cāmare chatram tālavṛṃtam ca darpaṇam dattvā nīrājanam kuryāt sarva maṃgala nisvanaiḥ gīta nṛtya ādibhiḥ ca eva jaya śabda samanvitaḥ haime ca rājate tāmre pātre vā mṛd maye śubhe padmakaiḥ śobhitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ bījaiḥ dadhi akṣata ādibhiḥ triśūla śaṃkha yugma abja nandyāvartaiḥ karīṣa jaiḥ aṣṭau pradīpān paritas vidhāya ekam tu madhyame teṣu vāmā ādikāḥ cintyāḥ pūjyāḥ ca nava śaktayaḥ kavacena samācchādya saṃrakṣya astreṇa sarvatas dhenumudrām ca saṃdarśya pāṇibhyām pātram uddharet athavā āropayet pātre paṃca dīpān yathākramam tris pradakṣiṇa yogena bhrāmayet mūlavidyayā kṛtvā puṣpa aṃjalim paścāt upahārān nivedayet pānīyam ca tatas dadyāt dattvā vā ācamanam punar prokṣayet prokṣaṇīyāni gāna nāṭyāni kārayet liṃga ādau śivayoḥ cintām kṛtvā śakti ajapet śivam pradakṣiṇam praṇāmam ca stavam ca ātma samarpaṇam vijñāpanam ca kāryāṇām kuryāt vinaya pūrvakam arghyam puṣpa aṃjalim dattvā baddhvā mudrām yathāvidhi paścāt kṣamāpayet devam udvāsya ātmani ciṃtayet pādya ādi mukha vāsa antam arghya ādyam ca ati saṃkaṭe puṣpa vikṣepa mātram vā kuryāt bhāva purassaram a saṃpūjya na bhuñjīta śivam ā prāṇasaṃcarāt yadi pāpaḥ tu bhuṃjīta svairam na niṣkṛtiḥ paredyus śaktitas dattvā suvarṇa ādyam śivāya ca śiva bhaktāya vā kṛtvā mahā pūjām śuciḥ bhavet iti śrī śivamahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śāstroktaśivapūjanavarṇanam nāma caturviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ an uktam ca atra pūjāyāḥ kamala upabhayāt iva haviḥ nivedanāt pūrvam dīpa dānāt anantaram kuryāt āvaraṇa abhyarcām prāpte nīrājane tatra īśāna ādi sadya antam rudra ādi astra antam eva ca śivasya vā śivāyāḥ ca prathama āvaraṇe japet aiśānyām pūrva bhāge ca dakṣiṇe ca uttare tathā paścime ca tathā āgneyyām aiśānyām nairṛte tathā vāyavyām punar aiśānyām catur dikṣu tatas param garbha āvaraṇam ākhyātam mantra saṃghātam eva vā tad bahis pūrvatas śakram yamam dakṣiṇatas yajet varuṇam vāruṇe bhāge dhanadam ca uttare budhaḥ īśa maiśe analam svīye nairṛte nirṛtim yajet mārute mārutam viṣṇum nairṛte vidhim aiśvare bahis padmasya vajra ādyāni abja antāni āyudhāni api prasiddha rūpāṇi āśāsu lokeśānām kramāt yajet devam devīm ca saṃprekṣya sarva āvaraṇa devatāḥ baddha aṃjali puṭāḥ dhyeyāḥ samāsīnāḥ yathāsukham sarva āvaraṇa devānām sva abhidhānaiḥ namaḥ yutaiḥ puṣpaiḥ saṃpūjanam kuryāt natvā sarvān yathākramam garbha āvaraṇam eva api yajet sva āvaraṇena vā haviḥ ca ṣaḍvidham deyam śuddham mudga annam eva ca eteṣu ekam anekam vā nānā vyaṃjana saṃyutam bhakṣyāṇi apūpa mukhyāni svādumanti phalāni ca raktacandana puṣpa āḍhyam pānīyam ca ati śītalam mṛdu elā rasa aktam ca khaṇḍam pūga phalasya ca dalāni nāgavallyāḥ ca yuktāni khadira ādibhiḥ śailam eva sitam cūrṇam na ati rūkṣam na dūṣitam karpūram ca atha kaṃkolam jāti ādi ca navam śubham ālepanam candanam syāt mūlakāṣṭham rajomayam kastūrikā kuṃkumam ca rasaḥ mṛgamada ātmakaḥ puṣpāṇi surabhīṇi eva pavitrāṇi śubhāni ca nirgandhāni ugra gandhāni dūṣitāni uṣitāni ca svayam eva viśīrṇāni na deyāni śiva arcane vāsāṃsi ca mṛdūni eva tapanīya mayāni ca vidyut valaya kalpāni bhūṣaṇāni viśeṣataḥ sarvāṇi etāni karpūra niryāsa aguru candanaiḥ ādhūpitāni puṣpa oghaiḥ vāsitāni samantataḥ candana aguru karpūra kāṣṭha guggulu cūrṇikaiḥ nityam pradīpitāḥ dīpāḥ śastāḥ karpūra saṃyutāḥ kapilā sambhavam śambhoḥ iṣṭam snāne ca pānake mṛdu upadhāna yuktāni sūkṣma tūla mayāni ca uccāvacāni ramyāṇi śayanāni sukhāni ca nadyaḥ samudra gāminyāḥ naṭāt vā ambhaḥ samāhṛtam śītam ca vastra pūtam tat viśiṣṭam snāna pānayoḥ chatram śaśi nibham cāru muktā dāma virājitam cāmare ca site sūkṣme cāmīkara pariṣkṛte rājahaṃsa dvaya ākāre ratna daṃḍa upaśobhite samantāt ratna sañchannam sraj vairaiḥ ca api bhūṣitam āsva pṛṣṭha ādi deśeṣu ratna cāmīkara ācitaḥ kāhalāni ca ramyāṇi nānā nāda karāṇi ca suvarṇa nirmitāni eva mauktika alaṃkṛtāni ca samudra kalpa san nādāḥ kalpanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ bhāṃḍāni api ca ramyāṇi patrāṇi api ca kṛtsnaśas tad ādhārāṇi sarvāṇi sauvarṇāni eva sādhayet rāja āvasatha vat kalpyam śilpa śāstra ukta lakṣaṇam ucca prākāra saṃbhinnam bhūdhara ākāra gopuram tapta jāṃbūnada mayam ratna stambha śata āvṛtam muktā dāma vitāna āḍhyam vidruma dvāra toraṇam cāmīkara mayaiḥ divyaiḥ mukuṭaiḥ kumbha lakṣaṇaiḥ rājanya arha nivāsaiḥ ca rāja vīthi ādi śobhitaiḥ procchrita prāṃśu śikharaiḥ prāsādaiḥ ca samantataḥ atyanta alaṃkṛta prāntam antar āvaraṇaiḥ iva uttama strī sahasraiḥ ca nṛtya geya viśāradaiḥ rakṣitam rakṣibhiḥ vīraiḥ gaja vāji ratha anvitaiḥ aneka puṣpa vāṭībhiḥ anekaiḥ ca sarovaraiḥ śiva āśrama rataiḥ bhaktaiḥ śiva śāstra ukta lakṣaṇaiḥ śāntaiḥ smita mukhaiḥ sphītaiḥ sat ācāra parāyaṇaiḥ śaivaiḥ māheśvaraiḥ ca eva śrīmadbhiḥ sevita dvijaiḥ sthāne śilā maye dānte dārave ca iṣṭakā maye kevalam mṛd maye vā api puṇya araṇye atha vā girau nadyām devālaye anyatra deśe vā atha gṛhe śubhe āḍhyaḥ vā atha daridraḥ vā svakām śaktim avaṃcayan bhaktyā vitta anusāreṇa śivam uddiśya yat kṛtam guhyāt guhyataram kṛṣṇa sarvatra parameśvare dānam adhyayanam sarve bhāva artham na atra saṃśayaḥ māsa upavāsaiḥ ca anyaiḥ ca śiva bhaktasya kim punar abhaktāḥ mānavāḥ ca asmin loke giri guhāsu ca rājasam siddhi dam kuryuḥ karmiṇaḥ rajasā āvṛtāḥ asurāḥ rākṣasāḥ ca eva tamaḥ guṇa samanvitāḥ aihika artham yajanti īśam narāḥ ca anye tādṛśāḥ tāmasam rājasam vā api sāttvikam bhāvam eva ca āśritya bhaktyā pūjā ādyam kurvan bhadram samaśnute yatas pāpa arṇavāt trātum bhaktiḥ nauḥ iva nirmitā śivam prapannaḥ ced kṛṣṇa pūjyaḥ sarva sura asuraiḥ abhuktānām kvacid api phalam na asti yatas tatas vakṣyāmi ati rahasyam te śṛṇu kṛṣṇa vacaḥ mama iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe śivaśāstroktapūjanavarṇanam nāma paṃcaviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ saṃpūjya amantrakam bhaktyā śivam parama kāraṇam taiḥ taiḥ pāpaiḥ pramucyeta varṣaiḥ dvādaśabhiḥ kramāt bhaktaḥ ced na aparaḥ kaścid bhikṣā āhāraḥ jita indriyaḥ abbhakṣāḥ vāyubhakṣāḥ ca ye ca anye vrata karśitāḥ teṣām etaiḥ vrataiḥ na asti śiva loka samāgamaḥ bhaktyā paṃcākṣareṇa eva yaḥ śivam sakṛt arcayet tasmāt tapāṃsi yajñān ca sarve sarva sva dakṣiṇāḥ baddhaḥ vā api atha muktaḥ vā paścāt pañca akṣareṇa ced yaḥ sakṛt patitaḥ vā api mūḍhaḥ vā mucyate naraḥ ṣaḍakṣareṇa vā devam sūkta mantreṇa pūjayet alabdhāt labdhaḥ eva atra viśiṣṭaḥ na atra saṃśayaḥ tasmāt nityam śivam bhaktyā sūkta mantreṇa pūjayet sa ciram saṃsarati asmin saṃsāre duḥkha sāgare te vāñchanti sadā kālam harasya caraṇa ambujam tasmāt sarvam parityajya śiva eka āhita mānasaḥ tvaritam vyādhiḥ abhyeti tasmāt pūjyaḥ pinākadhṛk dvāra yāgam javanikām parivāra bali kriyām puṣpam dhūpam ca dīpam ca dadyāt annam jalaiḥ saha tatas dadyāt mahā pīṭhe tiṣṭhan balim udañc mukhaḥ tat sarvam sa avaśeṣam vā caṇḍāya vinivedayet hutvā ca vidhivat paścāt pūjā śeṣam samāpayet kṛtvā prayogam vidhivat yāvat mantram japam tatas nitya utsavam prakurvīta yathā uktam śiva śāsane astram pāśupatam divyam tatra āvāhya samarcayet śivasya āropyaḥ tat pātram dvijasya alaṃkṛtasya ca nyasta astra vapuṣā tena dīpta yaṣṭi dharasya ca nṛtya geya ādibhiḥ ca eva saha dīpa dhvaja ādibhiḥ mahāpīṭham samāvṛtya tris pradakṣiṇa yogataḥ punar praviṣṭaḥ dvāra sthaḥ yajamānaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ ādāya abhyantaram nītvā hi astram udvāsayet tatas pradakṣiṇa ādikam kṛtvā yathāpūrva uditam kramāt ādāya ca aṣṭa puṣpāṇi pūjām atha samāpayet iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe sāṃgopāṃgapūjāvidhānavarṇanam nāma ṣaḍviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ atha agni kāryam vakṣyāmi kuṇḍe vā sthaṃḍile api vā vedyām vā hi āyase pātre mṛd maye vā nave śubhe ādhāya agnim vidhānena saṃskṛtya ca tatas param kuṇḍam dvi hasta mānam vā hasta mātram atha api vā vṛttam vā catur asram vā kuryāt vedim ca maṇḍalam kuṇḍam vistāravat nimnam tad madhye aṣṭa dala ambujam aṃgulaiḥ kathyate sadbhiḥ caturviṃśatibhiḥ karaḥ mekhalānām trayam vā api dvayam ekam atha api vā yathāśobham prakurvīta ślakṣṇam iṣṭam mṛdā sthiram mekhalā madhyatas kuryāt paścime dakṣiṇe api vā śobhanām agnitaḥ kiṃcid nimnām unmīlikām śanais agreṇa kuṇḍa abhimukhīm kiṃcid utsṛjya mekhalām na utsedha niyamaḥ vedyāḥ sā mārdī vā atha saikatī maṃḍalam go śakṛt toyaiḥ mānam pātrasya na uditam kuṇḍam ca mṛd mayam vedim ālipet gomaya aṃbunā prakṣālya tāpayet pātram prokṣayet anyat aṃbhasā sva sūtra ukta prakāreṇa kuṇḍa ādau villikhet tatas arcana artham ca homa artham sarva dravyāṇi sādhayet prakṣālya kṣālanīyāni prokṣaṇyā prokṣya śodhayet maṇi jam kāṣṭha jam vā atha śrotriya āgāra sambhavam anyam vā abhyarhitam vahnim tatas sa ādhāram ānayet tris pradakṣiṇam āvṛtya kuṇḍa ādeḥ upari kramāt vahnibījam samuccārya tu ādadhīta agnim āsane yoni mārgeṇa vā tadvat ātmanaḥ saṃmukhena vā niyogaḥ pradeśa sarvam kuṃḍam kuryāt vicakṣaṇaḥ nirgamya pāvake bahye līnam biṃba ākṛti smaret śiva mūrtim samabhyarcya tatas dakṣiṇa pārśvataḥ nyasya mantram ghṛte mudrām darśayet dhenu saṃjñitām yajña dāru mayau vā api smārtau vā śilpa sammatau pāra ārṣa ṛcya sva sūtra ukta krameṇa śiva pūrvakaiḥ bhruṃ stum bruśrum krameṇa eva puṃḍrandram iti atas param bījāni sapta saptānām jihvānām anupūrvaśas tri śikhā madhyamā jihvā bahu rūpa samāhvayā raktā āgneyī nairṛtī ca kṛṣṇā anyā suprabhā matā atiriktā marujjihvā sva nāma anuguṇa prabhā sva bija anantaram vācyā svāhā antaḥ ca yathākramam sarpiṣā vā samidbhiḥ vā pariṣecanam ācaret evam kṛte śiva agniḥ syāt smaret tatra śiva āsanam tatra āvāhya yajet devam ardhanārīśvaram śivam dīpa antam pariṣicya atha samidh homam samācaret tāḥ pālāśyaḥ parāḥ vā api yājñiyāḥ dvādaśa aṃgulāḥ avakrāḥ na svayam śuṣkāḥ sa tvacaḥ nirvraṇāḥ samāḥ daśa aṃgulāḥ vā vihitāḥ kaniṣṭhā aṃguli saṃmitāḥ prādeśa mātrāḥ vā alābhe hotavyāḥ sakalāḥ api dūrvā patra sama ākārām catur aṃgulam āyatām dadyāt ājya āhutim paścāt annam akṣa pramāṇataḥ lājān tathā sarṣapān ca yavān ca eva tilān tathā sarpiṣā aktāni bhakṣyāṇi lehya coṣyāṇi sambhave daśa eva āhutayaḥ tatra pañca vā tritayam ca vā hotavyāḥ śaktitas dadyāt ekam eva atha vā āhutim śruveṇa ājyam samitya ādyā srucā aśeṣāt kareṇa vā tatra divyena hotavyam tīrthena ārṣeṇa vā tathā tatas homa viśiṣṭena ghṛtena āpūryya vai srucam nidhāya puṣpam tasya agre śruveṇa adhomukhena tām vauṣaṭ antena juhuyāt dhārām tu yava saṃmitām ittham pūrṇāhutim kṛtvā pariṣiṃcet ca pūrvavat tam api udvāsya vā nābhau yajet saṃdhāya nityaśas athavā vahnim ānīya śiva śāstra ukta vartmanā vāgīśī garbha saṃbhūtam saṃskṛtya vidhivat yajet anvādhānam punar kṛtvā paridhīn paridhāya ca pātrāṇi dvandva rūpeṇa nikṣipya iṣṭvā śivam tatas saṃśodhya prokṣaṇī pātram prokṣyatāni tat aṃbhasā praṇītā pātram aiśānyām vinyasya ā pūritam jalaiḥ ājya saṃskāra paryantam kṛtvā saṃśodhya sraj sruvau kṛtvā pṛthak pṛthak hutvā jātam agnim vicintayet raktam rakta ambara ālepam mālya bhūṣaṇa bhūṣitam tomaram tālavṛṃtam ca ghṛta pātram tathā itaraiḥ jātam dhyātvā evam ākāram jātakarma samācaret nāla apanayanam kṛtvā tatas saṃśodhya sūtakam śiva agni ruci nāmā asya kṛtvā āhuti purassaram aptoryāma avasāna antam kṛtvā saṃskāram asya tu ājya dhārā ādi homam ca kṛtvā sviṣṭakṛtam tatas brahma viṣṇu śiva īśānām lokeśānām tathā eva ca tad astrāṇām ca paritas kṛtvā pūjām yathākramam sādhayitvā ājya pūrvāṇi dravyāṇi punar eva ca kalpayitvā āsanam vahnau tatra āvāhya yathā purā saṃpūjya devam devīm ca tatas pūrṇa antam ācaret atha vā sva āśrama uktam tu vahni karma śiva arpaṇam buddhvā śiva āśramī kuryāt na ca tatra aparaḥ vidhiḥ kāleṣu ukteṣu na anyatra na ayogyebhyaḥ pradāpayet udvāsane kṛte yasmāt caṇḍa bhasma prajāpate agni kārye kṛte paścāt śiva śāstra ukta mārgataḥ vidyā kośam pratiṣṭhāpya yajet puṣpa ādibhiḥ kramāt vidyāyāḥ puratas kṛtvā guroḥ api ca maṇḍalam tatra āsana varam kṛtvā puṣpa ādyaiḥ gurum arcayet niveditam ca vā deve tat śeṣam ca ātma śuddhaye na tu tatra śivaḥ asmi iti buddhim kuryāt vicakṣaṇaḥ bhuktvā ācamya śivam dhyātvā hṛdaye mūlam uccaret kāla śeṣam nayet yogyaiḥ śiva śāstra kathā ādibhiḥ rātrau vyatīte pūrva aṃśe kṛtvā pūjām manoharām śivayoḥ śayanam tu ekam kalpayet ati śobhanam bhakṣya bhojya aṃbara ālepa puṣpa mālā ādikam tathā gṛhasthaḥ bhāryayā sārddham tad anye pratyūṣa samayam buddhvā mātrām ādyām udīrayet deśa kāla ucitam kṛtvā śauca ādyam api śaktitaḥ nirvartya śivayoḥ pūjām prārabheta purā uditam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe agnikāryavarṇanam nāma saptaviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ kartavyā mahatī pūjā hi adhikā vā api śaktitaḥ māsi māsi yathānyāyam brahmakūrcam prasādhya tu snāpayitvā śivam tena pibet śeṣam upoṣitaḥ brahmahatyā ādi doṣāṇām atīva mahatām api niṣkṛtiḥ brahmakūrcasya pānāt na anyā viśiṣyate pauṣe puṣya nakṣatre kuryāt nīrājanam vibhoḥ vaiśākhyām tu viśākhāyām kuryāt puṣpa mahālayam jyeṣṭhe mūla ākhya nakṣatre śītakumbham pradāpayet āṣāḍhe ca uttarāṣāḍhe pavitra āropaṇam tathā śrāvaṇe prākṛtāni āpi maṇḍalāni prakalpayet śraviṣṭhā ākhye tu nakṣatre prauṣṭhapadyām tatas param prokṣayet ca jala krīḍām pūrvāṣāḍhā āśraye dine āśvayujyām tatas dadyāt pāyasam ca navodanam kārtikyām kṛtikā yoge dadyāt dīpa sahasrakam mārgaśīrṣe tathā ārdrāyām ghṛtena snāpayet śivam aśaktaḥ teṣu kāleṣu kuryāt utsavam eva vā āsthānam vā mahā pūjām adhikam vā samarcanam āvṛtte api ca kalyāṇe praśasteṣu api karmasu daurmanasye durācāre duḥsvapne duṣṭa darśane snāna pūjā japa dhyāna homa dāna ādikāḥ kriyaḥ nirmita anuguṇāḥ kāryyāḥ puraścaraṇa pūrvikāḥ divyam śrīkaṃṭhanāthasya sthānam ādyam sa gacchati saṃprāpya vaiṣṇavam brāhmam rudra lokam viśeṣataḥ śrīkaṇṭhāt jñānam āsādya tasmāt śaiva puram vrajet saṃprāpya pauruṣam raudra sthānam adri indrajāpateḥ tatra api pūrva saṃskāra vaśena sa mahā dyutiḥ bhogān ca vividhān bhuktvā vidyeśvara padam vrajet aṇḍasya antar bahis vā atha sakṛt āvartate punar tatas labdhvā śiva jñānam parām bhaktim avāpya ca yaḥ ca atīva śive bhaktaḥ viṣaya āsakta citta vat eka āvṛttaḥ dvis āvṛttaḥ tris āvṛttaḥ nivartakaḥ tasmāt śiva āśritaḥ bhūtvā yena kena api hetunā saṃsāra kāraṇam yeṣām na praroḍhum alam bhavet prakṛti anuguṇam tasmāt vimṛśya etat aśeṣatas iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe nityanaimittikavidhivarṇanam nāma aṣṭāviṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca bhagavan tvad mukhāt eva śrutam śruti samam mayā sva āśritānām śiva proktam nityanaimittikam tathā aihika āmuṣmikam ca api tat ca pañcavidham punar kriyā mayam tathā bhinnam homa dāna arcana kramāt sarva śaktimatām eva na anyeṣām saphalam bhavet tasmāt kāmyāni karmāṇi kuryāt ājñā dharaḥ udvijaḥ śaivaiḥ māheśvaraiḥ ca eva kāryam antar bahis kramāt yathā tathā na bhidyante śaivāḥ māheśvarāḥ api śiva āśritāḥ hi te śaivāḥ jñāna yajña ratāḥ narāḥ māheśvarāḥ samākhyātā karma yajña ratāḥ bhuvi tasmāt ābhyantare kuryuḥ śaivāḥ māheśvarāḥ vahis supralipte mahī pṛṣṭhe darpaṇa udara saṃnibhe prācīm utpādayet pūrvam śāstra dṛṣṭena vartmanā eka hastam dvi hastam vā maṇḍalam parikalpayet ratna hema ādibhiḥ cūrṇaiḥ yathā saṃbhava saṃbhṛtaiḥ daleṣu siddhayaḥ kalpyāḥ kesareṣu sa śaktikāḥ rudrāḥ vāma ādayaḥ tu aṣṭau pūrva ādi dalataḥ kramāt karṇikāyām ca vairāgyam bījeṣu nava śaktayaḥ skande śiva ātmakaḥ dharmaḥ nāle jñānam śiva āśrayam paṃca āvaraṇa saṃyuktam pūjayet aṃbayā saha śārdūla carma vasanam kiñcid smita mukha aṃbujam tri locana aravinda āḍhyam kṛta bāla indu śekharam dakṣiṇam nīla jīmūta samāna rucira prabham bhrukuṭī kuṭilam ghoram rakta vṛtta īkṣaṇa trayam savilāsam trinayanam candra ābharaṇa śekharam dakṣiṇe śūla paraśu vajra khaḍga anala ujjvalam savye ca nāga nārāca ghaṇṭā pāśa aṃkuśa ujjvalam nivṛttyā a jānu saṃbaddha māna ābheḥ ca pratiṣṭhayā ākaṃṭham vidyayā tadvat ālalāṭam tu śāntayā tad ūrddhvam śānti atīta ākhya kalayā parayā tathā paṃca adhva vyāpinam sākṣāt kalā paṃcaka vigraham sadya pādam ca tad mūrtim aṣṭatriṃśat kalā mayam mātṛkā mayam īśānam paṃca brahma mayam tathā okāra ākhya mayam ca eva haṃsa śaktyā samanvitam tathā icchā ātmikayā śaktyā samārūḍha aṃka maṃḍalam jñāna ākhyayā dakṣiṇatas vāmatas ca kriyā ākhyayā tattva traya mayam sākṣāt vidyā mūrtim sadāśivam mūrti mūlena saṃkalpya sakalīkṛtya ca kramāt saṃpūjya ca yathānyāyam argha antam mūlavidyayā brahmabhiḥ ca ṣaḍaGgaiḥ ca tatas mātṛkayā saha śāntena vā tathā anyaiḥ ca veda mantraiḥ ca kṛtsnaśas pādya ādi mukha vāsa antam kṛtvā prasthāpanam vinā paṃca āvaraṇa pūjām tu hi ārabheta yathākramam iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe kāmyakarmavarṇanam nāma ekonatriṃśattamaḥ adhyāyaḥ tatra ādau śivayoḥ pārśve dakṣiṇe vāmatas kramāt tatas brahmāṇi paritas īśāna ādi yathākramam sa śaktikāni sadya antam prathama āvaraṇe yajet ṣaṣ aṃgāni api tatra eva hṛdaya ādīni anukramāt śivasya ca śivāyāḥ ca vāhneya ādi samarcayet tatra vāmā ādikān rudrān aṣṭau vāma ādi śaktibhiḥ arcayet vā na vā paścāt pūrva ādi paritas kramāt dvitīya āvaraṇam prītyā procyate śraddhayā śṛṇu sūkṣmam dakṣiṇa diś patre saha śaktyā samarcayet śrīkaṇṭham nairṛte patre tad śaktim tasya vāmatas tathā eva mārute patre śikhaṃḍīśam samarcayet aṣṭasu kramaśas dikṣu pūrva ādi paritas kramāt ugraḥ bhīmaḥ mahādevaḥ iti aṣṭau mūrtayaḥ kramāt anantaram tatas ca eva mahādeva ādayaḥ kramāt śaktibhiḥ saha saṃpūjyāḥ tatra ekādaśa mūrttayaḥ kapardī īśaḥ ca kathyante tathā ekādaśa śaktayaḥ tatra aṣṭau prathamam pūjyāḥ vāhneya ādi yathākramam devadevaḥ pūrva patre īśānam ca agni gocare tasmin āvaraṇe bhūyas vṛṣa indram puratas yajet nandinam dakṣiṇe tasya mahākālam tathā uttare śāstāram vahni diś patre mātR̥ dakṣiṇa diś dale gajāsyam nairṛte patre ṣaṇmukham vāruṇe punar jyeṣṭhām vāyudale gaurīm uttare caṃḍam aiśvare śāstṛ nandīśayoḥ madhye muni indram vṛṣabham yajet śāstṛ mātṛ samūhasya madhye bhṛṃgīśvaram tatas jyeṣṭhā kumārayoḥ madhye śriyam śiva pada arccitām jyeṣṭhā gaṇāmbayoḥ madhye mahāmoṭīm samarcayet gaṇa ambā caṇḍayoḥ madhye devīm durgām prapūjayet atra eva āvaraṇe bhūyas śiva anucara saṃhatim rudra prathama bhūta ākhyām vividhām ca sa śaktikām śivāyāḥ ca sakhī varggam japet dhyātvā samāhitaḥ evam tṛtīya āvaraṇe vitate pūjite sati bhānuḥ pūrva dale pūjyaḥ dakṣiṇe caturānanaḥ caturṇām api devānām pṛthak āvaraṇāni atha tasya aṃgāni ṣaḍ eva ādau dīptā ādyābhiḥ ca śaktibhiḥ amoghā vidyutā ca eva pūrva ādi paritas sthitāḥ dvitīya āvaraṇe pūjyāḥ catasraḥ mūrtayaḥ kramāt pūrva ādi uttara paryantāḥ śaktayaḥ ca tatas param ādityaḥ bhāskaraḥ bhānuḥ raviḥ ca iti anupūrvaśas bodhanī paścime bhāge āpyāyinī uttare punar samantataḥ yajet etān tṛtīya āvaraṇe kramāt athavā dvādaśa ādityāḥ dvitīya āvaraṇe yajet tṛtīya āvaraṇe ca eva rāśīn dvādaśa pūjayet sapta chandaḥ mayān ca eva vālakhilyān ca pūjayet evam tṛtīya āvaraṇe samabhyarcya divākaram brahmāṇam arcayet paścāt tribhiḥ āvaraṇaiḥ sahaḥ hiraṇyagarbham pūrvasyām virājam dakṣiṇe tatas kālaḥ jāti aṃjana prakhyaḥ puruṣaḥ sphaṭika upamaḥ triguṇaḥ rājasaḥ ca eva tāmasaḥ sāttvikaḥ tathā dvitīya āvaraṇe pūjyāḥ pūrva ādi paritas kramāt tṛtīya āvaraṇe paścāt arcayet ca prajāpatīn aṣṭau pūrvān ca pūrva ādau trīn prāk paścāt anukramāt dakṣaḥ ruciḥ bhṛguḥ ca eva marīciḥ ca tathā aṃgirāḥ vasiṣṭhaḥ ca iti vikhyātāḥ prajānām patayaḥ tu ime teṣām bhāryyāḥ ca taiḥ sārddham pūjanīyāḥ yathākramam prasūtiḥ ca tathā khyātiḥ sambhūtiḥ eva ca dhṛtiḥ smṛtiḥ kṣamā ca eva sannatiḥ ca anasūyakā devamātā arundhatī ca sarvāḥ khalu pativratāḥ prathama āvaraṇe vedān caturaḥ vā prapūjayet tṛtīya āvaraṇe paścāt dharmaśāstra purassarāḥ vaidikyaḥ nikhilāḥ vidyāḥ pūjyāḥ eva samantataḥ aṣṭadhā vā caturdhā vā kṛtvā pūjām samantataḥ dakṣiṇe paścime paścāt rudram sa āvaraṇam yajet tasya brahma ṣaṣ aṃgāni prathama āvaraṇam smṛtam dvitīya āvaraṇe ca eva vidyeśvara mayam tathā catasraḥ mūrtayaḥ tasya pūjyāḥ pūrva ādeḥ kramāt triguṇāḥ sakalaḥ devaḥ purastāt śiva saṃjñakaḥ rājasaḥ dakṣiṇe brahmā sṛṣṭi kṛt pūjyate bhavaḥ tāmasaḥ paścime ca agniḥ pūjyaḥ saṃhārakaḥ haraḥ sāttvikaḥ sukha kṛt saumye viṣṇuḥ viśvapatiḥ mṛḍaḥ evam paścima digbhāge śambhoḥ ṣaḍviṃśakam śivam vāsudevam puraskṛtvā prathama āvaraṇe yajet aniruddham dakṣiṇatas pradyumnam paścime tatas saumye saṃkarṣaṇam paścāt vyatyastau vā yajet imau rāmaḥ ca anyatamaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ bhavān aśvamukhaḥ api ca tṛtīya āvaraṇe cakruḥ pūrva bhāge samarcayet nārāyaṇa ākhyām yāmyestram kvacid avyāhatam yajet evam tri āvaraṇaiḥ sākṣāt viśva ākhyām paramam harim mahāviṣṇum sadā viṣṇum mūrtīkṛtya samarcayet ittham viṣṇoḥ catur vyūha kramāt mūrti catuṣṭayam gaṇāṃbikā ca vāyavye lakṣmīm raudre samarcayet evam bhānu ādi mūrttīnām tad śaktīnām anantaram pūjām vidhāya lokeśān tatra eva āvaraṇe yajet vāyum somam kuberam ca paścāt īśānam arcayet āyudhāni maheśasya paścāt bāṃhyam samarcayet śrīmat triśūlam aiśāne vajram māhendra diś mukhe nairṛte tu yajet khaḍgam pāśam vāruṇa gocare aṃkuśam mārute bhāge pinākam ca uttare yajet paścima abhimukham raudram kṣetrapālam samarcayet pañcama āvaraṇam ca eva sampūjya anantaram bahis tatas samantataḥ pūjyāḥ sarvāḥ vai devayonayaḥ khecarāḥ ṛṣayaḥ siddhāḥ daityāḥ yakṣāḥ ca rākṣasāḥ ananta ādyāḥ ca nāga indrāḥ nāgaiḥ tad tad kula udbhavaiḥ ḍākinī bhūta vetāla preta bhairava nāyakāḥ pātāla vāsinaḥ ca anye nānā yoni samudbhavāḥ nadyaḥ samudrāḥ girayaḥ kānanāni sarāṃsi ca paśavaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ vṛkṣāḥ kīṭa ādyāḥ kṣudra yonayaḥ narāḥ ca vividha ākārāḥ mṛgāḥ ca kṣudra yonayaḥ bhuvanāni antar aṇḍasya tatas brahmāṇḍa koṭayaḥ bahiraṃḍāni asaṃkhyāni bhuvanāni saha adhipaiḥ brahmāṃḍa dhārakāḥ rudrāḥ daśa dikṣu vyavasthitāḥ yat gauḍa yat ca māmeyam yat vā śāktam tatas param tat sarvam śivayoḥ pārśve buddhvā sāmānyatas yajet prītyā saṃprekṣamāṇāḥ ca devam devīm ca sarvadā ittham āvaraṇa abhyarcām kṛtvā a vikṣepa śāntaye su vyañjana samāyuktam śuddham cāru mahā carum dvātriṃśat āḍhakaiḥ mukhyam adhamam tu āḍhaka avaram tatas nivedya pānīyam tāṃbūlam ca upadaṃśakaiḥ nīrājana ādikam kṛtvā pūjā śeṣam samāpayet bhoga upayogya dravyāṇi viśiṣṭāni eva sādhayet śaṭhasya upekṣakasya api vyaṃgyam ca eva anutiṣṭhataḥ na phalanti eva karmāṇi kāmyāni iti satām kathā tasmāt samyak upekṣām ca tyaktvā sarva aṃga yogataḥ kuryāt kāmyāni karmāṇi phala siddhim yadi icchati ittham pūjām samāpya atha devam devīm praṇamya ca tatas stotram upāsya ante tu aṣṭottaraśata avarām vidyā pūjām guroḥ pūjām kṛtvā paścāt yathākramam yathodayam yathāśrāddham sadasyān api pūjayet tatas udvāsya deveśam sarvaiḥ āvaraṇaiḥ saha maṇḍalam gurave dadyāt yāga upakaraṇaiḥ saha śiva āśritebhyaḥ vā dadyāt sarvam eva anupūrvaśas samabhyarcya yathānyāyam sarva āvaraṇa devatāḥ aihikam vā phalam kiṃcid āmuṣmika phalam tu vā śreyaḥ rūpasya kṛtsnasya tat idam śreṣṭa sādhanam mahādevam samuddiśya kṛtam karma aṃśa bhedaiḥ anekaśas tau imau śivayoḥ pārśve nityam ittham mayā arcitau tayoḥ ājñām puraskṛtya prārthitam me prayacchatām mūrddha abhimāninī mūrtiḥ śivasya paramātmanaḥ śiva arcana ratam śāntam śānti śambhoḥ pāda arcane ratam prathamam śiva bījeṣu kalāsu ca catuṣkalam añjana ādi pratīkāśam aghoram ghora vigraham dvitīyam śivabījeṣu kalāsu aṣṭa kalā anvitam śaṃbhoḥ dakṣiṇa digbhāge śaktyā saha vikaraṇaḥ tathā sarva bhūtasya damanaḥ tādṛśāḥ ca aṣṭa śaktayaḥ atha anantaḥ ca sūkṣmaḥ ca śivaḥ ca api eka netrakaḥ eka rudra ākhya martiḥ ca śrīkaṇṭhaḥ ca śikhaṃḍakaḥ tathā aṣṭau śaktayaḥ teṣām dvitīya āvaraṇe te me kāmam prayacchantu śivayoḥ eva śāsanāt bhava ādyāḥ mūrtayaḥ ca aṣṭau tāsām api ca śaktayaḥ mahādeva ādayaḥ ca anye tathā ekādaśa mūrtayaḥ śaktibhiḥ sahitāḥ sarve tṛtīya āvaraṇe sthitāḥ satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām diśantu phalam īpsitam sita abhra śikhara ākāraḥ kakudā pariśobhitaḥ rakta āsya śṛṃga caraṇau rakta prāya vilocanaḥ praśasta lakṣaṇaḥ śrīmān prajvalat maṇi bhūṣaṇaḥ śiva priyaḥ śiva āsaktaḥ śivayoḥ dhvaja vāhanaḥ tathā tad caraṇa nyāsa pāvita apara vigrahaḥ tayoḥ ājñām puraskṛtya sa me kāmam prayacchatu śarvasya antaḥpura dvāri sārddham parijanaiḥ sthitaḥ sarveṣām śiva dharmāṇām adhyakṣa tve abhiṣecitaḥ śiva priyaḥ śiva āsaktaḥ śrīmat śūla vara āyudhaḥ śiva āśriteṣu saṃsaktaḥ tu anuraktaḥ ca taiḥ api satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām sa me kāmam prayacchatu mahādeva āśritānām tu nityam eva abhirakṣatu śiva priyaḥ śiva āsaktaḥ śivayoḥ arcakaḥ sadā satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām sa me diśatu kāṃkṣitam tayoḥ ājñām puraskṛtya sa me kāmam prayacchatu matta mātaṃga vadanaḥ gaṃgā umā śaṃkara ātmajaḥ śiva jñāna mada udbhinnaḥ tridaśānām avighna kṛt vighna kṛt ca asura ādīnām vighneśaḥ śiva bhāvitaḥ satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām sa me diśatu kāṃkṣitam ṣaṇmukhaḥ śiva sambhūtaḥ dala īkṣaṇaḥ kumāraḥ sukumārāṇām rūpa udāharaṇam mahat satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām sa me diśatu kāṃkṣitam tayoḥ ājñām puraskṛtya sā me diśatu kāṃkṣitam trailokya vanditā sākṣāt ulkā ākārā gaṇa aṃbikā jagat
