abhirakṣitā yajñasya ca śiraḥ hartā dakṣasya ca durātmanaḥ śivasya anucaraḥ śrīmān śiva śāsana pālakaḥ śivayoḥ śāsanāt eva sa me diśatu kāṃkṣitam śivayoḥ pūjane saktā sa me diśatu kāṃkṣitam viṣṇoḥ vakṣaḥ sthitā lakṣmīḥ śivayoḥ pūjane ratā śivayoḥ śāsanāt eva sā me diśatu kāṃkṣitam mahāmoṭī mahādevyāḥ pāda pūjā parāyaṇā tasyāḥ eva niyogena sā me diśatu kāṃkṣitam kauśikī siṃham ārūḍhā pārvatyāḥ paramā sutā viṣṇoḥ nidrā mahāmāyā mahā mahiṣa mardinī satkṛtya śāsanam mātuḥ sā me diśatu kāṃkṣitam bhūta ākhyāḥ ca mahā vīryyāḥ mahādeva sama prabhāḥ śiva priyatamāḥ nityam śiva lakṣaṇa lakṣitāḥ saumya ādhārāḥ tathā miśrāḥ ca antarāla dvaya ātmikāḥ satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām te me kāmam diśantu vai devyā priya sakhī vargaḥ devī lakṣaṇa lakṣitaḥ sahitaḥ rudra kanyābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ ca api anekaśas tṛtīya āvaraṇe śaṃbhoḥ bhaktyā nityam samarcitaḥ satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām sa me diśatu maṃgalam divākaraḥ maheśasya mūrtiḥ dīpti su maṃḍalaḥ nirguṇaḥ guṇa saṃkīrṇaḥ tathā eva guṇa kevalaḥ avikāra ātmakaḥ ca ādyaḥ ekaḥ sāmānya vikriyaḥ asādhāraṇa karmā ca sṛṣṭi sthiti laya kramāt evam tridhā caturddhā ca vibhaktāḥ paṃcadhā punar caturtha āvaraṇe śaṃbhoḥ pūjitaḥ ca anugaiḥ saha satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām sa me diśatu maṃgalam divākara ṣaṣ aṃgāni dīptā ādyāḥ ca aṣṭa śaktayaḥ ādityaḥ bhāskaraḥ bhānuḥ raviḥ ca iti anupūrvaśas arkaḥ brahmā tathā rudraḥ viṣṇuḥ ca āditya mūrtayaḥ vistara asutara abodhinī āpyāyinī aparāḥ punar uṣā prabhā tathā prājñā sandhyā ca iti api śaktayaḥ soma ādi ketu paryantāḥ grahāḥ ca śiva bhāvitāḥ śivayoḥ ājñayā anunnāḥ maṃgalam pradiśantu me athavā dvādaśa ādityāḥ tathā dvādaśa śaktayaḥ grāmaṇyaḥ ca tathā yakṣāḥ rākṣasāḥ ca asurāḥ tathā satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām maṃgalam pradiśantu me brahmā atha devadevasya mūrtiḥ bhū maṇḍala adhipaḥ catuḥṣaṣṭi guṇa aiśvaryaḥ buddhi tattve pratiṣṭhitaḥ nirguṇaḥ guṇa saṃkīrṇaḥ tathā eva guṇa kevalaḥ avikāra ātmakaḥ devaḥ tatas sādhāraṇaḥ puras asādhāraṇa karmā ca sṛṣṭi sthiti laya kramāt tridhā caturddhā ca vibhaktaḥ paṃcadhā punar caturtha āvaraṇe śaṃbho pūjitaḥ ca saha anugaiḥ satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām sa me diśatu maṃgalam hiraṇyagarbhaḥ loka īśaḥ virāj kālaḥ ca pūruṣaḥ prajānām patayaḥ ca eva dakṣa ādyāḥ brahma sūnavaḥ śiva ājñā vaśa gāḥ sarve diśantu mama maṃgalam catvāraḥ ca tathā vedāḥ sa itihāsa purāṇakāḥ dharma śāstrāṇi vidyābhiḥ vaidikībhiḥ samanvitāḥ paraspara viruddha arthāḥ śiva prakṛti pādakāḥ satkṛtya śivayoḥ ājñām maṃgalam pradiśantu me vāhneya maṇḍala adhīśaḥ pauruṣa aiśvaryavān prabhuḥ śiva abhimāna saṃpannaḥ nirguṇaḥ triguṇa ātmakaḥ kevalam sāttvikaḥ ca api rājasaḥ ca eva tāmasaḥ asādhāraṇa karmā ca sṛṣṭi ādi karaṇāt pṛthak janakaḥ tanayaḥ ca api viṣṇoḥ api niyāmakaḥ śivasya ājñām puraskṛtya sa me diśatu maṃgalam tasya brahma ṣaṣ aṃgāni vidyeśa antam tathā aṣṭakam catvāraḥ mūrti bhedāḥ ca śiva pūrvāḥ śiva arcakāḥ śivaḥ bhavaḥ haraḥ ca eva mṛḍaḥ ca eva tathā aparaḥ śivasya ājñām puraskṛtya maṃgalam pradiśantu me atha viṣṇuḥ maheśasya śivasya eva parā tanuḥ vāri tattva adhipaḥ sākṣāt avyakta pada saṃsthitaḥ asādhāraṇa karmā ca sṛṣṭi ādi karaṇāt pṛthak dakṣiṇa aṃga bhavena api sparddhamānaḥ svayaṃbhuvā ādyena brahmaṇā sākṣāt sṛṣṭaḥ sraṣṭā ca tasya tu aṃḍasya antar ekatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ atha vakṣyāmi śaivānām iha eva phala dam nṛṇām dṛṣṭa siddhi karam karma na anyathā phala dam yatas parīkṣya śakuna ādyaiḥ tadā ādau niṣkṛtim ācaret sa khalu aśraddha dhānaḥ syāt na aśraddhaḥ phalam ṛcchati yathā ukta kāriṇām puṃsām iha eva phala darśanāt rātrau haviṣyam aśnīyāt pāyasam vā phalāni vā hiṃsā ādi yat niṣiddham syāt na kuryāt manasā api tat sadā bhasma anuliptām gassu veṣaḥ ca śuciḥ bhavet ittham ācāravān bhūtvā sva anukūle śubhe ahani pūrva ukta lakṣaṇe deśe puṣpa dāma ādi alaṃkṛte ālipya śakṛtā bhūmim hasta māna avarām yathā tādṛśe svarṇa nirmāṇe kande samyak vidhānataḥ tatra aṇima ādikam sarvam saṃkalpya manasā punar ratna jam vā atha sauvarṇam sphaṭikam vā sa lakṣaṇam tatra māheśvarī kalpyā mūrtiḥ mūrtimataḥ prabhoḥ śārdūla carma vasanā kiṃcid vihasita ānanā atha vā aṣṭa bhujā ciṃtyā ciṃtakasya yathāruci tadā triśūla paraśu khaḍga vajrāṇi dakṣiṇe vāme pāśa aṃkuśau tadvat kheṭam nāgam ca bibhratī bāla arka sadṛśa prakhyā prativaktram tri locanā tasyāḥ pūrva mukham saumyam su ākāra sadṛśa prabham dakṣiṇam nīla jīmūta sadṛśam ghora darśanam paścimam pūrṇa candra ābham saumyam indu kalā dharam tad aṃka maṃḍala ārūḍhā śaktiḥ māheśvarī parā mahālakṣmīḥ iti khyātā śyāmā sarva manoharā mūrtim kṛtvā evam ākārām sakalīkṛtya ca kramāt paṃcāmṛtam ca pūrṇāni bījāni ca viśeṣataḥ purastāt maṇḍalam kṛtvā ratna cūrṇa ādi alaṃkṛtam karṇikāyām pravinyasyet īśāna kalaśam punar tatas vidyeśa kalaśān aṣṭau pūrva ādi vat kramāt tīrtha ambu pūritān kṛtvā sūtreṇa āveṣṭya pūrvavat dukūla ādyena vastreṇa samācchādya samantataḥ tatas kuśa udaka ādyāni svarṇa ratna udakāni api gandham puṣpa ādi dīpān ca pūjā karma samācaret pala avaraḥ syāt ālepaḥ ekādaśa pala uttaraḥ suvarṇa ratna puṣpāṇi śubhāni surabhīṇi ca nīlotpala ādi utpalāni bilva patrāṇi anekaśas kamalāni ca raktāni śvetāni api ca śaṃbhave kapilā ghṛta saṃsiddhāḥ dīpāḥ karpūra varti jāḥ paṃca brahma ṣaṣ aṃgāni pūjyāni āvaraṇāni ca naivedyaḥ payasā siddhaḥ sa guḍa ājyaḥ mahā caruḥ pāṭalā utpala padma ādyaiḥ pānīyam ca sugandhitam paṃca saugandhika upetam tāṃbūlam ca susaṃskṛtam suvarṇa ratna siddhāni bhūṣaṇāni viśeṣataḥ darśanīyāni deyāni gāna vādya ādibhiḥ saha eka avarā tri uttarā ca pūjā phala vaśāt iha daśa saṃkhyā varaḥ homaḥ pratidravyam śata uttaraḥ ghora rūpaḥ śivaḥ ciṃtyaḥ māraṇa uccāṭana ādiṣu śiva liṃge śiva agnau ca hi anyāsu pratimāsu ca ciṃtyaḥ saumya tanuḥ śaṃbhuḥ kārye śāntika pauṣṭike āyasau sruc sruvau kāryau māraṇa ādiṣu karmasu tad anyatra tu sauvarṇau śāntika ādyeṣu kṛtsnaśas samṛddhi kāmaḥ juhuyāt mahā dāridrya śāntaye jātī puṣpeṇa vaśyā arthī juhuyāt sa ghṛtena tu tāḍanam rudhireṇa syāt kharasya uṣṭrasya ca ubhayoḥ māraṇa uccāṭane kuryāt rohi bījaiḥ tila anvitaiḥ jvara upaśānti dam kṣīram saubhāgya phala dam tathā kṣīreṇa tandulaiḥ ca eva caruṇā kevalena vā śāntikam pauṣṭikam vā api saptabhiḥ samidh ādibhiḥ dravyaiḥ viśeṣataḥ home vaśyam ākarṣaṇam tathā samidhaḥ śānti kāryeṣu pālāśa khadira ādikāḥ karavīra arka jāḥ kraurye kaṇṭakinyaḥ ca vigrahe praśāntaḥ śāntikam kuryāt pauṣṭikam ca viśeṣataḥ atīva duravasthāyām pratīkāra antaram na ced manasā karmmaṇā vācā yaḥ api kaḥ api śiva āśritaḥ sva rāṣṭra patim uddiśya śivā śritam atha api vā sva rāṣṭra pālakam tasmāt śiva bhaktam ca kañcana na hiṃsyāt abhicāra ādyaiḥ yadi icchet sukham ātmanaḥ anyam kam api ca uddiśya kṛtvā vai māraṇa ādikam bāṇaliṃge api vā kuryāt nirdhanaḥ dhanavān api svayaṃbhūte atha vā liṃge ārṣake vaidike api vā kvacid aṃśe tu yaḥ śaktaḥ tu aśaktaḥ kvacid aṃśake tat sarvam gurave dadyāt dakṣiṇām ca tatas pṛthak yaḥ svayam sādhayet śaktyā guru ādi nirapekṣayā saḥ api evam ācaret atra na gṛhṇīyāt svayam punar svayam gṛhṇāti yaḥ lobhāt pūjā aṃga dravyam uttamam kāṃkṣitam na labhet mūḍhaḥ na atra kāryā vicāraṇā arcitam yat tu tat liṃgam gṛhṇīyāt vā navā svayam gṛhṇīyāt yadi tat nityam svayam vā anyaḥ api vā arcayet api śatrubhiḥ ākrāntaḥ vyādhibhiḥ vā api anekaśas mṛtyoḥ āsya gataḥ ca api mucyate nirapāyataḥ pūjāyate ati kṛpaṇaḥ riktaḥ vaiśravaṇāyate kāmāyate virūpaḥ api vṛddhaḥ api taruṇāyate viṣāyate yat amṛtam viṣam api amṛtāyate mahīdharāyate śvabhram sa ca śvabhrāyate giriḥ padmākarāyate vahniḥ saraḥ vaiśvānarāyate striyaḥ lakṣmīḥ sucaritāyate svairapreṣyāyate vāṇī kīrtiḥ tu gaṇikāyate svairācārāyate medhā vajrasūcīyate manaḥ mahāvātāyate śaktiḥ balam mattagajāyate śatru pakṣāyate sarvaḥ eva suhṛd janaḥ śatravaḥ kuṇapāyante jīvantaḥ api sa bāndhavāḥ āpannaḥ gata ariṣṭaḥ svayam khalu amṛtāyate aniśam kriyamāṇā api ratiḥ tu abhinavāyate anāgata ādikam sarvam karasthāmalakāyate iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe aihikasiddhikarmavarṇanam nāma dvātriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ nandīśvara mahākāla bhṛṃgīśa ādyaiḥ gaṇeśvaraiḥ pātāla vāsibhiḥ daityaiḥ śeṣa ādyaiḥ ca mahā uragaiḥ svam svam padam anuprāptam sarvaiḥ ayam anuṣṭhitaḥ gaṇeśaḥ ca gaṇeśa tvam anena vidhinā gataḥ sitacandana toyena liṃgam snāpya śivam śivām sarva upahāra saṃmiśram tatas liṃgam nivedayet iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe āmuṣmikakarmavidhivarṇanam nāma trayastriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ sā sarvā labhyeta sadyas liṃgabera pratiṣṭhayā tasmāt pratiṣṭhite liṃge bhavet sarvam patiṣṭhitam kim anyat iha vaktavyam pratiṣṭhām prati kāraṇam partiṣṭhitam śivena api liṃgam vaiśveśvaram yatas sthāpayet parameśasya liṃgam beram atha api vā śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca kim idam liṃgam ākhyātam katham liṃgī maheśvaraḥ avyaktam liṃgam ākhyātam triguṇa prabhava apyayam anādi anantam viśvasya yat upādānakāraṇam tat eva mūlaprakṛtiḥ māyā ca gagana ātmikā aśuddham ca eva śuddham yat śuddha aśuddham ca tat tridhā bhūtāni ca indriyaiḥ jātāḥ līyante atra śiva ājñayā atas eva śivaḥ liṃgaḥ liṃgam ājñāpayet yatas yatas na tat anājñātam kāryāya prabhavet svatas liṃgam ca śivayoḥ dehaḥ tābhyām yasmāt adhiṣṭhitam śaktiḥ rājñām yat ādatte prasūte tat carācaram yena ādau mohitau syātām brahma nārāyaṇau api vāri śayyā gataḥ viṣṇuḥ suṣvāpa an ākulaḥ sukham dadarśa puṇḍarīkākṣam svapantam tam an ākulam māyayā mohitaḥ śambhoḥ viṣṇum āha pitāmahaḥ sa tu hasta prahāreṇa tīvreṇa abhihataḥ kṣaṇāt iti viṣṇu vacaḥ śrutvā prabhu tva guṇa sūcakam vatsa iti mām kutas brūṣe guruḥ śiṣyam iva ātmanaḥ mām na jānāsi kim nātham prapaṃcaḥ yasya me kṛtiḥ tridhā ātmānam vibhajya idam sṛṣṭvā atha paripālyate saṃharāmi name kaścid sraṣṭā jagati vidyate iti ukte sati saḥ api āha brahmāṇam viṣṇuḥ avyayaḥ bhavān api mama eva aṃgāt avatīrṇaḥ purā avyayāt mad niyogāt tvam ātmānam tridhā kṛtvā jagattrayam tava api janakam sākṣāt mām evam avamanyase mad prasādāt iyam bhrāntiḥ apaiṣyati tava acirāt tasya jvālā sahasreṇa brahma viṣṇū vimohitau visṛjya yuddham kim tu etat iti aciṃtayatām tadā tadā samudyatau syātām tasya ādi antam parīkṣitum tatra haṃsa ākṛtiḥ brahmā viśvatas pakṣa saṃyutaḥ tāvatkālam gataḥ ca ūrddhvam tasya antam jñātum icchayā śrānta utyantam a dṛṣṭvā antam pāpa tā adhas pitāmahaḥ samāgatau atha anyonyam vismaya smera vīkṣaṇau māyayā mohitau śaṃbhoḥ kṛtyākṛtyam na jagmatuḥ pṛṣṭhatas pārśvatas tasya ca agratas ca sthitau ubhau praṇipatya kim ātmā idam iti aciṃtayatām tadā iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe harividhimohavarṇanam nāma catustriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ a u mā iti tri mātrābhiḥ parastāt ca arddha mātrayā tatra ākāraḥ śritaḥ bhāge jvalat liṃgasya dakṣiṇe arddha mātrā ātmakaḥ nādaḥ śrūyate liṃga mūrddhani veda ātmanā tadā avyaktaḥ praṇavaḥ vikṛtim gataḥ makāraḥ sāma saṃjātaḥ nādaḥ tu ātharvaṇī śrutiḥ ṛgayam sthāpayāmāsa samāsāt tu artham ātmanaḥ rajaḥ guṇeṣu brahmāṇam mūrtiṣu ādyam kriyāsu api sṛṣṭim lokeṣu pṛthivīm tattveṣu ātmānam avyayam liṃga bhāgeṣu adhobhāgam bīja ākhyam kāraṇa traye catuḥṣaṣṭi guṇa aiśvaryam bauddham yat aṇima ādiṣu atha upasthāpayāmāsa sva artham daśavidham yajuḥ sattvam guṇeṣu viṣṇum ca mūrtiṣu ādyam kriyāsu api sthitim lokeṣu antarikṣam vidyām tattveṣu ca triṣu kalā adhvasu pratiṣṭhām ca vāmam brahmasu paṃcasu madhyam tu liṃga bhāgeṣu yonim ca triṣu hetuṣu prākṛtam ca yathā aiśvaryam tasmāt viśvam yajuḥ mayam tatā upasthāpayāmāsa sāma artham daśadhā ātmanaḥ tamaḥ guṇeṣu atho rudram mūrtiṣu ādyam kriyāsu ca saṃhṛtim triṣu lokeṣu tattveṣu śivam uttamam liṃga bhāgeṣu pīṭha ūrddhvam bījinam kāraṇa traye pauruṣam ca tathā aiśvaryam ittham sāmnā tatam jagat tatas maheśvaram sākṣāt mūrtiṣu api sadāśivam kriyāsu niṣkriyasya api śivasya paramātmanaḥ śāntim ca śāntyatītām ca vyāpikām kalāsu api tatpūruṣam tathā īśānam brahma brahmasu paṃcasu yatra āvāhya samārādhyaḥ kevalaḥ niṣkalaḥ śivaḥ kāraṇeṣu traya atītāt māyā vikṣobha kāraṇāt anantāt śuddhavidyāyāḥ parastāt ca maheśvarāt sarva vidyeśvara adhīśāt na parāt ca sadāśivāt paṃcavaktrāt daśa bhujāt sākṣāt sakala niṣkalāt tatas parāt niśādhīśān nāda ākhyāt ca tatas parāt tatas parāt suṣumnā īśāt brahmarandhra īśvarāt api paramam kāraṇam sākṣāt svayam niṣkāraṇam śivam kāraṇānām ca dhātāram dhyātārām dhyeyam avyayam sarva aiśvaryeṇa saṃpannam sarva īśvaram anīśvaram aiśvaryāt ca api māyā iyāt aśuddhāt mānuṣa ādikāt aparāt ca parāt tyājyāt adhiśuddha adhva gocarāt tad parāt śuddhavidyā ādyāt unmanā antāt parātparāt apāram apara adhīnam nirasta atiśayam sthiram yasmāt garīyasī tasmāt viśvam vyāptam atharvaṇāt ṛgvedaḥ punar āha idam jāgrat rūpam mayā ucyate bhogya ātmanā pariṇatāḥ vidyā vedyāḥ yatas mayi sāma ca āha suṣupti ākhyam evam sarvam mayā ucyate mama arthena śivena idam tāmasena abhidhīyate atharvā āha turāya ākhyam turīya atītam eva ca adhva ātmakam tu tritayam śiva vidyā ātma saṃjñitam tat traiguṇyam trayī sādhyam saṃśodhyam ca pada eṣiṇā adhva atītam turīya ākhyam nirvāṇam paramam padam dvayoḥ pramāpakaḥ nādaḥ nadāntaḥ ca mada ātmakaḥ tasmāt mama artha svātantryāt pradhānaḥ parameśvaraḥ śivaḥ hi praṇavaḥ hi eṣa praṇavaḥ hi śivaḥ smṛtaḥ eka akṣarāt akāra ākhyāt ātmā brahma abhidhīyate eka akṣarāt ukāra ākhyāt dvidhā viṣṇuḥ udīryate eka akṣarāt makāra ākhyāt śivaḥ rudraḥ udāhṛtaḥ dakṣiṇa aṃgāt maheśasya jātaḥ brahmā ātma saṃjñikaḥ vāma aṃgāt abhavat viṣṇuḥ tatas vidyā iti saṃjñitaḥ hṛdayāt nīlarudraḥ bhūt śivasya śiva saṃjñikaḥ sṛṣṭeḥ pravartakaḥ brahmā sthiteḥ viṣṇuḥ vimohakaḥ kāraṇa traya hetuḥ ca śivaḥ parama kāraṇam ṛcaḥ yajūṃṣi sāmāni śākhāḥ ca anyāḥ sahasraśas svapna anubhūtam iva tat tābhyām na adhyavasīyate liṃge api mudritam sarvam yathā vedaiḥ udāhṛtam tat dṛṣṭvā mudritam liṃge prasādāt liṃginaḥ tadā utpattim vilayam ca eva yathātmyam ca ṣaḍadhvanām tatas parataram dhāma dhāmavantam ca pūruṣam niruttarataram brahma niṣkalam śivam īśvaram paśu pāśa mayasya asya prapaṃcasya sadā patim prakāśa eka rasam śāntam prasannam satata uditam sarva kalyāṇa nilayam śaktyā tādṛśayā anvitam jñātvā devam virūpākṣam brahma nārāyaṇau tadā racayitvā aṃjalim mūrdhni bhītau tau vācam ūcatuḥ ajñaḥ vā aham abhijñaḥ vā tvayā ādau deva nirmitaḥ īdṛśīm bhrāntim āpannaḥ iti kaḥ atra aparādhyati āstām mama idam ajñānam tvayi sannihate prabho viṣṇuḥ uvāca stotum deva na vāc asti mahimnaḥ sadṛśī tava prabhoḥ agre vidheyānām tūṣṇīṃbhāvaḥ vyatikramaḥ kāraṇa tvam tvayā dattam vismṛtam tava māyayā mohitaḥ ahaṃkṛtaḥ ca api punar eva asmi śāsitaḥ vijñāpitaiḥ kim bahubhiḥ bhītaḥ asmi bhṛśam īśvara iti vijñāpitaḥ tābhyām īśvarābhyām maheśvaraḥ īśvaraḥ uvāca tatas chinnā prajā sṛṣṭiḥ jagat kāraṇa bhūtayoḥ evam nivārita avadya liṃga āvirbhāva līlayā tasmāt bhūyas vivādam ca vrīḍām ca utsṛjya kṛtsnaśas purā mama ājñayā sārddham samasta jñāna saṃhitāḥ yuvābhyām hi mayā dattā kāraṇa tva prasiddhaye mantra ratnam ca sūtra ākhyam paṃcākṣara mayam param dadāmi ca punar sarvam yathāpūrvam mama ājñayā yatas vinā yuvām tena na kṣamau sṛṣṭi rakṣaṇe evam uktvā mahādevaḥ nārāyaṇa pitāmahau mantrarājam dadau tābhyām jñānasaṃhitayā saha tau labdhvā mahatīm divyām ājñām māheśvarīm parām mahārtham mantra ratnam ca tathā eva sakalāḥ kalāḥ daṃḍa vat praṇatim kṛtvā devadevasya pādayoḥ atiṣṭhatām vīta bhayau ānanda astimitau tadā liṃgam kvāpi tirobhūtam na tābhyām upalabhyate tatas prabhṛti śakra ādyāḥ sarve eva sura asurāḥ ṛṣayaḥ ca narāḥ nāgāḥ nāryaḥ ca api vidhānataḥ liṃga pratiṣṭhā kurvanti liṃge tam pūjayanti ca iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe harividhimohanivāraṇam nāma paṃcatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī kṛṣṇaḥ uvāca śiva śāstra ukta mārgeṇa kuryāt liṃgam pramāṇa vat svīkṛtya atha śubha sthānam bhū parīkṣām vidhāya ca daśa upacārān kurvīta lakṣaṇa uddhāra pūrvakān teṣām daśa upacārāṇām pūrvam pūjya vināyakam sthāna śuddhi ādikam kṛtvā liṃgam snāna ālayam nayet śalākayā kāṃcanayā kuṃkuma ādi rasa aktayā lakṣitam lakṣaṇam śilpa śāstreṇa vilikhet tatas aṣṭa mṛd salilaiḥ vā atha paṃca mṛd salilaiḥ tathā liGgam piṃḍikayā sārdham paṃcagavyaiḥ ca śodhayet nītvā adhivāsayet tatra liṃgam piṃḍikayā saha diggaja aṣṭaka saṃpanne dikpāla aṣṭa ghaṭa anvite aṣṭa maṃgalakaiḥ yukte kṛta dikpālaka arcite tejasam dāravam vā api kṛtvā padmāsana aṃkitam dvārapālān samabhyarcya bhadra ādīn caturaḥ kramāt sa kūrcābhyām tu vastrābhyām samāveṣṭyam samantataḥ prāpayya śanakais toyam pīṭhikā upari śāyayet prāc śiraskam adhas sūtram piṃḍikām ca asya paścime sarva maṃgala saṃyuktam liṃgam tatra adhivāsayet visṛjya pūjitam tatra śodhayitvā ca pūrvavat saṃpūjya utsava mārgeṇa śayana ālayam ānayet tatra api śayana sthānam kuryāt maṃḍala madhyatas aiśānyām padmam ālikhya śuddha lipte mahī tale śiva kuṃbham śodhayitvā tatra āvāhya śivam yajet tasya paścimatas ca api caṃḍikā padmam ālikhet tatra liṃgam samānīya sarva maṃgala niḥsvanaiḥ saha piṃḍikayā āveṣṭya śāyayet ca yathā purā vidyeśa kalaśān nyasyet madhye śaivīm ca varddhanīm te ca aṣṭa mūrttayaḥ kalpyāḥ pūrva ādi paritas sthitāḥ catvāraḥ ca atha vā dikṣu svadhyetāraḥ sa jāpakāḥ juhuyuḥ te viraṃci ādyāḥ catasraḥ mūrtayaḥ smṛtāḥ daiśikaḥ prathamam teṣām aiśānyām paścime ācāryyāt pādam arddham vā juhuyuḥ ca apare dvijāḥ pūrvam pūrṇāhutim hutvā ghṛtena aṣṭa uttaram śatam hutvā a hutvā spṛśet liṃgam vedikām ca punar punar pūrṇāhutim tatas hutvā kramāt dadyāt ca dakṣiṇām ācāryāt pādam arddham vā hotR̥ṇām sthapateḥ api tatas śvabhre vṛṣam haimam kūrcam vā api niveśya ca mṛd aṃbhasā paṃcagavyaiḥ punar śuddha jalena ca śodhitām candana āliptām śvabhre brahmaśilām kṣipet kara nyāsam tatas kṛtvā navabhiḥ śakti nāmabhiḥ haritāla ādi dhātūn ca bīja gandha auṣadhaiḥ api śivaśāstra ukta vidhinā kṣipet brahmaśilā upari pratiliṃgam tu saṃsthāpya kṣīram vṛkṣa samudbhavam prāc udak pravarām kiṃcid sthāpayet mūlavidyayā piṃḍikām ca atha saṃyojya śāktam mūlam anusmaran dattvā ca arghyam ca puṣpāṇi kuryuḥ yavanikām punar yathāyogyam niṣeka ādi liṃgasya puratas tadā ānīya śayana sthānāt kalaśān vinyaset kramāt mahā pūjām atha ārabhya saṃpūjya kalaśān daśa aṃguṣṭha anāmikā yogāt ādāya tam udīrayet śaktim nyaset tathā vidyām vidyeśān ca yathākramam varddhanyām piṃḍikā liṃgam vidyeśa kalaśaiḥ punar abhiṣicya āsanam paścāt ādhāra ādyam prakalpayet kṛtvā paṃca kalā nyāsam dīptam liṃgam anusmaret āvāhayet śivau sākṣāt prāñjaliḥ prāc udak mukhaḥ vṛṣa adhirājam āruhya vimānam vā nabhastalāt alaṃkṛtam saha āyāntam devyā devam anusmaran tatas paṃca upacārān ca kṛtvā pūjām samāpayet na atas parataraḥ kaścid vidhiḥ pañca upacārakāt pratiṣṭhām liṃga vat kuryāt pratimāsu api sarvatas jala adhivāse śayane śāyayet tāntu adhomukhīm kumbha uda śāyitām mantraiḥ hṛdi tām san niyojayet kṛta ālayām parām āhuḥ pratiṣṭhām akṛta ālayāt śaktaḥ kṛta ālayaḥ paścāt pratiṣṭhā vidhim ācaret aśaktaḥ ced pratiṣṭhāpya liṃgam beram atha api vā śakteḥ anuguṇam paścāt prakurvīta śiva ālayam kṛtvā liṃgam vā lakṣaṇa anvitam devīm kṛtvā śubhe deśe tatra abjam pūrvavat likhet paṃca brahmāṇi tad bījaiḥ teṣu paṃcasu paṃcabhiḥ viśodhya liṃgam beram vā mṛd toya ādyaiḥ yathā purā nidhāya puṣpam śirasi prokṣayet prokṣaṇī jalaiḥ kumbhaiḥ īśāna vidyā antaiḥ snāpayet mūlavidyayā tatas paṃcakalā nyāsam kṛtvā pūjām ca pūrvavat liṃgāni bāṇa saṃjñāni sthāpanīyāni vā na vā tāni pūrvam śivena eva saṃskṛtāni yatas tatas śeṣāṇi sthāpanīyāni yāni dṛṣṭāni bāṇa vat a pīṭhe pīṭham āveśya kṛtvā saṃprokṣaṇam vidhim yajet tatra śivam teṣām pratiṣṭhā na vidhīyate dagdham ślatham kṣata aṃgam ca kṣipet liṃgam jalāśaye saṃdhāna yogyam saṃdhāya pratiṣṭhā vidhim ācaret berāt vā vikalāt liṃgāt deva pūjā purassaram udvāsya hṛdi saṃdhānam tyāgam vā yuktam ācaret dvi rātre ca mahā pūjām saṃprokṣaṇam atas param māsāt ūrdhvam aneka aham pūjā yadi vihanyate pratiṣṭhā procyate kaiścid kaiścid saṃprokṣaṇa kramaḥ saṃprokṣaṇe tu liṃga ādeḥ devam udvāsya pūrvavat aṣṭa pañca krameṇa eva snāpayitvā mṛd aṃbhasā prokṣayet prokṣaṇī toyaiḥ mūlena aṣṭa uttaram śatam sa puṣpam sa kuśam pāṇim nyasya liṃgasya mastake paṃca vāram japet mūlam aṣṭottaraśatam tatas tatas mūlena mūrddha ādi pīṭha antam saṃspṛśet api iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe ptatiṣṭhāvidhivarṇanam nāma ṣaṭtriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śīkṛṣṇaḥ uvāca jñāne kriyāyām caryāyām sāram uddhṛtya saṃgrahāt sa adhikāram ca sa aṃgam ca sa vidhim sa prayojanam yadi asti maraṇam pūrvam yoga ādi anupamardataḥ sadyas sādhayitum śakyam yena syāt na ātma hā naraḥ tat ca tat kāraṇam ca eva tad kāla karaṇāni ca sthāne pṛṣṭam tvayā kṛṣṇa sarva praśna artha vedinā tatas krameṇa tat sarvam vakṣye śṛṇu samāhitaḥ yā vṛttiḥ sa samāsena yogaḥ sa khalu paṃcadhā avyākṣepā manaḥ vṛttiḥ mantra yogaḥ udāhṛtaḥ prāṇāyāma mukhā sā eva sparśe yogaḥ abhidhīyate vilīna avayavam viśvam rūpam saṃbhāvyate yatas abhāva yogaḥ saṃproktaḥ anābhāsāt vastunaḥ sataḥ śiva svabhāvaḥ eva ekaḥ ciṃtyate nirupādhikaḥ dṛṣṭe tathā ānuśravike viraktam viṣaye manaḥ viṣaya dvaya doṣāṇām guṇānām īśvarasya ca darśanāt eva satatam viraktam jāyate manaḥ aṣṭa aṃgaḥ vā ṣaṣ aṃgaḥ vā sarva yogaḥ samāsatas samādhiḥ iti yogāṃgāni aṣṭāv uktāni sūribhiḥ dhyānam samādhiḥ yogasya ṣaḍ aṃgāni samāsatas pṛthak lakṣaṇam eteṣām śivaśāstre samīritam śiva āgameṣu ca anyeṣu viśeṣāt kāmika ādiṣu yoga śāstreṣu api tathā purāṇeṣu api keṣu ca śaucam tuṣṭiḥ tapaḥ ca eva japaḥ praṇidhiḥ eva ca iti paṃca prabhedaḥ syāt niyamaḥ sva aṃśa bhedataḥ paryaṃkam ca yatheṣṭam ca proktam āsanam aṣṭadhā nāsāpuṭena apareṇa pūraṇāt pūrakam matam recaka ādiṣu yaḥ bhyāsaḥ nāḍī śodhana pūrvakaḥ kanyakā ādi krama vaśāt prāṇāyāma nirodhanam tat caturddhā upadiṣṭam syāt mātrā guṇa vibhāgataḥ uttamaḥ tu tris uddhātaḥ ṣaḍviṃśat mātrakaḥ paraḥ sveda kaṃpa ādi janakaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ tad uttaraḥ ānanda udbhava romāṃca netra aśrūṇām vimocanam jānum pradakṣiṇīkṛtya na drutam na vilaṃbitam aṃgulī sphoṭanam kuryāt sā mātrā iti prakīrtitā mātrā krameṇa vijñeyāḥ ca udvāta krama yogataḥ nāḍī viśuddhi pūrvam tu prāṇāyāmam samācaret prāṇasya vijayāt eva jīyante deha vāyavaḥ ava apāna ākhyaḥ yat āhāra ādi bhujyate vyānaḥ vyānaśayati aṃgāni aśeṣāṇi vivardhayan samam nayati sarva aṃgam samānaḥ tena gīyate na jahāti mṛtam ca api sarvavyāpī dhanaṃjayaḥ krameṇa abhyasyamānā iyam prāṇāyāma pramāṇavān sarva roga kṣayaḥ ca eva balam tejaḥ surūpa tā tapāṃsi pāpa kṣaya tā yajña dāna vrata ādayaḥ namaḥ pūrvāṇi indriyāṇi svargam narakam eva ca tasmāt sukha arthī matimān jñāna vairāgyam āsthitaḥ indriya aśvān nigṛhya āśu sva ātmanā ātmānam uddharet dhāraṇā nāma cittasya sthāna bandhaḥ samāsatas sthānam ca śivaḥ eva ekaḥ na anyat doṣa trayam yatas kālam kaṃca avadhīkṛtya sthāne avasthāpitam manaḥ tasmāt dhīram manaḥ kuryāt dhāraṇa abhyāsa yogataḥ avyākṣiptena manasā dhyānam nāma tat ucyate paraḥ dhyeyaḥ samāpta ātharvaṇī śrutiḥ tathā śivā parā dhyeyā sarva bhūta gatau śivau samādhiḥ nāma yoga aṃgam antimam parikīrtitam svarūpa śūnya vat bhānam samādhiḥ abhidhīyate evam śive vilīna ātmā samādhi sthaḥ iha ucyate tathā samādhi niṣṭhaḥ api tasmāt na vicalet sudhīḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīya saṃhitāyām vāyu naimiṣeya ṛṣi saṃvāde uttara khaṇḍe yogagativarṇanam nāma saptatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ daśā ete yuñjatām puṃsām antarāyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ dhātu vaiṣamya jāḥ doṣāḥ vyādhayaḥ karma doṣa jāḥ duḥkham ajñāna jam puṃsām cittasya ādhyātmikam viduḥ ādhibhautikam aṃga uttham yat ca duḥkham purā kṛtaiḥ ādhidaivikam ākhyātam aśani astra viṣa ādikam icchā vighāta jam mokṣam daurmanasyam pracakṣate vārttāḥ vārttāsu vijñānam sarveṣām eva dehinām tathā āsvādaḥ ca divyeṣu raseṣu āsvādaḥ ucyate sparśana adhigamaḥ tadvat vedanā nāma viśrutā gandha ādīnām ca divyānām ābrahma bhuvana adhipāḥ kṛśa tā sthūla tā bālyam vārddhakyam ca eva yauvanam evam aṣṭaguṇam prāhuḥ paiśācam pārthivam padam vinā kumbha ādikam pāṇau jala sañcaya dhāraṇam tat idam ṣoḍaśaguṇam āpyam aiśvaryam adbhutam śarīrāt agni nirmāṇam tad tāpa bhaya varjjanam sthāpanam vā analasya ā apsu pāṇau pāvaka dhāraṇam dvābhyām deha vinirmāṇam āpya aiśvarya samanvitam manojava tvam bhūtānām kṣaṇāt antar praveśanam parvvata ādi mahā bhāra dhāraṇam ca aprayatnataḥ guru tvañca laghu tvañca pāṇau anila dhāraṇam dvātriṃśat guṇam aiśvaryyam mārutam kavayaḥ viduḥ ākāśa laṃghanam ca eva sva dehe tad niveśanam ākāśa piṇḍīkaraṇam aśarīra tvam eva ca ābhimānikam aiśvaryam prājāpatyam pracakṣate etat cāndramasaiḥ bhogaiḥ ṣaṭpañcāśadguṇam mahat sargaḥ saṃkalpa mātreṇa trāṇam saṃharaṇam tathā sva adhikāraḥ ca sarveṣām bhūta citta pravartanam śubha aśubhasya karaṇam prājāpatyaiḥ ca saṃyutam bauddhāt asmāt param gauṇam aiśvaryam prākṛtam viduḥ vaiṣṇavam tat samākhyātam tasya eva bhuvana sthitiḥ tat pauruṣam ca gauṇam ca gaṇeśam padam aiśvaram viṣṇunā tat padam kiṃcid jñātum anyaiḥ na śakyate vijñāna siddhayaḥ ca eva sarvāḥ eva aupasargikāḥ yathākāmam guṇān bhogān bhuktvā muktim prayāsyati atha prayogam yogasya vakṣye śṛṇu samāhitaḥ śubhe kāle śubhe deśe śiva kṣetra ādike punar na daṃśa maśaka ākīrṇe sarpa śvāpada saṃkule na ca duṣṭa mṛga ākīrṇe na bhaye durjana āvṛte śmaśāne caitya valmīke jīrṇāgāre catuṣpathe nadīnada samudrāṇām tīre rathyā antare na jīrṇa udyāna goṣṭha ādau na aniṣṭe na ca nindite na ca ati ciṃtā ākulitaḥ na ca ati kṣudh pipāsitaḥ āsanam mṛdulam ramyam vipulam su samam śuci danta agra saṃsthitāḥ jihvām acalām sanniveśya ca sva deha āyatanasya antar viciṃtya śivam aṃbayā mūle nāsā agratas nābhau kaṃṭhe vā tālu randhrayoḥ bhrū madhye dvāra deśe vā lalāṭe mūrdhni vā smaret tatra sa āvaraṇam vā api nirāvaraṇam eva vā vidyut samāna varṇe ca parṇe varṇa avasānake ṣoḍaśa arasya patrāṇi svarāḥ ṣoḍaśa tāni vai pūrva ādīni kramāt etat padmam kandasya mūlataḥ kakāra ādi ṭakāra antāḥ varṇāḥ parṇāni anukramāt go kṣīra dhavalasya uktāḥ ḍa ādi pha antāḥ yathākramam vidhūma aṃgāra varṇasya varṇāḥ vādyāḥ ca mūlādhāra aravindasya hema ābhasya yathākramam vakāra ādi sakāra antāḥ varṇāḥ parṇa mayāḥ sthitāḥ eteṣu atha aravindeṣu yatra eva abhiratam manaḥ tatra eva devam devīm ca ciṃtayet dhīrayā dhiyā aṃguṣṭha mātram amalam dīpyamānam samantataḥ śuddha dīpa śikhā ākāram sva śaktyā pūrṇa maṇḍitam indu rekhā sama ākāram tārā rūpam atha api vā nīvāra śūkaḥ sadṛśam bisa sutrābham eva vā kṣiti ādi tattva vijayam dhyātā yadi api vāñchati tad tad tattva adhipām eva mūrtim sthūlām viciṃtayet sadāśiva antāḥ brahma ādya bhava ādyāḥ ca aṣṭa mūrtayaḥ śivasya mūrtayaḥ sthūlāḥ śivaśāstre viniścitāḥ ghorā miśrā praśāntāḥ ca mūrtayaḥ tāḥ muni īśvaraiḥ phala abhilāṣa rahitaiḥ cintyāḥ cintā viśāradaiḥ ghorāḥ ced ciṃtitāḥ kuryuḥ pāpa roga parikṣayam saumye muktiḥ viśeṣeṇa śāntiḥ prajñā prasidhyati sidhyanti siddhayaḥ ca atra kramaśas na atra saṃśayaḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe yogagatau vighnotpattivarṇanam nāma aṣṭatriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrīkaṃṭhanātham smaratām sadyas sarva artha siddhayaḥ śive tu ciṃtite sākṣāt sarvāḥ sidhyanti siddhayaḥ mūrti antareṣu dhyāteṣu śiva rūpam viciṃtayet dhyānam ādau sa viṣayam tatas nirviṣayam jaguḥ tatra nirviṣayam dhyānam na asti iti eva satām matam tasmāt sa viṣayam dhyānam bāla arka kiraṇa āśrayam sūkṣma āśrayam nirviṣayam na aparam paramārthataḥ yat vā sa viṣayam dhyānam tat sa ākāra samāśrayam nirākāra ātma saṃvittiḥ dhyānam nirviṣayam matam nirākāra śraya tvena sa ākāra āśrayataḥ tathā tasmāt sa viṣayam dhyānam ādau kṛtvā sa bījakam śamaḥ sarva āpadām ca eva śāntiḥ iti abhidhīyate tamasaḥ anta bahis nāśaḥ praśāntiḥ parigīyate svastha tā yā tu sā buddhaḥ prasādaḥ parikīrtitaḥ kāraṇāni ca sarvāṇi sa bāhya abhyantarāṇi ca śraddadhānaḥ prasanna ātmā dhyātā sadbhiḥ udāhṛtaḥ dhyāyataḥ kṣaṇa mātram vā śraddhayā parameśvaram śiva dhyānasya pūrṇasya sākṣāt uktam prayojanam dhyāyataḥ kṣaṇa mātram vā śraddhayā parameśvaram siddha yoga phalaḥ yogī lokānām hita kāmyayā bhogān bhuktvā yathākāmam viharet vā atra vartatām yaḥ tu āsannām mṛtim martyaḥ dṛṣṭa ariṣṭam ca bhūyasā sa yoga ārambha nirataḥ śiva kṣetram samāśrayet prāṇān vinā api roga ādyaiḥ svayam eva parityajet kṛtvā api anaśanam ca eva hutvā ca aṃgam śiva anale kṣiptvā vā śiva tīrtheṣu sva deham avagāhanāt roga ādyaiḥ vā atha vivaśaḥ śiva kṣetram samāśritaḥ yathā hi maraṇam śreṣṭham uśanti anaśana ādibhiḥ śāstra viśraṃbha dhīreṇa manasā kriyate yatas śiva nindā ratam hatvā pīḍitaḥ svayam eva vā yaḥ tyajet dustyajān prāṇān na sa bhūyas prajāyate śiva nindā ratam hantum aśaktaḥ yaḥ svayam mṛtaḥ śiva arthe yaḥ tyajet prāṇān śiva bhakta artham eva vā na tena sadṛśaḥ kaścid mukti mārga sthitaḥ naraḥ tasmāt śīghratarā muktiḥ tasya saṃsāra maṃḍalāt na eva āśaucam prapadyeta tad putra ādi viśeṣataḥ śiva cāra artham athavā śiva vidyā artham eva vā khanet vā bhuvi tad deham dahet vā śucinā agninā kṣipet vā apsu śivāsu eva tyajet vā kāṣṭha loṣṭa vat kalyāṇam eva kurvīta śaktyā bhaktān ca tarpayet śrī sūtaḥ uvāca tadā brahma samādeśāt devī sākṣāt sarasvatī prasannā svādu salilā prāvartata nadī śubhā sarasvatīm nadīm dṛṣṭvā munayaḥ hṛṣṭa mānasāḥ atha santarpya deva ādīn tadīyaiḥ salilaiḥ śivaiḥ smarantaḥ pūrva vṛttāntam yayuḥ vārāṇasīm prati tadā te himavat pādāt pantatīm dakṣiṇāmukhīm dṛṣṭvā bhāgīrathī tatra snātvā tad tīrataḥ yayuḥ tatas vārāṇasīm prāpya muditāḥ sarve eva te tadā uttara pravāhāyām gaṃgāyām avagāhya ca tathā vilīyamāneṣu tapasviṣu mahātmasu prāk eva eṣām tu gamanāt pavanaḥ loka pāvanaḥ darśanam naimiṣīyāṇām saṃvādaḥ taiḥ mahātmanaḥ śuddhām buddhim tatas teṣām sa ambe sa anucare śive samāptim ca api satrasya dīrghapūrvasya satriṇām tadā anavasarāt eva dvāḥsthaiḥ dvāri nivāritāḥ munayaḥ brahma bhavanāt bahis pārśvam upāviśan atha tumburuṇā gāne samatām prāpya nāradaḥ tyaktvā paraspara sparddhām maitrīm ca paramām gataḥ upavīṇayitum devam nakuli īśvaram īśvaram bhavanāt niryayau dhātuḥ jaladāt aṃśumān iva tam dṛṣṭvā ṣaṭkulīyāḥ te nāradam muni go vṛṣam praṇamya avasaram śaṃbhoḥ papracchuḥ parama ādarāt tena te viviśuḥ veśma piṃḍībhūyāṃḍa janmanaḥ praviśya dūratas devam praṇamya bhuvi daṃḍa vat samīpe tad anujñātāḥ parivṛtya upatasthire tān tatra avasthitān pṛṣṭvā kuśalam kamalāsanaḥ vṛttāntam vaḥ mayā jñātam vāyuḥ eva āha naḥ yatas bhavadbhiḥ kim kṛtam paścāt māruta iṃtarhite sati gaṃgātīrtha isya gamanam yātrām vārāṇasīm prati darśanam tatra liṃgānām sthāpitānām sura īśvaraiḥ ākāśe mahataḥ tasya tejaḥ rāśeḥ ca darśanam munīnām vilayam tatra nirodham tejasaḥ tatas yāthātmya vedanam tasya ciṃtitasya api ca ātmabhiḥ munibhiḥ kathitam śrutvā viśvakarmā caturmukhaḥ kaṃpayitvā śiraḥ kiṃcid prāha gaṃbhīrayā girā pratyāsīdati yuṣmākam siddhiḥ āmuṣmikī parā prasāda abhimukhaḥ bhūtaḥ iti bhūtārtha sūcitam vārāṇasyām tu yuṣmābhiḥ yat dṛṣṭam divi dīptimat tat liṃga saṃjñitam sākṣāt tejaḥ māheśvaram param tatra līnāḥ ca munayaḥ śrauta pāśupata vratāḥ muktāḥ babhūvuḥ svasthāḥ ca naiṣṭhikāḥ dagdha kilbiṣāḥ sa ca ayam arthaḥ sūcyeta yuṣmad dṛṣṭena tejasā prayāta dakṣiṇam meroḥ śikharam deva sevitam pratīkṣya āgamanam sākṣāt bhūtanāthasya nandinaḥ purā sanatkumāraḥ api dṛṣṭvā api parameśvaram ajñānāt sarva yogi indra mānī vinaya dūṣitaḥ tatas aparādhāt kruddhena mahā uṣṭraḥ nandinā kṛtaḥ upāsya devam devīm ca nandinam ca anunīya vai kathaṃcid uṣṭra tā tasya prayatnena nivāritā prāpitaḥ hi yathāpūrvam sanat pūrvām kumāra tām avajñāya hi mām eva tathā aham kṛtavān muniḥ brahmaṇaḥ pūrvajaḥ putraḥ mām mūḍhaḥ iva saṃsmaran saḥ evam vyāhṛtaḥ bhūyas sarva bhūta gaṇāgraṇīḥ yat parājñāpanam mūrdhnā prātar pratigṛhītavān prasāda artham gaṇasya asya tapaḥ carati duścaram draṣṭavyaḥ ca iti yuṣmābhiḥ prāk gaṇeśa samāgamāt tad prasāda artham acirāt nandī tatra āgamiṣyati iti satvaram ādiśya preṣitā viśvayoginā kumāraśikharam meroḥ dakṣiṇam munayaḥ yayuḥ iti śrī śiva mahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe naimiṣarṣiyātrāvarṇanam nāma catvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca amṛta svādu śiśira svacchāḥ gādha laghu udakam samantataḥ saṃghaṭitam pala saṃcayaiḥ taraṃgaiḥ abhra saṃkāśaiḥ ākāśam iva bhūmi gam sukha avataraṇa ārohaiḥ sthalaiḥ nīla śilā mayaiḥ sopāna mārgau ruciraiḥ śobhamāna aṣṭa diś mukham tatra avatīrṇaiḥ ca yathā tatra uttīrṇaḥ ca bhūyasā snātaiḥ sita upavītaiḥ ca śukla a kaupīna valkalaiḥ jaṭā śikhāyanaiḥ muṃḍaiḥ tripuṃḍra kṛta maṃḍanaiḥ ghaṭaiḥ kamalinī patra puṭaiḥ ca kalaśaiḥ śivaiḥ kamaṇḍalubhiḥ anyaiḥ ca tādṛśaiḥ karaka ādibhiḥ ātma artham ca para artham ca devatā artham viśeṣataḥ ānīyamāna salilam ātta puṣpam ca nityaśas antar jala śilā ārūḍhaiḥ nīcānām sparśa śaṃkayā ācāravadbhiḥ munibhiḥ kṛta bhasma aṃga dhūsaraiḥ itas tatas majjadbhiḥ iṣṭa śiṣṭaiḥ śilā gataiḥ sthāne sthāne kṛta aneka bali puṣpa samīraṇaiḥ kvacid ca tṛṣayā āyāta mṛgī mṛga turaṃgamam kvacid pīta jana uttīrṇa mayūra vara vāraṇam kvacid kṛta taṭa āghāta vṛṣa prativṛṣa ujjvalam kvacid kāraṃḍava ravaiḥ kvacid sārasa kūjitaiḥ snāna pāna ādi karaṇaiḥ sva saṃpad druma jīvibhiḥ praṇayāt prāṇibhiḥ taiḥ taiḥ bhāṣamāṇam iva asakṛt kūla śākhi śikhā ālīna kokila ākula kūjitaiḥ ātapa upahatān sarvān na amantrayat ivāniśam vedyām vajra śilā mayyām mṛdule mṛga carmaṇi sanatkumāram āsīnam śaśvat bāla vapurddharam tad kāla mātra uparatam samādheḥ acala ātmanaḥ dadṛśuḥ naimiṣeyāḥ te praṇatāḥ ca upatasthire yāvat pṛṣṭavate tasmai procuḥ svāgata kāraṇam dadṛśe tad kṣaṇe tasmin vimānam bhānu sannibham gaṇeśvaraiḥ asaṃkhyeyaiḥ saṃvṛtam ca samantataḥ citra ratna vitāna āḍhyam muktā dāma virājitam nṛtyadbhiḥ ca eva gāyadbhiḥ vādayadbhiḥ ca saṃvṛtam vīra go vṛṣa cihnena vidrama druma yaṣṭinā kṛta gopura satkāram ketunā mānya hetunā divya siṃhāsana ārūḍham devyā suyaśayā saha śriyā ca vapuṣā ca eva tribhiḥ ca api vilocanaiḥ prākāraiḥ abhikṛtyānām pratyabhijñāpakam prabhoḥ sarva anugrahaṇam śaṃbhoḥ sākṣāt iva puras sthitam śilāda tanayam sākṣāt śrīmat śūla vara āyudham viśva īśvara gaṇa adhyakṣam viśveśvaram iva aparam viśvasya api vidhātR̥ṇām nigraha anugraha kṣamam kaṃṭhe nāgena maulau ca śaśāṃkena api alaṃkṛtam samāptam iva nirvāṇam sarvajñam iva saṃgatam dṛṣṭvā prahṛṣṭa vadanaḥ brahma putraḥ saha ṛṣibhiḥ praṇamya daṇḍa vat devam stutvā vyajñāpayat munīm ṣaṭkulīyāḥ ime dīrgham naimiṣe satram āsthitāḥ āgatāḥ brahmaṇā ādiṣṭāḥ pūrvam eva abhikāṃkṣayā śrutvā vākyam brahma putrasya nandī chittvā pāśān dṛṣṭipātena sadyas śaivam dharmam ca aiśvaram jñāna yogam dattvā bhūyas deva pārśvam jagāma sanatkumāreṇa ca tat samastam vyāsāya sākṣāt gurave mama uktam na a veda vidbhyaḥ kathanīyam etat purāṇa ratnam puraśāsanasya na abhakta śiṣyāya ca nāstikebhyaḥ dattam hi mohāt nirayam dadāti mārgeṇa sevā anugatena yaiḥ tat dattam gṛhītam paṭhitam śrutam vā atas gamiṣye samastam eva astu śivam sadā naḥ sūte kṛta āśiṣi gate munayaḥ su vṛttāḥ yāge ca paryavasite mahati prayoge kāle kalau ca viṣayaiḥ kaluṣāyamāṇe vārāṇasī parisare vasatim vinetuḥ atha ca te paśu pāśa mumukṣayā akhila tayā kṛta pāśupata vratāḥ vyāsaḥ uvāca etat śiva purāṇam hi samāptam hitam ādarāt tasmāt punar punar ca eva śrotavyam hi mumukṣubhiḥ pañcāvṛttiḥ prakartavyā purāṇasya asya sat dhiyā purātanāḥ ca rājānaḥ viprāḥ vaiśyāḥ ca sattamāḥ śroṣyati atha api yaḥ ca idam mānavaḥ bhakti tatparaḥ etat śiva purāṇam hi śivasya ati priyam param etat śiva purāṇasya vaktuḥ śrotuḥ ca sarvadā sa gaṇaḥ sa sutaḥ sa ambaḥ śam karotu sa śaṃkaraḥ iti śrī śivamahāpurāṇe saptamyām vāyavīyasaṃhitāyām uttara khaṇḍe vyāsopadeśaśrīśivamahāpurāṇamāhātmyavarṇanam nāma ekacatvāriṃśaḥ adhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ ayam granthaḥ
